# 🛡️ 去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构 (Debiasing, Fairness, Privacy & Industrial Systems)

> **研究范畴**：曝光/流行度/位置因果去偏 (Causal Rec)、双边公平性、联邦防投毒推荐、工业级全链路架构与评测基准

当前方向共收录 **687** 篇顶会/顶刊论文解读：

🔥 **高频交叉主题：** 生成式推荐 ×38 · 联邦推荐 ×23 · 大语言模型推荐 ×17 · 评测基准 ×15 · CTR预估与特征交叉 ×14 · 因果推断 ×13 · 多模态推荐 ×13 · 因果去偏 ×12

<dl data-paper-list>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2022&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排后处理&quot;,&quot;概率校准&quot;,&quot;IPS 去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2022/ctr_feature_interaction/obtaining_calibrated_probabilities_with_personalized_ranking_models/">Obtaining Calibrated Probabilities with Personalized Ranking Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 Gaussian/Gamma 参数化校准与 IPS 无偏风险最小化，校准个性化排序分数，ECE 最高降 25.85%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;在线审计/策略评估&quot;,&quot;Envy-freeness&quot;,&quot;Pure Exploration Bandits&quot;,&quot;Conservative Exploration&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/online_certification_of_preference_based_fairness_for_personalized_recommen/">Online certification of preference-based fairness for personalized recommender systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将推荐公平审计转化为带保守探索的纯探索老虎机，在线认证用户级无嫉妒性，并以41用户/75臂实现与总用户数无关的概率审计。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;大 batch 训练&quot;,&quot;自适应梯度裁剪&quot;,&quot;Embedding 频次不均衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2023/ctr_feature_interaction/cowclip_reducing_ctr_prediction_model_training_time_from_12_hours_to_10_min/">CowClip: Reducing CTR Prediction Model Training Time from 12 hours to 10 minutes on 1 GPU</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 ID 频次差异使大 batch 下 LR/正则缩放规则失效，提出按列自适应裁剪 CowClip，单卡 128× batch 无精度损失、76.8× 加速。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2023&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;应用层端到端&quot;,&quot;情感上下文&quot;,&quot;区块链存证&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2023/multimodal_rec/emotion_aware_music_recommendation_system_enhancing_user_experience_through/">Emotion-Aware Music Recommendation System: Enhancing User Experience Through Real-Time Emotional Context</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 CNN 歌词情感分类配合实时情绪输入驱动歌曲推荐，并用 Alchemy 区块链存证偏好与版权；仅有界面截图级定性验证，无任何量化评测与消融实验。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;端到端决策支持&quot;,&quot;因果推断&quot;,&quot;逆倾向加权&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/evaluating_digital_agriculture_recommendations_with_causal_inference/">Evaluating Digital Agriculture Recommendations with Causal Inference</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用因果图与后门准则评估棉花播种推荐对产量的影响，在171块田实测 ATE 提升 12%–17%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多重稳健估计 (MR)&quot;,&quot;逆倾向加权 (IPS)&quot;,&quot;误差插补&quot;,&quot;MNAR 去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/multiple_robust_learning_for_recommendation/">Multiple Robust Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用多个倾向/误差插补模型构成基向量并岭回归求解组合系数，把 DR 升级为多重稳健估计器，Coat/Yahoo 全库评测 MSE 与 nDCG@10 显著优于 SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2023&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端打分（跨域知识迁移）&quot;,&quot;差分隐私数据发布&quot;,&quot;生成式对抗迁移&quot;,&quot;表征解耦&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2023/cross_domain_multi_scenario/ppgencdr_a_stable_and_robust_framework_for_privacy_preserving_cross_domain_/">PPGenCDR: A Stable and Robust Framework for Privacy-Preserving Cross-Domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用条件 GAN 在源域做选择性 RDP 扰动并发布生成器，配合控制论稳定器与批内特征解耦，在 Leave-One-Out + 99 负采样协议下较 PriCDR 提升 6–32%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/排序&quot;,&quot;定向拓扑攻击&quot;,&quot;节点掩码&quot;,&quot;LightGCN&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/revisiting_item_promotion_in_gnn_based_collaborative_filtering_a_masked_tar/">Revisiting Item Promotion in GNN-based Collaborative Filtering: A Masked Targeted Topological Attack Perspective</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次将GNN协同过滤物品推广重述为定向拓扑攻击，以节点掩码降噪和目标列梯度实现小预算高效攻击；Gowalla/Yelp2018全库top-50命中显著超越启发式基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;离线评估&quot;,&quot;Item Sampling&quot;,&quot;Top-K Metric Estimation&quot;,&quot;EM&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/towards_reliable_item_sampling_for_recommendation_evaluation/">Towards Reliable Item Sampling for Recommendation Evaluation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出MSE最优item-sampling估计器与自适应采样EM，低样本下准确恢复Top-K指标。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;联邦训练（服务器聚合）&quot;,&quot;无目标投毒攻击&quot;,&quot;对比学习均匀性正则&quot;,&quot;拜占庭鲁棒聚合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/untargeted_attack_against_federated_recommendation_systems_via_poisonous_it/">Untargeted Attack against Federated Recommendation Systems via Poisonous Item Embeddings and the Defense</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 ClusterAttack 用簇内方差最小化的恶意 item 梯度塌缩嵌入空间打乱排序，并给出基于对比学习均匀性正则的 UNION 防御与服务器端梯度过滤。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦图神经网络&quot;,&quot;群体公平&quot;,&quot;本地差分隐私&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/no_prejudice_fair_federated_graph_neural_networks_for_personalized_recommen/">No Prejudice! Fair Federated Graph Neural Networks for Personalized Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在联邦图推荐中以隐私保护的归纳式图扩展+LDP，把群体公平正则转化为梯度缩放因子，RMSE 差异降 47%–99%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2024&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;广告创意排序&quot;,&quot;并行级联架构&quot;,&quot;联合训练与知识蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2024/ctr_feature_interaction/parallel_ranking_of_ads_and_creatives_in_real_time_advertising_systems/">Parallel Ranking of Ads and Creatives in Real-Time Advertising Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 Peri-CR 广告-创意并行排序架构与 JAC 离线联合蒸馏模型，配 NSCTR 指标；线上 CTR +10.12%、RPM +7.67% 且零延迟增长。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2024&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;多阶段排序（召回/粗排/精排）&quot;,&quot;Multi-Agent RL&quot;,&quot;Cascading Information Chain&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2024/rl_bandit_rec/unex_rl_reinforcing_long_term_rewards_in_multi_stage_recommender_systems_wi/">UNEX-RL: Reinforcing Long-Term Rewards in Multi-Stage Recommender Systems with UNidirectional EXecution</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多阶段推荐建模为单向执行 MARL，用 CIC 重放全链观测并配合 SG/CQR 降方差；离线模拟器与 Kwai 在线 A/B 显著提升长期观看时长。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;推荐透明度与数据能动性&quot;,&quot;K-12 AI 素养教育&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/an_xai_social_media_platform_for_teaching_k_12_students_ai_driven_profiling/">An XAI Social Media Platform for Teaching K-12 Students AI-Driven Profiling, Clustering, and Engagement-Based Recommending</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向 K-12 的 XAI 社交媒体教学平台，实时可视化数据采集、画像聚类与推荐机制，209 名学生实测揭示三类使用画像。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;可持续推荐&quot;,&quot;多目标损失&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/bites_of_tomorrow_personalized_recommendations_for_a_healthier_and_greener_/">Bites of Tomorrow: Personalized Recommendations for a Healthier and Greener Plate</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出绿色食品推荐任务 GRAPE，用双注意力建模用户对可持续指标的动态偏好，并以两种 Green Loss 在保精度前提下提升推荐列表绿色度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;模型遗忘/隐私合规&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;反向 BPR&quot;,&quot;图卷积推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/multi_modal_recommendation_unlearning_for_legal_licensing_and_modality_cons/">Multi-Modal Recommendation Unlearning for Legal, Licensing, and Modality Constraints</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个多模态推荐遗忘框架 MMRecUn，用反向 BPR 衰减遗忘集、正向 BPR 保真保留集，Recall 最高提升 49.85% 且比从头重训快 1.3 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;联邦基础模型&quot;,&quot;组门控路由&quot;,&quot;多面向用户建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2025/sequential_rec/multifaceted_user_modeling_in_recommendation_a_federated_foundation_models_/">Multifaceted User Modeling in Recommendation: A Federated Foundation Models Approach</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>联邦场景下用组门控网络将用户分层路由到组级 FFN，配合用户私有 FFN 实现多面向个性化，CTR 任务 AUC 最高提升 1.16%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦协同过滤&quot;,&quot;变分自编码器&quot;,&quot;门控双编码器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/personalized_federated_collaborative_filtering_a_variational_autoencoder_ap/">Personalized Federated Collaborative Filtering: A Variational AutoEncoder Approach</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 FedCF 重构为 VAE 任务，用门控双编码器分离共享/个性化知识，HR@20 较 FedRAP 提升约 97%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排/事后干预&quot;,&quot;稀疏自编码器&quot;,&quot;单义神经元&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/extracting_interaction_aware_monosemantic_concepts_in_recommender_systems/">Extracting Interaction-Aware Monosemantic Concepts in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用预测感知 SAE 从双塔嵌入中提取单义神经元，在 ML1M/Last.FM 上实现高纯度概念发现与事后可控干预。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;路径积分归因&quot;,&quot;反事实保真度&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/fidelity_aware_recommendation_explanations_via_stochastic_path_integration/">Fidelity-Aware Recommendation Explanations via Stochastic Path Integration</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将路径积分归因引入推荐解释，用随机基线采样适配稀疏二值交互，在三种模型三数据集上刷新反事实保真度基准。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;参数高效微调&quot;,&quot;向量量化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/plug_and_play_parameter_efficient_tuning_of_embeddings_for_federated_recomm/">Plug-and-Play Parameter-Efficient Tuning of Embeddings for Federated Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>冻结预训练全量物品嵌入、仅微调并上传压缩嵌入（LoRA/Hash/RQ-VAE），在联邦推荐中同时降通信与提精度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/服务端黑盒查询&quot;,&quot;成员推理攻击&quot;,&quot;混合推荐系统隐私&quot;,&quot;相对成员度量&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/reference_recommendation_based_membership_inference_attack_against_hybrid_b/">Reference Recommendation based Membership Inference Attack against Hybrid-based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>针对混合推荐系统提出参考推荐相对成员度量 MIA，仅需两次黑盒查询，ASR 达 0.93 且比影子训练快 93 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICWSM&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;算法审计&quot;,&quot;心理健康推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM智能体仿真&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/seeking_help_facing_harm_auditing_tiktok_s_mental_health_recommendations/">Seeking Help, Facing Harm: Auditing TikTok&#x27;s Mental Health Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>30个LLM智能体7天控制审计TikTok推荐流，揭示交互行为主导心理健康内容曝光、求助意图无法形成安全边界。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI/ACM AIES&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;广告投放审计&quot;,&quot;马甲账号审计&quot;,&quot;人口统计公平性&quot;,&quot;LLM 广告&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/the_beginning_of_chatgpt_ads/">The Beginning of ChatGPT Ads</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个 ChatGPT 广告投放审计：91 个马甲账号 3×3 种族×收入设计，发现低收入账号更易被投放广告、种族无显著差异。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICWSM 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业审计&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;算法审计&quot;,&quot;党派倾斜与谣言曝光&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/AAAI/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/the_role_of_follow_networks_and_twitter_s_content_recommender_on_partisan_s/">The Role of Follow Networks and Twitter&#x27;s Content Recommender on Partisan Skew and Rumor Exposure during the 2022 U.S. Midterm Election</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：AAAI Conference on Artificial Intelligence">AAAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 18 个自动化审计账号在 2022 美国中期选举期间对比算法流与时间流，量化推荐算法与关注网络对党派倾斜、谣言与低质信息曝光的交互效应。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL/EMNLP 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;位置偏差&quot;,&quot;对抗学习&quot;,&quot;新闻推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/debiasgan_eliminating_position_bias_in_news_recommendation_with_adversarial/">DebiasGAN: Eliminating Position Bias in News Recommendation with Adversarial Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>通过位置感知/位置随机双分支与对抗学习消除新闻推荐位置偏差，在 News/Uniform 上 AUC 相对提升 1.1–1.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;基础设施与评测&quot;,&quot;模块化设计&quot;,&quot;可复现研究&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2023/sequential_rec/newsreclib_a_pytorch_lightning_library_for_neural_news_recommendation/">NewsRecLib: A PyTorch-Lightning Library for Neural News Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>EMNLP 2023 序列与会话推荐，提出高度模块化的神经新闻推荐开源库，统一评估协议与组件消融，解决研究不可复现与不公平比较痛点。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL&quot;,&quot;可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;排序/重排&quot;,&quot;文本攻击&quot;,&quot;隐蔽性评估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/stealthy_attack_on_large_language_model_based_recommendation/">Stealthy Attack on Large Language Model based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示LLM推荐对测试期标题文本攻击的脆弱性，黑盒改写可使目标曝光最高提升520%，且整体NDCG与语义几乎不变。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NAACL 2025 Findings&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM 分层卸载&quot;,&quot;客户端性能均衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/a_federated_framework_for_llm_based_recommendation/">A Federated Framework for LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>FELLRec 用动态聚合+课程升温平衡客户端性能，并以分层卸载降低 LLM 联邦推荐端侧开销。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NAACL 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;广告文案质量评估&quot;,&quot;基准数据集&quot;,&quot;广告文案生成&quot;,&quot;人类对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/adtec_a_unified_benchmark_for_evaluating_text_quality_in_search_engine_adve/">AdTEC: A Unified Benchmark for Evaluating Text Quality in Search Engine Advertising</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个公开的搜索广告文案质量多维评测基准，定义五任务并对比 PLM/LLM/人类评估者表现。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;认知偏差攻击&quot;,&quot;LLM 商品推荐鲁棒性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/bias_beware_the_impact_of_cognitive_biases_on_llm_driven_product_recommenda/">Bias Beware: The Impact of Cognitive Biases on LLM-Driven Product Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次将人类认知偏差作为黑盒重排攻击注入商品描述，揭示 LLM 推荐器对 social proof 等偏差高度敏感且难以防御。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NAACL 2025 Findings&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;地理偏见&quot;,&quot;公平性审计&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/richer_output_for_richer_countries_uncovering_geographical_disparities_in_g/">Richer Output for Richer Countries: Uncovering Geographical Disparities in Generated Stories and Travel Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在10万旅行请求与20万故事生成上量化5个LLM的地理不平等：穷国推荐独特性低、地理实体少、故事更多苦难与悲伤，且与人均GDP显著相关。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;对话推荐&quot;,&quot;用户身份偏差&quot;,&quot;推荐透明度&quot;,&quot;人口统计对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/stereotype_or_personalization_user_identity_biases_chatbot_recommendations/">Stereotype or Personalization? User Identity Biases Chatbot Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 LLM 聊天机器人无论用户显式还是隐式透露种族身份都会产生刻板推荐，且在被追问时系统性掩盖种族影响，跨 7 个主流模型、4 个种族、12 万条合成请求验证。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;权威偏见审计&quot;,&quot;反事实扰动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/authority_bias_in_conversational_search_engines_for_academic_paper_recommen/">Authority Bias in Conversational Search Engines for Academic Paper Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>内容受控反事实审计揭示 LLM 学术推荐存在显著权威偏见，并提出 say-do gap 与前沿模型反向失效两大诊断现象。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACL 2026 Findings&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;信息茧房&quot;,&quot;模糊测试&quot;,&quot;立场检测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/content_fuzzing_for_escaping_information_cocoons_on_digital_social_media/">Content Fuzzing for Escaping Information Cocoons on Digital Social Media</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以立场检测置信度为灰盒反馈，用 LLM 迭代改写帖子翻转机器立场标签，助创作者突破信息茧房，跨 3 数据集 4 模型平均逃逸率 40%–91%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2026 Findings&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;GEO 网页污染&quot;,&quot;检索增强鲁棒性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/llm_rec/one_polluted_page_is_enough_evaluating_web_content_pollution_in_llm_recomme/">One Polluted Page Is Enough: Evaluating Web Content Pollution in LLM Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 FORGE 基准，在冻结检索包上本地改写真实品牌为虚假品牌，量化 12 个 LLM 推荐器被 GEO 网页污染欺骗的比率，并揭示推理与防御的反效果。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;EMNLP 2026 Findings&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;全链路（输入/数据/模型/输出）&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec 公平性&quot;,&quot;二维分类法&quot;,&quot;评估协议&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACL_EMNLP/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/rethinking_fairness_in_llm_based_recommender_systems_a_survey/">Rethinking Fairness in LLM-Based Recommender Systems: A Survey</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACL &amp; EMNLP Conferences">ACL / EMNLP (RecSys &amp; CRS)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个 LLM4Rec 公平性综述，提出「偏置机制×公平目标」二维分类法，系统梳理数据集、评估协议、缓解策略与可信交叉议题。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACM MM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;时长偏差&quot;,&quot;对抗学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACM_MM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/dvr_micro_video_recommendation_optimizing_watch_time_gain_under_duration_bi/">DVR: Micro-Video Recommendation Optimizing Watch-Time-Gain under Duration Bias</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Multimedia">ACM MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出去时长偏差的无偏指标 WTG/DCWTG 与模型无关的对抗去偏框架 DVR，在两个真实微视频数据集上 WTG@10 相对提升逾 300%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACM MM 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;后处理/模型修正&quot;,&quot;属性遗忘 (Attribute Unlearning)&quot;,&quot;属性推断攻击 (AIA)&quot;,&quot;MMD 分布对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACM_MM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/making_users_indistinguishable_attribute_wise_unlearning_in_recommender_sys/">Making Users Indistinguishable: Attribute-wise Unlearning in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Multimedia">ACM MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出训练后属性遗忘 PoT-AU，用可分性损失+正则损失仅微调用户嵌入，D2D-MMD 变体把攻击 AUC 压至随机水平且推荐指标不降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACM MM&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;不变风险最小化&quot;,&quot;帕累托多目标优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACM_MM/2023/multimodal_rec/pareto_invariant_representation_learning_for_multimedia_recommendation/">Pareto Invariant Representation Learning for Multimedia Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Multimedia">ACM MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 IID-OOD 权衡建模为多目标优化，梯度自适应加权 ERM/IRM 学不变掩码，三数据集 IID 与 OOD 全面领先。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACM MM&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回（训练后嵌入级遗忘后处理）&quot;,&quot;属性遗忘 (Attribute Unlearning)&quot;,&quot;用户嵌入分布对齐&quot;,&quot;可复现性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACM_MM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/reproducibility_companion_paper_making_users_indistinguishable_attribute_wi/">Reproducibility Companion Paper: Making Users Indistinguishable: Attribute-wise Unlearning in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Multimedia">ACM MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>复现属性遗忘 U2U-R/D2D-R 两法：在 NMF/LightGCN 上验证 ML-100K/1M/LFM-2B 的推荐保真度、抗攻击性与百倍级效率优势。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACM MM&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;模态失衡&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;反事实推断&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACM_MM/2024/multimodal_rec/modality_balanced_learning_for_multimedia_recommendation/">Modality-Balanced Learning for Multimedia Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Multimedia">ACM MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出反事实知识蒸馏 CKD，用单模态教师 + 通用/特定双层蒸馏 + 反事实因果效应重加权，解决多模态推荐中模态欠优化，插拔式提升 6 个骨干网络 Recall@20 平均 3.4%–45.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACM MM&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;端侧重排&quot;,&quot;混合精度量化&quot;,&quot;设备-云协同&quot;,&quot;超网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACM_MM/2025/sequential_rec/chord_customizing_hybrid_precision_on_device_model_for_sequential_recommend/">CHORD: Customizing Hybrid-precision On-device Model for Sequential Recommendation with Device-cloud Collaboration</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Multimedia">ACM MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>云端超网络按用户画像生成逐通道混合精度量化策略（2-bit 编码），端侧单次前向完成个性化适配与推理，精度反超全精度基线且传输降 173.8×。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACM MM 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;后处理/嵌入层&quot;,&quot;机器遗忘 (Machine Unlearning)&quot;,&quot;互信息最小化&quot;,&quot;属性推断攻击&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACM_MM/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/lego_a_lightweight_and_efficient_multiple_attribute_unlearning_framework_fo/">LEGO: A Lightweight and Efficient Multiple-Attribute Unlearning Framework for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Multimedia">ACM MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多属性遗忘拆为并行嵌入校准与权重组合两步，以互信息最小化保证同时遗忘，动态需求下近零额外开销。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACM MM&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;端侧推理&quot;,&quot;参数编辑&quot;,&quot;原型聚类&quot;,&quot;端云协同&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACM_MM/2025/cold_start_rec/tackling_device_data_distribution_real_time_shift_via_prototype_based_param/">Tackling Device Data Distribution Real-time Shift via Prototype-based Parameter Editing</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Multimedia">ACM MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>云端神经适配器免反传生成参数编辑矩阵，多原型聚类+跨层知识迁移实现端侧实时分布偏移适配，1s 内完成个性化且 NDCG@5 显著超越微调。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ACM MM&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;模型遗忘/隐私删除&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;反向更新&quot;,&quot;参数组隔离&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ACM_MM/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/tru_targeted_reverse_update_for_efficient_multimodal_recommendation_unlearn/">TRU: Targeted Reverse Update for Efficient Multimodal Recommendation Unlearning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Multimedia">ACM MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示多模态推荐遗忘中排序/模态/模块三层非均匀性，提出门控+分支缩放+参数组隔离的靶向反向更新，逼近重训练前沿且收敛更快。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Gumbel 分布&quot;,&quot;变分编码器&quot;,&quot;评分长尾失衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/collaborative_filtering/a_gumbel_based_rating_prediction_framework_for_imbalanced_recommendation/">A Gumbel-based Rating Prediction Framework for Imbalanced Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 Gumbel 变分编码器把评分频率映射到非正态空间，多尺度卷积融合评论交互特征并加残差个性化，5 数据集上 MAE 最高相对降 57.82%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;逆倾向加权 IPS&quot;,&quot;曝光偏差&quot;,&quot;邻居聚合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/debiasing_neighbor_aggregation_for_graph_neural_network_in_recommender_syst/">Debiasing Neighbor Aggregation for Graph Neural Network in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>把逆倾向权重下推进 GNN 邻居聚合，用加权随机游走拉普拉斯归一化，推理期免训练重聚合即可在 Alexa 上提升 NDCG@5 14.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;逆倾向得分 IPS&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;,&quot;双视角去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/dually_enhanced_propensity_score_estimation_in_sequential_recommendation/">Dually Enhanced Propensity Score Estimation in Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出用户/物品双视角时序 IPS 与双 Transformer，理论无偏并显著提升序列推荐。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果推断&quot;,&quot;回声室缓解&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/dynamic_causal_collaborative_filtering/">Dynamic Causal Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用含环因果图与马尔可夫吸收态分析刻画推荐回声室，提出后门调整+反事实历史的 ∂CCF，内容多样性变化平均降低 7.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;数据基准与离线评估&quot;,&quot;Exposure Bias&quot;,&quot;Missing-at-Random&quot;,&quot;多任务反馈信号&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/kuairand_an_unbiased_sequential_recommendation_dataset_with_randomly_expose/">KuaiRand: An Unbiased Sequential Recommendation Dataset with Randomly Exposed Videos</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>快手无感插入百万级随机曝光视频，构建首个带丰富特征与12类反馈的大规模无偏序列推荐数据集，支撑 IPS 全库评估。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;离线评估基准&quot;,&quot;全观测数据&quot;,&quot;曝光偏差&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/kuairec_a_fully_observed_dataset_and_insights_for_evaluating_recommender_sy/">KuaiRec: A Fully-observed Dataset and Insights for Evaluating Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>发布快手全观测数据集KuaiRec，揭示数据密度与曝光偏差会改变CRS离线评测排序，矩阵补全仅部分缓解。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回(端到端联邦训练)&quot;,&quot;联邦学习&quot;,&quot;用户聚类&quot;,&quot;图神经网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/personalized_federated_recommendation_via_joint_representation_learning_use/">Personalized Federated Recommendation via Joint Representation Learning, User Clustering, and Model Adaptation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 PerFedRec，用联邦 GNN 联合学习表征、用户聚类与三级模型自适应，实现个性化联邦推荐并降低通信开销，HR@10 较 FedAvg 平均提升 29.47%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排（候选集内排序）&quot;,&quot;对话推荐系统&quot;,&quot;流行度偏差&quot;,&quot;双策略学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/quantifying_and_mitigating_popularity_bias_in_conversational_recommender_sy/">Quantifying and Mitigating Popularity Bias in Conversational Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统量化对话推荐中曝光/成功率/对话效用三重流行度偏差，提出 PAL+CSM+DPL 三阶段模型无关去偏框架，在 Yelp/Lastfm 上平均降偏 42.5% 且成功率提升 12.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;排序评测/鲁棒性分析&quot;,&quot;Rank List Sensitivity&quot;,&quot;交互级扰动&quot;,&quot;级联效应&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/rank_list_sensitivity_of_recommender_systems_to_interaction_perturbations/">Rank List Sensitivity of Recommender Systems to Interaction Perturbations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出排序列表敏感度 RLS 指标与 CASPER 级联扰动法，量化单条交互扰动致全库排序剧变，并揭示低准确率用户更脆弱</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;多任务学习与列表级重排&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;端侧推理&quot;,&quot;自适应束搜索&quot;,&quot;实时特征交叉&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/multi_task_reranking/real_time_short_video_recommendation_on_mobile_devices/">Real-time Short Video Recommendation on Mobile Devices</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>端侧轻量排序模型融合实时反馈交叉特征，并以自适应束搜索做上下文感知重排，线上有效播放/点赞/关注分别 +1.28%/+8.22%/+13.6%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;多任务学习与列表级重排&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;校准推荐&quot;,&quot;置信度加权&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/multi_task_reranking/towards_confidence_aware_calibrated_recommendation/">Towards Confidence-aware Calibrated Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出置信度感知校准重排CCL，用用户画像大小加权MILP平衡相关性、校准与多样性，MovieLens上nDCG与覆盖率提升</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;用户侧推荐 (User-side RecSys)&quot;,&quot;度量恢复 (Metric Recovery)&quot;,&quot;公平性公理化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/towards_principled_user_side_recommender_systems/">Towards Principled User-side Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 k-NN 度量恢复理论证明用户侧推荐可行，并提出满足一致性/可靠性/局部性三公理的 Consul 算法，通信开销降低约 8–19 倍且性能近 Oracle。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;增量更新&quot;,&quot;特征先验&quot;,&quot;贝叶斯模型先验&quot;,&quot;长尾物品&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/ctr_feature_interaction/an_incremental_update_framework_for_online_recommenders_with_data_driven_pr/">An Incremental Update Framework for Online Recommenders with Data-Driven Prior</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用特征级 CTR 先验 (FP) 与贝叶斯输出级模型先验 (MP) 约束滑窗增量训练，流式切分评估下 Criteo/CIKM2019 AUC 超 ASMG，线上 CTR +1.99%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;端到端训练与在线推理服务&quot;,&quot;LSH 动态稀疏&quot;,&quot;CPU 推理加速&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/bolt_an_automated_deep_learning_framework_for_training_and_deploying_large_/">BOLT: An Automated Deep Learning Framework for Training and Deploying Large-Scale Search and Recommendation Models on Commodity CPU Hardware</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 LSH 动态稀疏层在商用 CPU 上训练十亿级搜索推荐模型，辅以稀疏超参自动调优与稀疏推理，精度持平 GPU 基线而延迟与碳排降一个数量级。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业可信&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;双稳健估计&quot;,&quot;选择偏差&quot;,&quot;不确定性门控&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/cdr_conservative_doubly_robust_learning_for_debiased_recommendation/">CDR: Conservative Doubly Robust Learning for Debiased Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 DR 去偏中占比&gt;35% 的“有毒插补”，用 MC-Dropout 估计插补均值/方差做保守门控，即插即用，KuaiRand NDCG@5 提升 6.27%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业系统&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果推断&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;,&quot;反事实采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/contrastive_counterfactual_learning_for_causality_aware_interpretable_recom/">Contrastive Counterfactual Learning for Causality-aware Interpretable Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用因果图定位曝光混杂，提出CCL以反事实正采样做对比学习去偏，在Coat/Yahoo! R3无偏测试集全库排序上超越IPS/CVIB。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排（推理期图增广）&quot;,&quot;反事实图增广&quot;,&quot;人口统计学均等&quot;,&quot;二部图扰动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/counterfactual_graph_augmentation_for_consumer_unfairness_mitigation_in_rec/">Counterfactual Graph Augmentation for Consumer Unfairness Mitigation in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 B 维可微二值边选择向量对二部图做反事实增广，以 ApproxNDCG 组间差损失只抬升劣势群体效用，让“解释不公平”直接变成“缓解不公平”。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回（用户表征学习）&quot;,&quot;本地差分隐私 LDP&quot;,&quot;去中心化图神经网络&quot;,&quot;超图兴趣凝聚&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/decentralized_graph_neural_network_for_privacy_preserving_recommendation/">Decentralized Graph Neural Network for Privacy-Preserving Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>DGREC 用「用户内物品超图兴趣凝聚 + 邻域 1-bit 梯度编码共享」，构建首个去中心化 GNN 推荐框架，满足 (1.5,ε)-RDP 且解码无偏，全库排序 recall@20 较最佳隐私基线提升 7.6%–25.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;端到端评测&quot;,&quot;行为测试&quot;,&quot;切片公平性&quot;,&quot;推荐系统评估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/evalrs_a_rounded_evaluation_of_recommender_systems/">EvalRS: a Rounded Evaluation of Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出EvalRS挑战，以LFM音乐数据和RecList切片/行为测试补充MRR/HR，采用留一法、25%用户×4次随机评审与bootstrap校验，推动全方位评测。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;工业架构与可信系统&quot;,&quot;训练系统/端到端&quot;,&quot;分布式混合并行&quot;,&quot;Meta-IO 数据流水线&quot;,&quot;冷启动元学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/g_meta_distributed_meta_learning_in_gpu_clusters_for_large_scale_recommende/">G-Meta: Distributed Meta Learning in GPU Clusters for Large-Scale Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向 MAML 类元学习 DLRM 的 GPU 混合并行训练系统，以 AlltoAll/AllReduce 分工与 Meta-IO 流水线把端到端吞吐提升 22%+、模型交付缩短 4 倍，线上 CVR +6.48%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;实例级嵌入维度搜索&quot;,&quot;伯努利门与极化正则&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/ctr_feature_interaction/ihas_instance_wise_hierarchical_architecture_search_for_deep_learning_recom/">iHAS: Instance-wise Hierarchical Architecture Search for Deep Learning Recommendation Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用带极化正则的伯努利门为每个样本搜出字段级嵌入维度，再聚类并重训簇专属 DLRM；Avazu AUC 0.7815（+0.0038），推理最快。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022 (EvalRS DataChallenge)&quot;,&quot;去偏公平&quot;,&quot;召回 + 列表级配额重排&quot;,&quot;Item-based VAE&quot;,&quot;流行度偏差 MRED&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/item_based_variational_auto_encoder_for_fair_music_recommendation/">Item-based Variational Auto-encoder for Fair Music Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 VAE-CF 转置为物品视角并按流行度分组训练、加组间重建公平正则, 配合配额式列表拼装与 BPRMF 兜底, 用 MRED 与行为测试换取长尾曝光公平。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;数据与评测基准&quot;,&quot;搜索推荐联合建模&quot;,&quot;多行为序列&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/kuaisar_a_unified_search_and_recommendation_dataset/">KuaiSAR: A Unified Search And Recommendation Dataset</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个快手真实搜索-推荐统一行为数据集，含跨服务跳转与搜索入口标签，2.6万用户1966万交互</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;多任务学习与列表级重排&quot;,&quot;重排/模型选择&quot;,&quot;帕累托前沿&quot;,&quot;多目标优化&quot;,&quot;校准推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/multi_task_reranking/post_hoc_selection_of_pareto_optimal_solutions_in_search_and_recommendation/">Post-hoc Selection of Pareto-Optimal Solutions in Search and Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 PDU 从帕累托前沿事后挑出唯一可部署解：用样本级 utopia 距离替代聚合点距离，并支持用户级校准。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;样本选择偏差 (SSB)&quot;,&quot;解耦表征&quot;,&quot;工业级部署&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/ctr_feature_interaction/rec4ad_a_free_lunch_to_mitigate_sample_selection_bias_for_ads_ctr_predictio/">Rec4Ad: A Free Lunch to Mitigate Sample Selection Bias for Ads CTR Prediction in Taobao</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>借推荐样本作免费午餐，用伪样本映射+SABN+对抗对齐/去相关解耦系统混杂与用户兴趣，缓解广告CTR样本选择偏差，线上CTR +6.6%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/排序（物品侧特征编辑）&quot;,&quot;Algorithmic Recourse&quot;,&quot;Counterfactual Explanation&quot;,&quot;多利益相关方可解释性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/recrec_algorithmic_recourse_for_recommender_systems/">RecRec: Algorithmic Recourse for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次形式化内容提供方视角的推荐补救，用特征空间梯度下降+L1/IHT 稀疏化把物品推进目标人群 top-10，三数据集成功率 80–100%、RBO&gt;99.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;工业级系统与模型压缩&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;嵌入表剪枝&quot;,&quot;混合精度量化&quot;,&quot;特征域选择&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/shark_a_lightweight_model_compression_approach_for_large_scale_recommender_/">SHARK: A Lightweight Model Compression Approach for Large-scale Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用一阶泰勒近似替代 Permutation 做特征域剪枝，并按行频次优先级混合 int8/fp16/fp32 量化，30% 内存下 AUC 持平，线上省 70% 存储、QPS +30%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;数据注入(训练期投毒，影响召回与排序)&quot;,&quot;Shilling Attack&quot;,&quot;图节点生成&quot;,&quot;影响函数&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/single_user_injection_for_invisible_shilling_attack_against_recommender_sys/">Single-User Injection for Invisible Shilling Attack against Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次研究仅注入 1 个虚假用户画像的 shilling 攻击，将投毒建模为二部图节点生成（毒性特征生成 + Gumbel-Top-K 连边），单用户 HR@50 最高超最好基线 9.75 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2023&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;端侧推理与更新&quot;,&quot;组合码&quot;,&quot;更新压缩&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/sequential_rec/towards_communication_efficient_model_updating_for_on_device_session_based_/">Towards Communication-Efficient Model Updating for On-Device Session-Based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出组合码压缩物品嵌入，并用栈/队列与MMD自适应压缩端侧更新，在Gowalla/Lastfm上以60倍更小更新逼近云端重训精度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;分组公平&quot;,&quot;item2vec&quot;,&quot;MR-ITF 指标&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/track2vec_fairness_music_recommendation_with_a_gpu_free_customizable_driven/">Track2Vec: fairness music recommendation with a GPU-free customizable-driven framework</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用特征分桶 + item2vec 分组召回 + 投票集成构建无 GPU 的公平音乐推荐，并提出 MR-ITF 逆频率公平指标，榜单超官方基线约 198%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2022&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Triplet Loss&quot;,&quot;矩阵分解&quot;,&quot;公平性评测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/collaborative_filtering/triplet_losses_based_matrix_factorization_for_robust_recommendations/">Triplet Losses-based Matrix Factorization for Robust Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>多重 triplet 损失 + 稀有实体加权矩阵分解，在 LFM-1b 多面公平评测下 MRED 类指标较基线改善 55–89%，总分第 3。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;Uplift Modeling&quot;,&quot;CATE&quot;,&quot;约束优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/uplift_modeling_from_causal_inference_to_personalization/">Uplift Modeling: from Causal Inference to Personalization</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向个性化营销的 Uplift 建模教程：从 CATE 估计到成本约束下的干预分配与生产落地，系统梳理元学习器/定制方法、Qini-AUUC 评估与部署挑战。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;混排与供给调控（策略层）&quot;,&quot;双稳健学习 (DRL)&quot;,&quot;增益建模 Uplift / CATE&quot;,&quot;广告供给个性化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/collaborative_filtering/ads_supply_personalization_via_doubly_robust_learning/">Ads Supply Personalization via Doubly Robust Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 RCT 已知倾向分构造双稳健伪结局，轻量随机森林即可在长周期广告供给密度调控上把 CATE 估计与线上变现效率同时拉升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;内容侧攻击/精排&quot;,&quot;对抗文本改写&quot;,&quot;排名提升损失&quot;,&quot;黑盒替代模型&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/adversarial_text_rewriting_for_text_aware_recommender_systems/">Adversarial Text Rewriting for Text-aware Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 ATR 文本改写攻击，以排名提升损失和两阶段微调攻破文本感知推荐系统，黑盒可行且改写文本较自然。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回/精排共用特征底座&quot;,&quot;NeighborHash 哈希表&quot;,&quot;批量点查 KV 存储&quot;,&quot;版本一致性协议&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/an_enhanced_batch_query_architecture_in_real_time_recommendation/">An Enhanced Batch Query Architecture in Real-time Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 NeighborHash（邻域探测+内联偏移）替代传统哈希，配 NVMe 分层 KV 与版本强一致滚动更新，批量点查吞吐近随机访存上界 90%，线上 CTR +2.2%~6.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;云端协同训练/召回表征&quot;,&quot;联邦学习&quot;,&quot;正则化经验风险最小化&quot;,&quot;局部差分隐私&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/efficient_and_robust_regularized_federated_recommendation/">Efficient and Robust Regularized Federated Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将联邦推荐重构为凸 RERM 问题，提出 RFRec/RFRecF，通信轮次降至 O(log 1/ε)、兼具鲁棒性与差分隐私。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;多任务学习与列表级重排&quot;,&quot;粗排&quot;,&quot;Pareto优化&quot;,&quot;遗忘曲线&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/multi_task_reranking/pareto_based_multi_objective_recommender_system_with_forgetting_curve/">Pareto-based Multi-Objective Recommender System with Forgetting Curve</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Ebbinghaus 遗忘曲线建模快滑负反馈，并以 Pareto 求解器自适应权衡粗排一致性与 recency，KuaiRec 与微信视频号 A/B 验证，GMV +1.45%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2024&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;精排/端到端策略学习&quot;,&quot;Offline RL&quot;,&quot;Reward Shaping&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/rl_bandit_rec/roler_effective_reward_shaping_in_offline_reinforcement_learning_for_recomm/">ROLeR: Effective Reward Shaping in Offline Reinforcement Learning for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用免训练 kNN 奖励整形与簇内距离不确定性惩罚修正 world model 奖励估计，在 KuaiRec 全观测模拟器上累积奖励较 DORL 提升 62.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回/序列排序&quot;,&quot;模型水印&quot;,&quot;自回归OOD序列&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/watermarking_recommender_systems/">Watermarking Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 AOW 自回归 OOD 水印，为序列推荐植入黑盒可验证版权序列；四数据集 Full-Ranking 达 100% R@1，兼顾效用与抗蒸馏/微调。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;离线评测&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-a-Judge&quot;,&quot;Serendipity&quot;,&quot;Prompt Engineering&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/a_universal_framework_for_offline_serendipity_evaluation_in_recommender_sys/">A Universal Framework for Offline Serendipity Evaluation in Recommender Systems via Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM-as-a-Judge 给推荐列表逐项打 1-5 级惊喜度分，绕开人工真值与阈值定义，实现跨数据集跨模型的离线惊喜度统一评测。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;因果推断&quot;,&quot;图聚合权重&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/causality_aware_graph_aggregation_weight_estimator_for_popularity_debiasing/">Causality-aware Graph Aggregation Weight Estimator for Popularity Debiasing in Top-K Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将图聚合证明为后门调整，用 VAE 式编码器-解码器估计无偏聚合权重，配合动量更新，小众物品 Recall@20 提升约 20%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;内容无关审核&quot;,&quot;立场中立&quot;,&quot;观点极化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/content_agnostic_moderation_for_stance_neutral_recommendation/">Content-Agnostic Moderation for Stance-Neutral Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出不依赖内容特征的立场中立化后处理审核，用双聚类离散化打散用户-物品共簇，仿真闭环中显著降低立场偏度且 CTR 仅降约 11%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;持续学习&quot;,&quot;灾难性遗忘&quot;,&quot;稳定性-可塑性权衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/sequential_rec/continual_recommender_systems/">Continual Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统梳理持续推荐的两大技术路线（经验回放与正则化），并延伸至资源受限与序列交互场景，给出可信性、基础模型与搜推统一三大开放方向。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端推荐&quot;,&quot;语义ID&quot;,&quot;对比对齐&quot;,&quot;因果去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/generative_rec/das_dual_aligned_semantic_ids_empowered_industrial_recommender_system/">DAS: Dual-Aligned Semantic IDs Empowered Industrial Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出单阶段双对齐语义ID框架，联合优化RQ-VAE量化与CF去偏对齐，快手全场景A/B eCPM+3.48%、冷启动+8.98%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;流式推荐&quot;,&quot;参数高效侧调&quot;,&quot;可扩展混合专家&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/multimodal_rec/efficient_multimodal_streaming_recommendation_via_expandable_side_mixture_o/">Efficient Multimodal Streaming Recommendation via Expandable Side Mixture-of-Experts</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>冻结 BERT/ViT 主干，用可扩展侧调 MoE 增量吸收新时段知识并按利用率剪枝，在三个流式数据集上 HR@10 全面超越 SOTA 且显存/训练时间更省。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;负采样公平性&quot;,&quot;分组超参优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/evaluating_and_addressing_fairness_across_user_groups_in_negative_sampling_/">Evaluating and Addressing Fairness Across User Groups in Negative Sampling for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>实证揭示负采样比 K 增大使活跃用户增益、非活跃用户受损，提出分组特定负采样比并用带先验 Hyperband 优化，全库排序下 NDCG 提升 2–10%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;持续学习&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/federated_continual_recommendation/">Federated Continual Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 FCRec 新任务与 F3CRec 框架，客户端自适应回放+服务端物品级时序均值，在四个数据集三个 FedRec 骨干上 N@20 平均提升 5.6%–32.1%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图数据增广&quot;,&quot;HSIC 去偏&quot;,&quot;群体公平&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/improving_recommendation_fairness_via_graph_structure_and_representation_au/">Improving Recommendation Fairness via Graph Structure and Representation Augmentation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用双先验假设定位敏感边与敏感特征，做图剪枝+特征掩码双增广，HSIC去偏+对比学习保效用，ML-1M 上 DP@10 降 3.4%、EO@10 降 7.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;观看时长建模&quot;,&quot;因果去偏标注&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/leveraging_watch_time_feedback_for_short_video_recommendations_a_causal_lab/">Leveraging Watch-time Feedback for Short-Video Recommendations: A Causal Labeling Framework</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用分位数把观看时长转成 WPR 与二值标签，并在标注阶段按视频时长分组做因果去偏，离线全指标最优、线上观看时长 +2.23%。</p>
</dd>
