<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;缓存缩放律&quot;,&quot;目标注意力&quot;,&quot;流式训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/marm_unlocking_the_future_of_recommendation_systems_through_memory_augmenta/">MARM: Unlocking the Future of Recommendation Systems through Memory Augmentation and Scalable Complexity</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以外部缓存替代多层自注意力，将序列兴趣建模复杂度从 O(n²d) 降至 O(n·d)，离线 GAUC +0.43%、线上人均时长 +2.079%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;终身行为序列&quot;,&quot;下一动作损失&quot;,&quot;推理系统优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/sequential_rec/transact_v2_lifelong_user_action_sequence_modeling_on_pinterest_recommendat/">TransAct V2: Lifelong User Action Sequence Modeling on Pinterest Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 NN 检索压缩终身序列 + 下一动作对比损失 + Triton 融合推理，Pinterest 线上 Repin +6.35%、Hide -12.80%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;推理阶段数据治理&quot;,&quot;数据最小化&quot;,&quot;Greedy Removal&quot;,&quot;隐私合规&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/what_data_is_really_necessary_a_feasibility_study_of_inference_data_minimiz/">What Data is Really Necessary? A Feasibility Study of Inference Data Minimization for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出性能约束式推理数据最小化问题与 Greedy Removal 算法，在三个隐式反馈数据集上实现最高 84.5% 数据削减且 PRR 保持 98%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;图重连&quot;,&quot;环境条件建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/graph_rec/beyond_observed_auxiliary_relations_environment_conditioned_modeling_for_mu/">Beyond Observed Auxiliary Relations: Environment-Conditioned Modeling for Multi-Behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以辅助行为可观测性为环境条件，用去偏稠密化与目标引导剪枝双模块重连多行为图，隐藏目标物品 HR@10 最高提升 44.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;竞价感知建模&quot;,&quot;多阶段一致性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/ctr_feature_interaction/bidding_aware_retrieval_for_multi_stage_consistency_in_online_advertising/">Bidding-Aware Retrieval for Multi-Stage Consistency in Online Advertising</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将竞价信号注入召回打分，用单调性约束+多任务蒸馏+任务注意力精炼实现召回-精排 eCPM 一致性，线上收入 +4.32%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回前过滤&quot;,&quot;内容过时性&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;多模态流量预测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/decomposing_staleness_in_recommender_systems_a_dual_filter_framework_for_su/">Decomposing Staleness in Recommender Systems: A Dual-Filter Framework for Supersession and Decay</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将推荐内容过时性拆解为“取代”与“衰减”双机制，用 LLM 蒸馏成对分类器与多模态流量比预测器做上游过滤，Google Discover 两年部署使过时举报降 54.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;置信度校准&quot;,&quot;目录忠实性幻觉&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/llm_rec/do_llm_recommenders_know_when_they_re_hallucinating_auditing_confidence_cal/">Do LLM Recommenders Know When They&#x27;re Hallucinating? Auditing Confidence Calibration in Catalog Faithfulness</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>联合审计零样本 LLM 推荐的目录幻觉率与言语化置信度校准，揭示校准误差方向由目录而非模型决定，并给出匹配器验证协议。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;反馈回路仿真&quot;,&quot;LLM幻觉与偏见诊断&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/echotrace_diagnosing_recursive_risks_in_llm_powered_recommender_systems/">EchoTrace: Diagnosing Recursive Risks in LLM-Powered Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出角色感知三阶段诊断框架，用受控反馈回路仿真追踪 LLM 偏见与幻觉在 LLM4RS 中的递归放大与生态极化。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多值因果干预&quot;,&quot;去偏CTR&quot;,&quot;Uplift建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/ctr_feature_interaction/jointly_optimizing_debiased_ctr_and_uplift_for_coupons_marketing_a_unified_/">Jointly Optimizing Debiased CTR and Uplift for Coupons Marketing: A Unified Causal Framework</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>UniMVT 用 MoE 解耦混淆表征 + logit 空间单调线性假设，统一估计去偏 Base CTR 与多值券面额 Uplift，线上收入 +18.14%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-Judge&quot;,&quot;语义ID&quot;,&quot;离线评估指标&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/llm_based_user_product_relevance_evaluation_for_dynamic_product_advertising/">LLM-based User-Product Relevance Evaluation for Dynamic Product Advertising</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 SID 分组意图压缩 + 智能体指南蒸馏小模型，构建无查询的 DPA 召回离线相关性指标，与线上 CP/CR/CVR 强对齐。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;语义ID生成式检索&quot;,&quot;静态价值先验重排&quot;,&quot;流行度偏差&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/generative_rec/revisiting_generative_vs_dense_retrieval_a_static_value_prior_reverses_the_/">Revisiting Generative vs. Dense Retrieval: A Static Value Prior Reverses the Gap</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在受控 GR/DR 对比下揭示指标选择反转胜负，用免训练静态价值先验重排 DR 即反超 GR 最佳物品价值并保持召回领先。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;轮播界面评估&quot;,&quot;位置折扣&quot;,&quot;眼动数据&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/revisiting_n2dcg_an_empirically_grounded_reformulation_of_carousel_recommen/">Revisiting N2DCG: An Empirically Grounded Reformulation of Carousel Recommendation Evaluation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>修正 N2DCG 的理想排序类别约束与镜像 F 型折扣，提出类别感知归一化与行-页折扣，在眼动数据与 2 万次布局仿真中显著提升评估保真度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;语义ID&quot;,&quot;工件级诊断&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/generative_rec/sidinspector_a_mapping_first_diagnostic_resource_for_semantic_id_tokenizers/">SIDInspector: A Mapping-First Diagnostic Resource for Semantic-ID Tokenizers</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出映射优先的 SID 分词器诊断资源，用 D1–D5 探针在生成器训练前分离容量、可寻址性与行为前缀对齐，并给出四类分词器工件画像。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;CIKM&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;Recall Ceiling&quot;,&quot;评估协议&quot;,&quot;闭候选重排&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/CIKM/2026/llm_rec/the_recall_ceiling_of_llm_recommendation_reranking/">The Recall Ceiling of LLM Recommendation Reranking</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Information and Knowledge Management">CIKM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>证明闭候选重排器 NDCG 受自身窗口召回率硬上界约束，Oracle 协议高估 92–95%，九类 LLM 与监督重排器在真实检索下均无法显著超越 CF。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Accuracy-Bias Tradeoff&quot;,&quot;Self-Sampling&quot;,&quot;tIPS&quot;,&quot;Self-Training&quot;,&quot;Self-Evaluation&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/self_sampling_training_and_evaluation_for_the_accuracy_bias_tradeoff_in_rec/">Self-Sampling Training and Evaluation for the Accuracy-Bias Tradeoff in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出SSTE，以自采样构造多偏差环境，联合训练与鲁棒自评估实现有益/有害偏差权衡；在Yahoo! R3/Product无偏测试与腾讯理财通在线A/B中均获提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;因果结构学习&quot;,&quot;域对抗训练&quot;,&quot;OOD 泛化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2025/cross_domain_multi_scenario/cross_domain_causal_preference_learning_for_out_of_distribution_recommendat/">Cross-Domain Causal Preference Learning for Out-of-Distribution Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用域对抗抽取域共享偏好、DAG 学习潜属性因果结构，缓解目标域稀疏下的 OOD 泛化，OOD 测试 HR@10 提升约 2-6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;DASFAA&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果去偏&quot;,&quot;变分推断解耦&quot;,&quot;未观测混杂&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/DASFAA/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/separating_and_learning_latent_confounders_to_enhancing_user_preferences_mo/">Separating and Learning Latent Confounders to Enhancing User Preferences Modeling</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Conference on Database Systems for Advanced Applications">DASFAA</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将前序推荐系统作为未观测混杂代理，用变分推断在隐空间解耦用户偏好与混杂，实现通用去偏，五数据集 Recall@10 全面领先。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;消费者公平&quot;,&quot;可复现性基准&quot;,&quot;Demographic Parity&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/consumer_fairness_in_recommender_systems_contextualizing_definitions_and_mi/">Consumer Fairness in Recommender Systems: Contextualizing Definitions and Mitigations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统复现 8 个消费者公平缓解方法并在统一协议下对照，揭示去偏对效用冲击微弱、DP 与 KS 两类公平度量互不一致、且差异影响并非总伤及少数群体。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回评测诊断&quot;,&quot;人口统计公平性&quot;,&quot;流行度偏置&quot;,&quot;Explainable Boosting Machine&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/revisiting_popularity_and_demographic_biases_in_recommender_evaluation_and_/">Revisiting Popularity and Demographic Biases in Recommender Evaluation and Effectiveness</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>复现并扩展 Ekstrand 等的公平性研究：用三种年龄分桶策略 + pop-index + EBM 归因，揭示效用差异主要由使用量与内容流行度驱动，而非纯人口属性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;Filter Bubble&quot;,&quot;动态仿真&quot;,&quot;校准推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/evolution_of_filter_bubbles_and_polarization_in_news_recommendation/">Evolution of Filter Bubbles and Polarization in News Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 4 万篇立场标注新闻与 500 个 Pew 仿真用户构建长程推荐闭环，揭示即使无说服效应，MF 反馈回路也会自发极化，且 SOTA 校准干预仅减缓不能阻断。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2023&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/可解释重排&quot;,&quot;路径推理&quot;,&quot;可复现性基准&quot;,&quot;推荐公平性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2023/graph_rec/knowledge_is_power_understanding_is_impact_utility_and_beyond_goals_explana/">Knowledge is Power, Understanding is Impact: Utility and Beyond Goals, Explanation Quality, and Fairness in Path Reasoning Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>复现 PGPR/CAFE/UCPR 三篇路径推理推荐，用 16 指标统一协议揭示其以效用与覆盖度换取惊喜度、多样性与可解释保真度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;差分隐私&quot;,&quot;安全多方计算&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/privacy_preserving_fair_item_ranking/">Privacy-Preserving Fair Item Ranking</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次将EOAA公平重排与MPC+DP结合，在三个真实数据集上以NDCG≥0.8降低物品不公平。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2023&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;在线服务/召回&quot;,&quot;API-centric 平台&quot;,&quot;多域推荐系统&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2023/cross_domain_multi_scenario/uptrendz_api_centric_real_time_recommendations_in_multi_domain_settings/">Uptrendz: API-Centric Real-time Recommendations in Multi-Domain Settings</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 API 为中心、schema 驱动的实时多域推荐平台，用系统级/物品级双层域划分与场景化算法配置实现一套服务支撑多业务，MovieLens-100k 与创业辅导两域实证可部署性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排（pairwise 训练期采样）&quot;,&quot;提供者组曝光公平&quot;,&quot;代价敏感元学习&quot;,&quot;重加权负采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/a_cost_sensitive_meta_learning_strategy_for_fair_provider_exposure_in_recom/">A Cost-Sensitive Meta-Learning Strategy for Fair Provider Exposure in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用类别代价重加权三元组负采样，使提供者组曝光对齐目录占比，且 NDCG 无损（ML-1M Exp@10 由 1.4% 升至 6.0%）。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2024&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;多轮交互在线推荐&quot;,&quot;联邦学习&quot;,&quot;本地差分隐私&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2024/agent_conversational_rec/federated_conversational_recommender_system/">Federated Conversational Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>FedCRS 将历史兴趣估计与交互式偏好诱导两阶段全部去中心化，用梯度裁剪+Laplace 噪声实现用户级 LDP，SR@15 逼近非隐私 SOTA</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2024&quot;,&quot;多任务学习与列表级重排&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;后处理分桶&quot;,&quot;提供者公平&quot;,&quot;类型校准&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2024/multi_task_reranking/moregin_multi_objective_recommendation_at_the_global_and_individual_levels/">MOReGIn: Multi-Objective Recommendation at the Global and Individual Levels</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>分桶后处理重排同时实现全局提供者地理公平与个体类型校准，在 Movies/Songs 双数据集上将 ΔTotal 降为 0、校准误差降一个量级。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2024&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;GNN&quot;,&quot;可复现性评测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2024/sequential_rec/performance_comparison_of_session_based_recommendation_algorithms_based_on_/">Performance Comparison of Session-based Recommendation Algorithms based on GNNs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>统一复现8个GNN会话推荐模型，发现简单kNN/规则基线在MRR@20上不劣甚至更优，并揭示种子与嵌入维度敏感。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/重排与评测协议&quot;,&quot;top-n 选择策略&quot;,&quot;超参优化偏差&quot;,&quot;显著性检验&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/revealing_the_hidden_impact_of_top_n_metrics_on_optimization_in_recommender/">Revealing the Hidden Impact of Top-N Metrics on Optimization in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>穷举 252 种 top-n 选择策略 × 20 数据集 × 11 算法，证明 top-n 评估截断带来的优化偏差在统计上可忽略（增益 ≤1.5%）</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;图神经网络推荐&quot;,&quot;鲁棒性评估&quot;,&quot;边级扰动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/robustness_in_fairness_against_edge_level_perturbations_in_gnn_based_recomm/">Robustness in Fairness against Edge-level Perturbations in GNN-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>ECIR 2024 去偏公平研究首次评估图推荐系统在边级扰动下的公平性鲁棒性，设计可微扰动目标，揭示消费者侧公平性极为脆弱。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;排序（Top-10 推荐）&quot;,&quot;Differential Privacy&quot;,&quot;Popularity Bias&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/the_impact_of_differential_privacy_on_recommendation_accuracy_and_popularit/">The Impact of Differential Privacy on Recommendation Accuracy and Popularity Bias</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统实证 1-Bit 差分隐私对三种协同过滤模型准确率与流行度偏差的影响，揭示 ε 越小准确率降幅越大且长尾偏好用户受损更重。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;表示解耦&quot;,&quot;可解释性量化&quot;,&quot;可复现性研究&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/are_representation_disentanglement_and_interpretability_linked_in_recommend/">Are Representation Disentanglement and Interpretability Linked in Recommendation Models? A Critical Review and Reproducibility Study</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>复现5个推荐模型×4数据集，量化解耦度与可解释性，发现解耦与效果无关但与表示可解释性强正相关。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;原型矩阵分解&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;文化多样性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/embedding_cultural_diversity_in_prototype_based_recommender_systems/">Embedding Cultural Diversity in Prototype-based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在 ProtoMF 原型矩阵分解上引入 K-近邻原型过滤与原型正交分布正则，以物品国别为文化代理缓解流行度偏差，全库排序下长尾平均排名降 27%、HR@10 反升 2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;用户画像预处理&quot;,&quot;提供方公平性&quot;,&quot;校准多样性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/how_to_diversify_any_personalized_recommender/">How to Diversify any Personalized Recommender?</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出用户画像级预处理：用逻辑回归识别用户代表类别、userKNN 生成候选，再按 λ% 增删交互，使任意推荐器在保持精度的同时提升多样性与少数类曝光公平。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;CVaR-DRO&quot;,&quot;Minimax Group Fairness&quot;,&quot;SASRec&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/improving_minimax_group_fairness_in_sequential_recommendation/">Improving Minimax Group Fairness in Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 CVaR-DRO 首次引入 SASRec 序列推荐，无需组标注即可提升小众/短序列用户的最差组 NDCG@20，Retailrocket 整体提升约 6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;异构知识图谱&quot;,&quot;多利益相关方可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;地域公平性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/okra_an_explainable_heterogeneous_multi_stakeholder_job_recommender_system/">OKRA: an Explainable, Heterogeneous, Multi-Stakeholder Job Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>异构知识图谱+多头GATv2构建求职者/企业双侧嵌入，谐波均值融合打分，nDCG@10较六基线最高提升约18%，并揭示城乡地域偏差。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;重复偏差&quot;,&quot;混合整数线性规划&quot;,&quot;下一篮推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/repeat_bias_aware_optimization_of_beyond_accuracy_metrics_for_next_basket_r/">Repeat-bias-aware Optimization of Beyond-accuracy Metrics for Next Basket Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 MILP 重排同时优化多样性/物品公平与重复偏差，在三个零售数据集上以可接受 Recall 损失显著改善 DS、logDP 与 RepBias。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2026&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图划分相似度&quot;,&quot;可复现性评测&quot;,&quot;长尾分析&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2026/collaborative_filtering/a_reproducible_and_fair_evaluation_of_partition_aware_collaborative_filteri/">A Reproducible and Fair Evaluation of Partition-aware Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在统一用户留出划分与 TPE 调参下复现 FPSR/FPSR+，发现 BISM 在 Amazon-CDs 反超，FPSR 家族在长尾显著占优。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2026&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;模态剔除&quot;,&quot;实证复现研究&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2026/multimodal_rec/are_multimodal_embeddings_truly_beneficial_for_recommendation_a_deep_dive_i/">Are Multimodal Embeddings Truly Beneficial for Recommendation? A Deep Dive into Whole vs. Individual Modalities</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用模态剔除（常量/噪声替换）在14个模型上做受控实证，发现文本主导、图像近乎无效，简单融合基线增益源于架构伪影。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测指标&quot;,&quot;个体公平&quot;,&quot;用户相似度&quot;,&quot;有效性差异&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/measuring_individual_user_fairness_with_user_similarity_and_effectiveness_d/">Measuring Individual User Fairness with User Similarity and Effectiveness Disparity</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 PUF 指标，用用户相似度加权有效性差异，首次同时刻画个体公平的两大要素，4 数据集 7 推荐器验证其敏感性与计算效率。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ECIR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;模糊逻辑&quot;,&quot;可解释规则学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ECIR/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/modeling_behavioral_patterns_in_news_recommendations_using_fuzzy_neural_net/">Modeling Behavioral Patterns in News Recommendations Using Fuzzy Neural Networks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：European Conference on Information Retrieval">ECIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用多层模糊神经网络端到端学习可读逻辑规则预测新闻点击，阈值可调控制规则复杂度，EB-NeRD 上 nDCG@10 相对 NRMS 提升约 21.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;端到端训练（联邦推荐）&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐投毒攻击&quot;,&quot;模型投毒与梯度操纵&quot;,&quot;隐私保护与鲁棒聚合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/fedrecattack_model_poisoning_attack_to_federated_recommendation/">FedRecAttack: Model Poisoning Attack to Federated Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用仅1%公开交互反解全局用户隐向量，配合指数尾部代理损失生成限幅分摊的投毒梯度，3%恶意用户即可把目标物品ER@5推至0.94+且HR@10仅降2.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2023&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;匹配/派单&quot;,&quot;Contextual Bandit&quot;,&quot;Capacity-Aware Assignment&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2023/rl_bandit_rec/towards_capacity_aware_broker_matching_from_recommendation_to_assignment/">Towards Capacity-Aware Broker Matching: From Recommendation to Assignment</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将经纪人匹配从 top-k 推荐转为容量感知指派，NN-UCB 在线估容量，MDP 价值函数+KM 全局分配，CBS 无损加速。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;端到端联邦训练&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;模型异构&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/hetefedrec_federated_recommender_systems_with_model_heterogeneity/">HeteFedRec: Federated Recommender Systems with Model Heterogeneity</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次让联邦推荐客户端训练不同维度物品嵌入，用填充聚合、双任务学习、去相关正则和关系蒸馏融合知识，采样NDCG@20提升1.9%–20.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;端到端训练&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;模型知识产权&quot;,&quot;预测蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/hide_your_model_a_parameter_transmission_free_federated_recommender_system/">Hide Your Model: A Parameter Transmission-free Federated Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出免参数传输联邦推荐框架，仅交换扰动预测分数，兼顾平台模型知识产权与用户数据隐私，通信降至KB级。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐安全/训练阶段&quot;,&quot;投毒攻击&quot;,&quot;流行物品嵌入&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/preventing_the_popular_item_embedding_based_attack_in_federated_recommendat/">Preventing the Popular Item Embedding Based Attack in Federated Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出模型无关、无需先验的联邦推荐投毒攻击 PIECK 与双正则防御，全库排序下 ER@10 显著提升且 HR 稳定。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2025&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;关键词推荐&quot;,&quot;二分图抽取&quot;,&quot;极端多标签分类&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2025/ctr_feature_interaction/graphex_a_graph_based_extraction_method_for_advertiser_keyphrase_recommenda/">GraphEx: A Graph-based Extraction Method for Advertiser Keyphrase Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用二分图从商品标题抽取关键词排列，绕开点击数据偏差，CPU 毫秒级推理，线上广告收入提升 8.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDE 2025&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;Steiner Tree&quot;,&quot;路径解释摘要&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDE/2025/graph_rec/path_based_summary_explanations_for_graph_recommenders_extended_version/">Path-based summary explanations for graph recommenders (extended version)</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Engineering">ICDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次提出路径解释的摘要化：用 Steiner Tree 与 Prize-Collecting Steiner Tree 将多条用户-物品解释路径压缩为连通子图，在可理解性、多样性、隐私与可扩展性上全面优于逐条路径基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异构图神经网络&quot;,&quot;差分隐私&quot;,&quot;隐私预算双层优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/heterogeneous_graph_neural_network_for_privacy_preserving_recommendation/">Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network for Privacy-Preserving Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>HeteDP 用「语义感知特征加噪 + DP-SGD 拓扑重构 + 双层隐私预算分配」双阶段保护异构图推荐，LP 最高相对提升 37.2%，但节点分类在强隐私下明显掉点。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;动态子网络路由&quot;,&quot;权重切片超网&quot;,&quot;Gumbel-Softmax&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2022/sequential_rec/one_person_one_model_learning_compound_router_for_sequential_recommendation/">One Person, One Model—Learning Compound Router for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>CANet 用轻量路由器为每条用户序列在嵌入/隐藏/层深三维复合空间内路由子网络，权重切片共享超网，Gumbel-Softmax 端到端可微，全库排序下减算 55~65% 而 NDCG 仅降约 1.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2023&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Thompson Sampling&quot;,&quot;自反馈闭环去偏&quot;,&quot;序贯训练范式&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2023/rl_bandit_rec/epsilon_non_greedy_a_bandit_approach_for_unbiased_recommendation_via_unifor/">Epsilon non-Greedy: A Bandit Approach for Unbiased Recommendation via Uniform Data</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用小编量均匀数据蒸馏无偏教师、Dropout 近似 Thompson 采样做非贪婪探索，并提出序贯训练范式模拟自反馈闭环，AUC 相对提升 4.5%–15.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDMW&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦学习&quot;,&quot;异质图注意力&quot;,&quot;跨校选课推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/heterogeneity_aware_cross_school_electives_recommendation_a_hybrid_federate/">Heterogeneity-aware Cross-school Electives Recommendation: a Hybrid Federated Approach</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>为跨校选课构建校内异质图 + 边感知注意力 + 约束矩阵分解联邦训练，在 1:99 采样排序下 HR@10 相对 FedGNN 提升 15.9%~23.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2023&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;组合嵌入 (Compositional Embedding)&quot;,&quot;可学习软阈值剪枝&quot;,&quot;嵌入表内存压缩&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2023/collaborative_filtering/learning_compact_compositional_embeddings_via_regularized_pruning_for_recom/">Learning Compact Compositional Embeddings via Regularized Pruning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>CERP 用双码本平衡哈希组合嵌入 + 软阈值联合剪枝 + 互补性正则，在 90%/95%/99% 稀疏预算下把嵌入层压到 18 维仍超越 PEP/QR。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;序列差分隐私&quot;,&quot;跨域序列推荐&quot;,&quot;随机响应机制&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/privacy_preserving_cross_domain_sequential_recommendation/">Privacy-Preserving Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>定义兼顾物品 ID 与顺序信息的序列差分隐私 SDP，设计扰动机制 M 在辅助域一次性加噪，使任意跨域序列模型可免改造地合法迁移知识。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;训练期数据投毒（数据层）&quot;,&quot;Transformer-GAN 伪造画像生成&quot;,&quot;评论文本增强与提示微调&quot;,&quot;推荐系统鲁棒性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/review_incorporated_model_agnostic_profile_injection_attacks_on_recommender/">Review-Incorporated Model-Agnostic Profile Injection Attacks on Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>R-Trojan 首次将商品评论文本引入投毒画像生成，用 Transformer-GAN 三模块（生成/代理/检测）联合优化迁移性与隐蔽性，在 3 个黑盒推荐器上 HR@10 最高提升 53.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;生成式端到端&quot;,&quot;条件扩散模型&quot;,&quot;反事实公平&quot;,&quot;群体公平&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/diffairec_generative_fair_recommender_with_conditional_diffusion_model/">DifFaiRec: Generative Fair Recommender with Conditional Diffusion Model</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用条件扩散模型重建评分向量，并以注意力反事实模块降低敏感属性敏感性，把公平与精度压缩为单一去噪损失，在 Full-List 排序下 Recall/NDCG 与群体公平指标同升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;推荐遗忘&quot;,&quot;RAG&quot;,&quot;遗忘偏置 (Unlearning Bias)&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/customized_retrieval_augmented_generation_with_llm_for_debiasing_recommenda/">Customized Retrieval-Augmented Generation with LLM for Debiasing Recommendation Unlearning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将推荐遗忘重构为 RAG 检索端过滤问题，用三种检索策略实现用户级原子遗忘，平均加速 4.5× 且保留约 90% 原模型效用。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2025&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;反事实推断&quot;,&quot;结构因果模型&quot;,&quot;全空间 CVR 建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2025/ctr_feature_interaction/on_predicting_post_click_conversion_rate_via_counterfactual_inference/">On Predicting Post-Click Conversion Rate via Counterfactual Inference</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 SCM 对未点击样本做 do(C=1) 反事实推断生成转化伪标签，全空间训练 CVR 模型，离线 AUC 平均 +1.01%，线上 CVR +17.35%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;数据投毒/鲁棒性评测&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent&quot;,&quot;群体托攻击&quot;,&quot;角色切换&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/an_efficient_and_effective_agentic_group_shilling_attack_on_recommender_sys/">An Efficient and Effective Agentic Group Shilling Attack on Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出协调者驱动的角色切换多智能体群体托攻击框架 AGAS，黑盒免训练、动态策略，HR@10 相对提升 155%–198% 且显著削弱检测器。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;并行迭代精炼&quot;,&quot;推理时可控生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2026/generative_rec/controllable_generative_recommendation_via_guided_token_refinement/">Controllable Generative Recommendation via Guided Token Refinement</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将语义 ID 生成从自回归改为并行掩码填充，用 logit 引导+置信度精炼+前缀树约束实现免训练实时多样性控制，Diversity@10 提升约 15% 且有效率保持 99.97%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;推荐遗忘&quot;,&quot;因果反事实解释&quot;,&quot;输出级分数干预&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/explain_then_forget_recommendation_unlearning_under_asymmetric_access_const/">Explain, Then Forget: Recommendation Unlearning under Asymmetric Access Constraint</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向 MLaaS 非对称访问约束的免参数推荐遗忘框架，用因果解释定位最小支持集并做输出级分数干预，遗忘效果逼近全量重训且延迟降低 9–55 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;观看时长分布预测&quot;,&quot;混合密度网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2026/sequential_rec/hierarchical_exponential_gaussian_mixtures_for_watch_time_distribution_pred/">Hierarchical Exponential-Gaussian Mixtures for Watch-Time Distribution Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用分层 skip-watch 分解与结构化初始化修复 EGMN 的方差坍缩与组件冗余，在固定骨干换头协议下提升 XAUC 与阈值事件预测，线上会话深度 +9.26%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICDM 2026&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;位置编码对齐&quot;,&quot;数据熵结构&quot;,&quot;评估协议泄漏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICDM/2026/sequential_rec/revisiting_self_attentive_sequential_recommendation_beyond_the_llm_paradigm/">Revisiting Self-Attentive Sequential Recommendation Beyond the LLM Paradigm</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE International Conference on Data Mining">ICDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 SASRec 为锚点对比推荐与语言建模的熵结构差异，诊断四条继承假设并给出数据优先议程，含同模型双视图实证。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;训练底座（分布式嵌入表分片）&quot;,&quot;强化学习设备放置&quot;,&quot;可泛化成本网络&quot;,&quot;算子融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/dreamshard_generalizable_embedding_table_placement_for_recommender_systems/">DreamShard: Generalizable Embedding Table Placement for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用成本网络估计 MDP 替代真实 GPU 执行，训练可泛化的 RL 策略完成嵌入表分片，在 DLRM/生产表上较最强基线最高提速 19%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;策略优化/LTV&quot;,&quot;偏好动态建模&quot;,&quot;安全偏移信任域&quot;,&quot;非齐次HMM&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2022/rl_bandit_rec/estimating_and_penalizing_induced_preference_shifts_in_recommender_systems/">Estimating and Penalizing Induced Preference Shifts in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可学用户偏好动态模型预测新策略诱导的偏好偏移，并以“安全偏移”信任域惩罚 RL 推荐器，兼顾参与度与偏好安全。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回/排序（全链路风险目标）&quot;,&quot;域传输 (Domain Transportation)&quot;,&quot;Wasserstein 约束&quot;,&quot;逆概率加权 (IPW)&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/from_intervention_to_domain_transportation_a_novel_perspective_to_optimize_/">From Intervention to Domain Transportation: A Novel Perspective to Optimize Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将推荐视为域传输，用 Wasserstein 约束把训练域与部署域绑定，以 WGAN 式极小极大博弈求解，在 Leave-One-Out Full-Ranking 协议下显著提升推荐器。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;延迟反馈&quot;,&quot;重要性重加权&quot;,&quot;流式训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2022/ctr_feature_interaction/generalized_delayed_feedback_model_with_post_click_information_in_recommend/">Generalized Delayed Feedback Model with Post-Click Information in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将点击后行为统一为随机后验反馈，用条件熵与时间衰减重加权代理损失，流式训练下 CVR 相对提升至 Oracle 差距的 74.9%/79.4%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/相关性预估&quot;,&quot;因果用户建模&quot;,&quot;不变学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/in_the_eye_of_the_beholder_robust_prediction_with_causal_user_modeling/">In the Eye of the Beholder: Robust Prediction with Causal User Modeling</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将用户建模为有界理性因果决策者，用因果/反因果与相信者/怀疑者类型选择MMD正则，提升分布漂移下相关性预测鲁棒性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;角度 Margin 对比损失&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/incorporating_bias_aware_margins_into_contrastive_loss_for_collaborative_fi/">Incorporating Bias-aware Margins into Contrastive Loss for Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用流行度偏置提取器为每个交互定制角度 margin 并注入 softmax 对比损失，在 8 个数据集的平衡/非平衡/时间切分评测上同时提升头尾性能 12.1%–58.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;模型选型 / AutoML（元学习）&quot;,&quot;数据集元特征（meta-features）&quot;,&quot;算法选择与超参预测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/on_the_generalizability_and_predictability_of_recommender_systems/">On the Generalizability and Predictability of Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>85 个数据集、315 个指标的元学习选型研究，RecZilla 直接预测算法与超参数，%Diff 33.2 优于 cf4cf-meta 43.5，推理仅 6.7 秒。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;工业级基准数据集&quot;,&quot;全链路评测基准&quot;,&quot;跨场景推荐&quot;,&quot;多任务 CTR&quot;,&quot;真实负反馈&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/tenrec_a_large_scale_multipurpose_benchmark_dataset_for_recommender_systems/">Tenrec: A Large-scale Multipurpose Benchmark Dataset for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>腾讯四场景 500 万用户/1.4 亿交互数据集，含真实负反馈与跨域重叠，在 10 类任务上实证经典基线差距远小于文献宣称。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果推荐&quot;,&quot;倾向得分估计&quot;,&quot;IPS&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/estimating_propensity_for_causality_based_recommendation_without_exposure_d/">Estimating Propensity for Causality-based Recommendation without Exposure Data</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>仅用交互数据，以流行度成对先验与 Beta 正则估计倾向/曝光，接入 IPS 因果推荐，在 DH/ML 上逼近真值。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;推荐策略分析&quot;,&quot;Price of Anarchy&quot;,&quot;Random Utility Model&quot;,&quot;内容创作者博弈&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/how_bad_is_top_k_recommendation_under_competing_content_creators/">How Bad is Top-$K$ Recommendation under Competing Content Creators?</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以价格无政府比 (PoA) 证明：内容创作者以用户参与度博弈时，top-$K$推荐的福利损失永远被$1+\frac{1}{(1+\beta)\log K}$ 小常数上界卡死且紧；用户曝光激励则可任意差。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS 2023&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;观点动力学&quot;,&quot;冲突感知&quot;,&quot;链接推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2023/social_group_rec/on_the_relationship_between_relevance_and_conflict_in_online_social_link_re/">On the Relationship Between Relevance and Conflict in Online Social Link Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用FJ观点动力学分析社交链接推荐中相关性与冲突减少的关系，提出冲突感知CA指标并在Reddit/Twitter评估13种算法。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排/排序学习&quot;,&quot;战略激励&quot;,&quot;多样性正则&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/performative_recommendation_diversifying_content_via_strategic_incentives/">Performative Recommendation: Diversifying Content via Strategic Incentives</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将创作者战略响应嵌入可微排序，以多样性正则激励内容多样化；Yelp 动态重训练下同 NDCG 损失使多样性翻倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR 2023&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;在线策略&quot;,&quot;Interaction-Grounded Learning&quot;,&quot;潜在奖励学习&quot;,&quot;个性化去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2023/rl_bandit_rec/personalized_reward_learning_with_interaction_grounded_learning_igl/">Personalized Reward Learning with Interaction-Grounded Learning (IGL)</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用逆运动学与3状态潜在奖励学习个性化隐式反馈，在仿真、Facebook新闻与生产图像推荐中提升公平性并抑制负反馈。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;平台机制设计&quot;,&quot;潜在博弈&quot;,&quot;尾部积分奖励&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/rethinking_incentives_in_recommender_systems_are_monotone_rewards_always_be/">Rethinking Incentives in Recommender Systems: Are Monotone Rewards Always Beneficial?</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>证明功绩制单调奖励 M3 存在常数福利损失，提出尾部积分 BRM 使创作者竞争成为潜在博弈并可用 BRCM 参数化优化社会福利。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;市场机制/平台生态&quot;,&quot;Supply-Side Equilibrium&quot;,&quot;Specialization&quot;,&quot;博弈论&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/supply_side_equilibria_in_recommender_systems/">Supply-Side Equilibria in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 D 维双线性博弈与凸对偶刻画推荐系统供给侧专业化相变，给出单 genre 均衡的充要条件与 β* 阈值，并在 MovieLens-100K 验证阈值随嵌入维度下降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;双重稳健估计 (DR)&quot;,&quot;靶向学习 (Targeted Learning)&quot;,&quot;逆倾向加权 (IPS)&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/tdr_cl_targeted_doubly_robust_collaborative_learning_for_debiased_recommend/">TDR-CL: Targeted Doubly Robust Collaborative Learning for Debiased Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用靶向学习把 DR 的校正项经验均值归零，并将插补误差拆为参数化+非参数化协同更新，Coat/Music 上 AUC、NDCG 全面超越 DR 家族且方差更小。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回/表征学习&quot;,&quot;纵向联邦学习&quot;,&quot;隐私保护图神经网络&quot;,&quot;随机投影&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/vertical_federated_graph_neural_network_for_recommender_system/">Vertical Federated Graph Neural Network for Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个纵向联邦 GNN 推荐框架 VerFedGNN，以随机投影传输邻居聚合矩阵、三元量化扰动公共梯度，RMSE 逼近集中式且去匿名攻击 F1 从 0.90 降至 0.02。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR 2024&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/在线探索策略&quot;,&quot;Strategic Bandit&quot;,&quot;Incentive-Compatible Mechanism&quot;,&quot;Clickbait&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/rl_bandit_rec/bandits_meet_mechanism_design_to_combat_clickbait_in_online_recommendation/">Bandits Meet Mechanism Design to Combat Clickbait in Online Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>把 CTR 建模为自利臂的策略动作，用 UCB-S 的淘汰威胁激励真实点击率，任意均衡下取得 Õ(√KT) 强策略遗憾。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;训练与模型维护（隐私卸载）&quot;,&quot;Machine Unlearning&quot;,&quot;Recommendation Fairness&quot;,&quot;Benchmark&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/cure4rec_a_benchmark_for_recommendation_unlearning_with_deeper_influence/">CURE4Rec: A Benchmark for Recommendation Unlearning with Deeper Influence</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个推荐卸载综合基准，以完整性、效用、效率、公平四维及 core/edge/random 三类卸载集系统评测 5 种方法。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/排序预测&quot;,&quot;因果去偏&quot;,&quot;倾向得分&quot;,&quot;RKHS核平衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/debiased_collaborative_filtering_with_kernel_based_causal_balancing/">Debiased Collaborative Filtering with Kernel-Based Causal Balancing</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用RKHS核自适应平衡倾向得分，提出KBIPS/KBDR，在COAT/MUSIC/PRODUCT提升AUC/NDCG/F1。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;差分隐私&quot;,&quot;加性个性化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/federated_recommendation_with_additive_personalization/">Federated Recommendation with Additive Personalization</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>FedRAP 以稀疏全局物品嵌入加本地个性化偏置实现联邦推荐，在六数据集 Leave-One-Out 采样排序下显著优于 FL 基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;arXiv (2024)&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排/在线曝光分配&quot;,&quot;多边公平约束优化&quot;,&quot;低regret Bandit&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/interpolating_item_and_user_fairness_in_multi_sided_recommendations/">Interpolating Item and User Fairness in Multi-Sided Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出FAIR多边公平约束与FORM低regret在线算法，在Amazon模拟中将公平推荐收入损失从约38%降至&lt;10%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR 2024&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回/训练&quot;,&quot;CVaR&quot;,&quot;iALS&quot;,&quot;卷积平滑&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/collaborative_filtering/safe_collaborative_filtering/">Safe Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以卷积平滑CVaR嵌入iALS，在保持平均Recall的同时显著改善尾部用户排序质量。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排（CF 表征训练）&quot;,&quot;对抗协同过滤&quot;,&quot;投毒攻击鲁棒性&quot;,&quot;个性化扰动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/understanding_and_improving_adversarial_collaborative_filtering_for_robust_/">Understanding and Improving Adversarial Collaborative Filtering for Robust Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>理论证明 ACF 降误差并据此按用户嵌入尺度分配扰动幅度，PamaCF 在 Gowalla/Yelp/MIND 上兼顾推荐与抗投毒。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML 2024&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;平台策略层/流量分配机制&quot;,&quot;博弈论均衡分析&quot;,&quot;探索强度机制设计&quot;,&quot;双层优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/rl_bandit_rec/unveiling_user_satisfaction_and_creator_productivity_trade_offs_in_recommen/">Unveiling User Satisfaction and Creator Productivity Trade-Offs in Recommendation Platforms</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 Cournot 创作竞争博弈 C4 刻画推荐探索强度 β 对创作者产量的反噬，证明 U-β 增、V-β 减的权衡，并用隐函数梯度双层优化求得个性化 β*，仿真福利提升 &gt;20%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;平台生态长期多样性&quot;,&quot;用户-创作者双重影响&quot;,&quot;极化动力学&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/user_creator_feature_polarization_in_recommender_systems_with_dual_influenc/">User-Creator Feature Polarization in Recommender Systems with Dual Influence</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建用户-创作者特征动力学，证明双重影响下推荐系统必然极化，并发现 top-k 截断可反向提升长期多样性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排/推荐策略分配&quot;,&quot;双边公平&quot;,&quot;Price of Fairness&quot;,&quot;冷启动误估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/user_item_fairness_tradeoffs_in_recommendations/">User-Item Fairness Tradeoffs in Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>把用户-物品双边公平建成带物品公平约束的极小极大优化，解析证明「多样性红利」与「冷启动误估放大」，并在 arXiv 原型系统上实测验证。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;堆叠因子分解机&quot;,&quot;稠密缩放律&quot;,&quot;特征交互&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2024/ctr_feature_interaction/wukong_towards_a_scaling_law_for_large_scale_recommendation/">Wukong: Towards a Scaling Law for Large-Scale Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以堆叠 FM + 线性压缩块构建纯稠密可缩放交互架构，在 6 公开数据集与 146B 内部数据上确立跨两数量级复杂度的推荐缩放律。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Max-Min Fairness&quot;,&quot;Jensen Gap&quot;,&quot;对偶优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/bridging_jensen_gap_for_max_min_group_fairness_optimization_in_recommendati/">Bridging Jensen Gap for Max-Min Group Fairness Optimization in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示MMF约束下小批量采样引发Jensen Gap，提出对偶重加权算法FairDual，在三个数据集六种骨干上同时提升准确率与公平性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;arXiv 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;保形预测&quot;,&quot;提示工程去偏&quot;,&quot;LLM推荐公平性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/facter_fairness_aware_conformal_thresholding_and_prompt_engineering_for_ena/">FACTER: Fairness-Aware Conformal Thresholding and Prompt Engineering for Enabling Fair LLM-Based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用保形分位数阈值+违规触发提示工程，在黑盒 LLM 推荐器上闭环降低人口统计偏见，MovieLens 违规数降 95.5% 且精度基本不掉。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;逆强化学习&quot;,&quot;心理奖励模型&quot;,&quot;分位数回归&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/rl_bandit_rec/mtrec_learning_to_align_with_user_preferences_via_mental_reward_models/">MTRec: Learning to Align with User Preferences via Mental Reward Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用分布化逆强化学习(QR-IQL)从行为轨迹反推用户心理奖励分布，作为辅助对齐信号注入CTR/RL推荐模型，离线AUC/NCIS全面提升，工业短视频A/B观看时长+7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;可复现评估&quot;,&quot;隐私保护数据构建&quot;,&quot;网页推荐&quot;,&quot;LLM检索式推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/orbit_open_recommendation_benchmark_for_reproducible_research_with_hidden_t/">ORBIT: Open Recommendation Benchmark for Reproducible Research with Hidden Tests</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>统一可复现推荐基准，含5个公开数据集标准化划分与ClueWeb-Reco隐私安全网页推荐隐藏测试集，评测12个模型并引入LLM-QueryGen基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;全流程数据集&quot;,&quot;分布偏移&quot;,&quot;级联一致性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/recflow_an_industrial_full_flow_recommendation_dataset/">RecFlow: An Industrial Full Flow Recommendation Dataset</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个覆盖工业六阶段漏斗全流程的推荐数据集，含1.9B阶段样本，揭示训练-服务分布偏移并验证阶段样本作为难负例与级联蒸馏的显著收益。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;端侧训练/联邦召回&quot;,&quot;函数秘密共享 FSS&quot;,&quot;稀疏安全聚合&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/secemb_sparsity_aware_secure_federated_learning_of_on_device_recommender_sy/">SecEmb: Sparsity-Aware Secure Federated Learning of On-Device Recommender System with Large Embedding</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 FSS 点函数对稀疏物品嵌入更新做无损安全聚合，通信降 90x、端侧计算降 70x 且不泄露评分物品索引。</p>
</dd>
