<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;数据增强/训练数据侧&quot;,&quot;偏好树推理&quot;,&quot;多样性推荐&quot;,&quot;合成交互去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/llm_rec/tree_of_preferences_for_diversified_recommendation/">Tree of Preferences for Diversified Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 构建粗到细偏好树挖掘欠探索兴趣并合成交互去偏，在 Twitter/Weibo/Amazon 上同时提升多样性与相关性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;arXiv&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;机制设计&quot;,&quot;拍卖策略防伪&quot;,&quot;重要性采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/truthful_aggregation_of_llms_with_an_application_to_online_advertising/">Truthful Aggregation of LLMs with an Application to Online Advertising</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>MOSAIC 用重要性采样+循环单调性支付实现 LLM 广告拍卖的真实报价与最优分布收敛，20 候选即近最优。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;前门因果识别&quot;,&quot;对抗代理中介&quot;,&quot;多触点归因&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/alm_mta_front_door_causal_multi_touch_attribution_method_for_creator_ecosys/">ALM-MTA: Front-Door Causal Multi-Touch Attribution Method for Creator-Ecosystem Optimization</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以前门识别+对抗代理中介实现大规模推荐系统多触点因果归因，离线 AUC 0.907、线上创作者 +0.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;留存优化&quot;,&quot;双边匹配&quot;,&quot;动态 LTR&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/rl_bandit_rec/beyond_match_maximization_and_fairness_retention_optimized_two_sided_matchi/">Beyond Match Maximization and Fairness: Retention-Optimized Two-Sided Matching</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将双边匹配推荐重构为留存最大化问题，用凹性 Jensen 下界把 NP-hard 排序松弛为 O(NlogN) 的 argsort 打分，在合成与真实约会平台数据上以约 70% 匹配量取得最高留存率。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果表征学习&quot;,&quot;互信息解耦&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/causal_representation_learning_for_generalisable_recommendation/">Causal Representation Learning for Generalisable Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出信息论解耦准则 J=I(g(T);Y|Z)−λI(g(T);Z)，用 InfoNCE+梯度反转实现，零推理开销，Spotify 千万级 A/B 提升 +0.75% 播放。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;反事实公平&quot;,&quot;表演性预测&quot;,&quot;双重鲁棒估计&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/copf_an_online_framework_for_deployment_stable_counterfactual_fairness_in_e/">COPF: An Online Framework for Deployment-Stable Counterfactual Fairness in Evolving Graphs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以曝光反事实定义群体机会差，用倾向日志+GA-DR残差+Residual-OI证书在线审计并控制演化图链路推荐的部署稳定公平性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;激励相容探索&quot;,&quot;上下文 Bandit&quot;,&quot;机制设计&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/rl_bandit_rec/incentivized_exploration_with_stochastic_covariates_a_two_stage_mechanism_d/">Incentivized Exploration with Stochastic Covariates: A Two-Stage Mechanism Design for Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向在线随机用户协变量的激励相容探索，提出两阶段 RCB 算法（冷启动补贴 + 逆比例间隙采样），在满足 ε-DBIC 约束下达到 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{KdT})$ 遗憾。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;持续学习基准&quot;,&quot;组合多样性&quot;,&quot;多任务奖励&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/kuairecv2_benchmarking_large_scale_continual_learning_for_diversified_and_m/">KuaiRecV2: Benchmarking Large-Scale Continual Learning for Diversified and Multi-task Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>快手 30 天 4.84 亿交互动态物品池基准，统一评测持续学习、Token 级多样性与多任务检索。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICLR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;低通谱滤波&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/low_pass_personalized_subgraph_federated_recommendation/">Low-pass Personalized Subgraph Federated Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用低通谱滤波提取跨子图稳定的结构信号做个性化联邦聚合，并以局部流行度偏差感知 margin 抑制长尾偏置，五数据集 NDCG@20 最高提升 24.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;自动出价拍卖&quot;,&quot;模型单调性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/ctr_feature_interaction/model_monotonicity_in_autobidding_auctions_when_do_better_predictions_lead_/">Model Monotonicity in Autobidding Auctions: When Do Better Predictions Lead to Better Outcomes?</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用划分细化刻画模型改进，系统证明自动出价拍卖中仅三种设定保证平台指标单调，其余均可反例击穿。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;ICML&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;模型更新/遗忘&quot;,&quot;Machine Unlearning&quot;,&quot;Low-Rank Adapter&quot;,&quot;Knowledge Distillation&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/obliviate_efficient_unlearning_in_recommender_systems/">Obliviate: Efficient Unlearning in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>两阶段低秩适配器+局部校准的推荐系统机器遗忘框架，20%删除率下兼顾遗忘完整性与效用，比全量重训快约 3 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;语义ID重分配&quot;,&quot;机器遗忘&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/generative_rec/tracer_token_reassignment_for_concept_erasure_in_generative_recommendation/">TRACER: Token ReAssignment for Concept ERasure in Generative Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个面向生成式推荐的概念遗忘框架，用可微 token 重分配替代直接抑制共享 SID，在 Amazon 三域上遗忘 HR@5 较 PISCES 再降 20%+ 且保留 97% 效用。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;两级 Softmax 采样&quot;,&quot;负采样偏差校正&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/collaborative_filtering/two_level_softmax_sampling_done_right_correcting_bias_from_size_imbalance_a/">Two-Level Softmax Sampling Done Right: Correcting Bias from Size Imbalance and Dispersion</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示两级 softmax 采样因簇大小失衡与簇内相似度离散导致的系统性偏差，提出 S-2LS/SD-2LS 校正，KL 保真度提升 1–4 个数量级且开销近乎为零。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;NeurIPS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信隐私&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;稀疏自编码器&quot;,&quot;叠加假设&quot;,&quot;可解释性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/ICLR_NeurIPS_ICML/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/user_embeddings_are_superpositions_of_interpretable_behavioral_modes/">User Embeddings are Superpositions of Interpretable Behavioral Modes</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：Top ML Conferences (RecSys Papers)">ICLR / NeurIPS / ICML (RecSys)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将机制可解释性的叠加假设迁移到用户嵌入，用 SAE 事后分解出可命名行为模式，支撑零样本自然语言受众检索与画像。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2022&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排（广告位拍卖/机制层）&quot;,&quot;GSP拍卖&quot;,&quot;组级公平分配&quot;,&quot;EF1/EFX&quot;,&quot;无遗憾学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2022/ctr_feature_interaction/fair_equilibria_in_sponsored_search_auctions_the_advertisers_perspective/">Fair Equilibria in Sponsored Search Auctions: The Advertisers&#x27; Perspective</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在 GSP 后接组级 EF1/EFX 公平分配并补偿受损广告主，证明无遗憾学习收敛到高社会福利均衡；真实日志上公平损失≤10%，补偿占收入20–40%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;联邦训练&quot;,&quot;模型投毒&quot;,&quot;梯度攻击&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/poisoning_deep_learning_based_recommender_model_in_federated_learning_scena/">Poisoning Deep Learning Based Recommender Model in Federated Learning Scenarios</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>无先验知识下用高斯近似良性用户嵌入与硬用户挖掘生成毒化梯度，0.1% 恶意用户即把冷门目标物品 ER@10 推至 1.0。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;逆倾向加权 IPS&quot;,&quot;对抗域适应去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/towards_resolving_propensity_contradiction_in_offline_recommender_learning/">Towards Resolving Propensity Contradiction in Offline Recommender Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出倾向无关泛化误差上界与对抗式矩阵分解 DAMF，无需 MCAR 数据与倾向估计，Yahoo! R3 上 MSE 相对降 26.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/端侧推理&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;双重个性化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/dual_personalization_on_federated_recommendation/">Dual Personalization on Federated Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>PFedRec 以端侧轻量模型、局部评分函数与物品嵌入后微调实现双重个性化，在 ML-100K/1M 上 HR@10 较 Fed 最优提升约 8–10%，并给出 LDP 隐私-效用分析。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;增量学习&quot;,&quot;特征陈旧性&quot;,&quot;经验回放&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2023/ctr_feature_interaction/feature_staleness_aware_incremental_learning_for_ctr_prediction/">Feature Staleness Aware Incremental Learning for CTR Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示增量训练中特征嵌入陈旧问题，用加权最大覆盖采样+陈旧度正则实现自适应历史回放，四数据集AUC平均提升1.21%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/端到端联邦个性化&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;低秩适配器(LoRA 式)&quot;,&quot;自适应门控融合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/federated_adaptation_for_foundation_model_based_recommendations/">Federated Adaptation for Foundation Model-based Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 FedPA：以预训练双塔推荐模型为底座，客户端仅训练低秩适配器（用户级私有 + 用户组级共享）并由自适应门控融合公共知识与个性化，联邦优化中冻结物品/预测模块，AUC 最高相对提升 3.4%，可训练参数减少 40%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;黑盒隐私评测&quot;,&quot;成员推断攻击&quot;,&quot;无影子攻击&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/shadow_free_membership_inference_attacks_recommender_systems_are_more_vulne/">Shadow-Free Membership Inference Attacks: Recommender Systems Are More Vulnerable Than You Thought</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出无影子成员推断攻击：空画像热门锚点+自适应用户距离比较，黑盒免训练，3.7秒取得高TPR低FPR。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;Test-Time Training&quot;,&quot;端云协同&quot;,&quot;参数校正&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2025/sequential_rec/device_cloud_collaborative_correction_for_on_device_recommendation/">Device-Cloud Collaborative Correction for On-Device Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>端侧 TTT 自校正网络 + 云端全局校正网络，以 RNN 级开销逼近 Transformer 精度，UAUC 最高提升 4.56%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图后门攻击&quot;,&quot;单节点触发器&quot;,&quot;LightGCN 鲁棒性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/single_node_trigger_backdoor_attacks_in_graph_based_recommendation_systems/">Single-Node Trigger Backdoor Attacks in Graph-Based Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出单节点触发器后门攻击 SNT-BA，仅注入 1 个虚假用户即可让目标物品在 99% 目标用户 Top-10 中曝光，同时 HR@20 仅降约 5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;IJCAI-ECAI 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;基础模型个性化&quot;,&quot;语义隐私边界&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/IJCAI/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/a_survey_of_personalized_federated_foundation_models_for_privacy_preserving/">A Survey of Personalized Federated Foundation Models for Privacy-Preserving Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence">IJCAI (RecSys Track)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>综述提出“主动语义保人格”范式，用几何正交约束与语义感知聚合解耦全局知识与用户人格，并给出五类开放问题与未来路线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;多任务学习与列表级重排&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;情感计算&quot;,&quot;双塔多标签&quot;,&quot;弱监督标注&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/multi_task_reranking/affective_signals_in_a_social_media_recommender_system/">Affective Signals in a Social Media Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建14类情感taxonomy与CARE弱标注管线，用双塔多标签模型产出32维情感embedding注入Facebook排序打分模块，离线归一化AUC损失降&gt;8%，20M用户在线A/B违规内容曝光降&gt;0.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2022&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排（出价预估）&quot;,&quot;删失数据建模&quot;,&quot;邻域似然损失&quot;,&quot;特征交叉&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/ctr_feature_interaction/arbitrary_distribution_modeling_with_censorship_in_real_time_bidding_advert/">Arbitrary Distribution Modeling with Censorship in Real-Time Bidding Advertising</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出邻域似然损失 NLL + ADM 框架，在无分布假设下建模 RTB 中标价删失分布，MAE 较 DLF 降 29%，推理仅 3ms。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2022&quot;,&quot;工业架构&quot;,&quot;训练系统/分布式训练&quot;,&quot;嵌入表分片&quot;,&quot;强化学习&quot;,&quot;成本建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/autoshard_automated_embedding_table_sharding_for_recommender_systems/">AutoShard: Automated Embedding Table Sharding for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用神经成本模型与深度强化学习自动分片嵌入表，在 MetaSyn/MetaProd 上平衡跨 GPU 负载并实现最高约 1.7× 加速。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;冷启动用户建模&quot;,&quot;元学习&quot;,&quot;对抗公平&quot;,&quot;多任务对抗&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/comprehensive_fair_meta_learned_recommender_system/">Comprehensive Fair Meta-learned Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次将个体/反事实/组公平统一为多任务对抗并嵌入元学习冷启动，在三个真实数据集上显著降AUC/CF/GF且总体不损MAE。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;隐私攻击评测（黑盒 top-N 输出）&quot;,&quot;成员推断攻击&quot;,&quot;解耦表征（Gaussian/vMF）&quot;,&quot;样本重加权与交替训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/debiasing_learning_for_membership_inference_attacks_against_recommender_sys/">Debiasing Learning for Membership Inference Attacks Against Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Gaussian+vMF 双先验 VAE 解耦差异向量、以 truth-level score 交替重加权，同时消除影子/目标推荐器落差与隐状态估计偏差，黑盒 AUC 由 &lt;0.7 升至 0.98+。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;排序 (Next-Item 全库打分)&quot;,&quot;曝光偏差&quot;,&quot;逆倾向打分 (IPS/ITPS)&quot;,&quot;BERT4Rec / Cloze Task&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/debiasing_the_cloze_task_in_sequential_recommendation_with_bidirectional_tr/">Debiasing the Cloze Task in Sequential Recommendation with Bidirectional Transformers</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>证明 IPS 无法去偏序列推荐的 Cloze 任务，提出引入 Choice 变量与时变曝光倾向的 ITPS 无偏估计器，在半合成与真实数据上稳定优于 BERT4Rec。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;时长混杂偏差&quot;,&quot;后门调整&quot;,&quot;观看时长分位数回归&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/deconfounding_duration_bias_in_watch_time_prediction_for_video_recommendati/">Deconfounding Duration Bias in Watch-time Prediction for Video Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用因果图指出视频时长是同时影响曝光与观看时长的混杂因子，提出分时长组的分位数回归 D2Q/Res-D2Q，在快手 13 亿样本与线上 A/B 中显著提升观看时长。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;端云协同调度（会话内机制选择）&quot;,&quot;Uplift 建模&quot;,&quot;反事实样本构造&quot;,&quot;标签平滑预算约束&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/device_cloud_collaborative_recommendation_via_meta_controller/">Device-Cloud Collaborative Recommendation via Meta Controller</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用因果 uplift 构造反事实样本，训练端侧 Meta Controller 在云会话推荐/端侧重排/云端刷新三种机制间动态调度，兼顾兴趣突变与算力预算。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;端到端训练（联邦训练阶段攻击）&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐安全&quot;,&quot;硬负采样投毒&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/fedattack_effective_and_covert_poisoning_attack_on_federated_recommendation/">FedAttack: Effective and Covert Poisoning Attack on Federated Recommendation via Hard Sampling</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用全局最难正负样本污染联邦推荐的本地训练数据，1% 恶意客户端即可使 HR@5 降 8%，并绕过鲁棒聚合与恶意梯度检测。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;知识图谱增强攻击&quot;,&quot;分层强化学习策略&quot;,&quot;黑盒投毒/逃逸攻击&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/knowledge_enhanced_black_box_attacks_for_recommendations/">Knowledge-enhanced Black-box Attacks for Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用物品知识图谱约束动作空间与状态表征，配合分层策略网络与 PPO 在纯黑盒下生成高质量虚假用户画像，ML-1M 上 HR@20 相对 PoisonRec 提升 10.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;近端因果推断&quot;,&quot;潜在混淆因子识别&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/debiasing_recommendation_by_learning_identifiable_latent_confounders/">Debiasing Recommendation by Learning Identifiable Latent Confounders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用代理变量做近端因果推断，学习可识别的潜在混淆因子，为反事实反馈提供可识别性保证；三数据集全库排序 NDCG@5 最高提升 3.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;冷启动与长尾推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;长尾偏差解耦&quot;,&quot;混合专家MoE&quot;,&quot;双侧分支网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/cold_start_rec/empowering_long_tail_item_recommendation_through_cross_decoupling_network_c/">Empowering Long-tail Item Recommendation through Cross Decoupling Network (CDN)</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用物品侧记忆/泛化 MoE 与用户侧双分支采样解耦长尾双重偏差，γ-adapter 软化尾部迁移，HR@50 整体+8.4%、尾部+11.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;离线评测与基准&quot;,&quot;切片指标 (Slice-based Metrics)&quot;,&quot;行为测试 (Behavioral Tests)&quot;,&quot;双向公平性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/evalrs_2023_well_rounded_recommender_systems_for_real_world_deployments/">EvalRS 2023. Well-Rounded Recommender Systems For Real-World Deployments</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出以切片指标、行为测试与双向公平性为核心的多维推荐评测范式，并发布 WASABI 增强音乐数据集与开源评测工具。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2023&quot;,&quot;多任务学习与列表级重排&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;训练稳定性&quot;,&quot;自适应梯度裁剪&quot;,&quot;Adagrad 优化器&quot;,&quot;多任务共享底层&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/multi_task_reranking/improving_training_stability_for_multitask_ranking_models_in_recommender_sy/">Improving Training Stability for Multitask Ranking Models in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>针对多任务精排 loss 发散，提出基于 L∞ 更新范数与双阈值自适应裁剪的 Clippy，Large+DCN 上 AUC 72.37 且可吃 2× 学习率</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2023&quot;,&quot;多任务学习与列表级重排&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;在线原始-对偶&quot;,&quot;曝光约束&quot;,&quot;跨通道建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/multi_task_reranking/multi_channel_integrated_recommendation_with_exposure_constraints/">Multi-channel Integrated Recommendation with Exposure Constraints</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多通道信息流推荐建模为带曝光上下界的在线线性规划，用对偶曝光分配与 PCR/CAR 上下文重排联合求解，淘宝 A/B 点击提升 3.00%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;逆倾向得分 (IPS)&quot;,&quot;离线策略评估 (OPE)&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/off_policy_evaluation_of_ranking_policies_under_diverse_user_behavior/">Off-Policy Evaluation of Ranking Policies under Diverse User Behavior</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 AIPS：以动作-奖励交互矩阵刻画上下文相关的异构用户行为，实现任意行为分布下无偏且方差最优的排序策略离线评估，并用树模型直接优化行为模型以最小化 MSE。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;路径特定反事实公平&quot;,&quot;潜中介分析&quot;,&quot;弱监督变分推断&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/path_specific_counterfactual_fairness_for_recommender_systems/">Path-Specific Counterfactual Fairness for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用双潜中介分解敏感特征的公平/不公平因果路径，以最小改动原则构造零路径特定偏差的公平世界，PSF-VAE 弱监督变分推断在保持多样性的同时显著降低不公平命中率。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/评分预测&quot;,&quot;Shilling Attack&quot;,&quot;Fake Review Generation&quot;,&quot;Reinforcement Learning&quot;,&quot;Adversarial Training&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/shilling_black_box_review_based_recommender_systems_through_fake_review_gen/">Shilling Black-box Review-based Recommender Systems through Fake Review Generation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个生成式假评论攻击 RBRS，RL+三类奖励与方面生成，黑盒显著提升预测漂移并验证对抗训练防御。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;序数回归树分解&quot;,&quot;不确定性方差约束&quot;,&quot;后门调整去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/ctr_feature_interaction/tree_based_progressive_regression_model_for_watch_time_prediction_in_short_/">Tree based Progressive Regression Model for Watch-Time Prediction in Short-video Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将观看时长序数分位离散化并用条件依赖二叉树逐层二分类，输出多项式分布求期望与方差，后门调整无缝嵌入，Kuaishou 线上时长 +0.25%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;联邦学习&quot;,&quot;投毒攻击&quot;,&quot;新闻推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/ua_fedrec_untargeted_attack_on_federated_news_recommendation/">UA-FedRec: Untargeted Attack on Federated News Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出联邦新闻推荐无目标投毒攻击，联合新闻相似性、用户模型与样本量三重扰动，1% 恶意客户端即可显著降效并绕过防御。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;延迟反馈&quot;,&quot;标签纠正&quot;,&quot;反事实标注&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2023/ctr_feature_interaction/unbiased_delayed_feedback_label_correction_for_conversion_rate_prediction/">Unbiased Delayed Feedback Label Correction for Conversion Rate Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用辅助 LC 模型对未转化样本做无偏标签纠正，配合反事实标注与嵌入交替训练，Criteo 上填补 Vanilla→Oracle 差距的 77.6% AUC。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/多任务&quot;,&quot;表征学习&quot;,&quot;维度坍缩&quot;,&quot;兴趣解缠&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/ads_recommendation_in_a_collapsed_and_entangled_world/">Ads Recommendation in a Collapsed and Entangled World</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>腾讯广告系统从特征编码、维度坍缩与任务兴趣纠缠三方面重构表征学习，并以在线 A/B 验证多嵌入、解缠与训练策略的 GMV 收益。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;对抗攻击&quot;,&quot;LLM Agent&quot;,&quot;Prompt Tuning&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/cheatagent_attacking_llm_empowered_recommender_systems_via_llm_agent/">CheatAgent: Attacking LLM-Empowered Recommender Systems via LLM Agent</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用掩码定位插入位 + LLM 智能体生成对抗扰动并以前缀提示自反思优化，在黑盒下显著击穿 P5/TALLRec 推荐系统。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;精排/召回模型压缩&quot;,&quot;持续学习&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/collaborative_filtering/continual_collaborative_distillation_for_recommender_system/">Continual Collaborative Distillation for Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 CCD，在非平稳数据流中协同更新教师与学生：双代理回放防遗忘、新实体初始化与教师吸收学生侧知识；Yelp 学生 H-mean 较最佳基线提升 10.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;时长偏差 (Duration Bias)&quot;,&quot;反事实观看时长 (CWT)&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/counteracting_duration_bias_in_video_recommendation_via_counterfactual_watc/">Counteracting Duration Bias in Video Recommendation via Counterfactual Watch Time</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>KDD 2024｜去偏与工业推荐：以“反事实观看时长(CWT)截断”重构时长偏差，用经济学边际收益推导变换函数与截断似然，KuaiRand/WeChat/Product 上 MAE 降约 25%、AUC 逼近 Oracle，线上 MWT +2.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果去偏&quot;,&quot;双稳健估计&quot;,&quot;噪声反馈&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/debiased_recommendation_with_noisy_feedback/">Debiased Recommendation with Noisy Feedback</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次联合建模 MNAR 与结果测量误差 (OME)，提出 OME-EIB/IPS/DR 估计量、弱可分误差参数辨识与交替去噪训练，在 Coat/Music/KuaiRec 上 AUC 相对提升约 1.5–2.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排后校准/线上推理&quot;,&quot;Post-hoc Calibration&quot;,&quot;Basis Function Ensemble&quot;,&quot;Multi-Field Calibration&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/ctr_feature_interaction/deep_ensemble_shape_calibration_multi_field_post_hoc_calibration_in_online_/">Deep Ensemble Shape Calibration: Multi-Field Post-hoc Calibration in Online Advertising</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多字段后置校准解耦为基函数形状校准与全字段数值校准，免分箱提升稀疏字段数据利用率，工业线上 CVR +2.5%、GMV +4.0%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;,&quot;端侧重排推理&quot;,&quot;端云协同个性化&quot;,&quot;子网掩码剪枝&quot;,&quot;超网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/sequential_rec/diet_customized_slimming_for_incompatible_networks_in_sequential_recommenda/">DIET: Customized Slimming for Incompatible Networks in Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>云端按实时序列用 GRU+超网络生成二进制子网 mask 并做 filter 级校正下发边缘，传输参数降至 3.1%、FLOPs 减半，NDCG 最高 +22.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与POI推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;扩散模型&quot;,&quot;云边端协同&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/diffusion_based_cloud_edge_device_collaborative_learning_for_next_poi_recom/">Diffusion-Based Cloud-Edge-Device Collaborative Learning for Next POI Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>KDD 2024 [时空轨迹与POI推荐] 提出云-边-端三层扩散协同框架DCPR，云端学类别运动模式、边端注入区域POI嵌入与时空偏置、端侧patch微调，配合DDIM式采样加速，在Foursquare/Weeplace采样排序上取得SOTA并兼顾端侧效率。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;特征选择&quot;,&quot;AUKC&quot;,&quot;基准评测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/ctr_feature_interaction/erase_benchmarking_feature_selection_methods_for_deep_recommender_systems/">ERASE: Benchmarking Feature Selection Methods for Deep Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>统一评测 11 种特征选择方法×4 个 DRS backbone，提出 AUKC 稳定性指标，工业在线裁剪 30% 特征降 20% 延迟且效果不降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;用户关系图&quot;,&quot;个性化物品嵌入&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/gpfedrec_graph_guided_personalization_for_federated_recommendation/">GPFedRec: Graph-guided Personalization for Federated Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>服务端用客户端物品嵌入构建用户关系图引导联邦聚合，Leave-One-Out 全库排序下 HR@10 最高相对提升 3.38%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/排序策略层&quot;,&quot;用户偏好动态&quot;,&quot;危害缓解&quot;,&quot;隐函数定理梯度&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/harm_mitigation_in_recommender_systems_under_user_preference_dynamics/">Harm Mitigation in Recommender Systems under User Preference Dynamics</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 MNL 选择+吸引力偏好动态建模“推荐→兴趣漂移→站外有害消费”路径，在平稳点用隐函数定理求梯度优化 CTR-危害权衡，CTR 与危害同时优于基线，最高 77%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;冷启动与长尾推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;MAML 元学习&quot;,&quot;Meta Embedding&quot;,&quot;工业级个性化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/cold_start_rec/limaml_personalization_of_deep_recommender_models_via_meta_learning/">LiMAML: Personalization of Deep Recommender Models via Meta Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将推荐网络解耦为 Meta/Global 双块，仅对 Meta Block 做 MAML 元学习并离线蒸馏成定长 meta embedding，实现百亿参数模型的少样本个性化在线服务，线上 CTR 提升 2.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排后广告拍卖/计费&quot;,&quot;OCPC&quot;,&quot;转化报告操纵&quot;,&quot;拍卖博弈&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/ctr_feature_interaction/optimized_cost_per_click_in_online_advertising_a_theoretical_analysis/">Optimized Cost Per Click in Online Advertising: A Theoretical Analysis</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用两广告主扩展式博弈比较 OCPC/CPC/CPA，揭示站外转化操纵与外部选项下的定价优势。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;互惠推荐&quot;,&quot;因果推断&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/revisiting_reciprocal_recommender_systems_metrics_formulation_and_method/">Revisiting Reciprocal Recommender Systems: Metrics, Formulation, and Method</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出覆盖/稳定性/双边均衡五项互惠推荐新指标，并以双边因果 treatment + 空位重排求解；全库排序下 CRecall 最高提升 11.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;低秩分解&quot;,&quot;模型压缩&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/ctr_feature_interaction/unified_low_rank_compression_framework_for_click_through_rate_prediction/">Unified Low-rank Compression Framework for Click-through Rate Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出AFM统一低秩压缩框架，对MLP输出特征与Embedding表做PCA低秩近似；在Criteo/Avazu/XYZ上以3–5×压缩实现更高AUC与1.35–2.7×吞吐。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;训练阶段数据投毒&quot;,&quot;对比学习推荐&quot;,&quot;谱分析&quot;,&quot;双层优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/unveiling_vulnerabilities_of_contrastive_recommender_systems_to_poisoning_a/">Unveiling Vulnerabilities of Contrastive Recommender Systems to Poisoning Attacks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>从谱分析揭示 InfoNCE 的全局离散效应正是 CL 推荐易受定向投毒的根源，提出双目标 CLeaR 攻击，Hit-Ratio@50 平均提升 1.5–3 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回（冷启动内容匹配）/供给生态&quot;,&quot;创作者竞争博弈&quot;,&quot;用户福利优化&quot;,&quot;乘法权重更新&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/user_welfare_optimization_in_recommender_systems_with_competing_content_cre/">User Welfare Optimization in Recommender Systems with Competing Content Creators</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用用户满意度动态重加权 UIR/SMT/HMT 引导创作者竞争，三周线上 A/B 使 LTR +1.13%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回 (Next-POI 全库打分)&quot;,&quot;Membership Inference Attack (LiRA)&quot;,&quot;Training Data Extraction&quot;,&quot;DP-SGD / Selective DP&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/where_have_you_been_a_study_of_privacy_risk_for_point_of_interest_recommend/">Where Have You Been? A Study of Privacy Risk for Point-of-Interest Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个面向 POI 推荐模型的位置/轨迹级隐私攻击套件（抽取+成员推断），揭示全库排序模型可被黑盒查询大幅记忆训练签到，并证明现有 DP 防御效用崩塌。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/策略决策&quot;,&quot;Contextual Bandit&quot;,&quot;子模优化&quot;,&quot;联名营销推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/rl_bandit_rec/a_unified_online_offline_framework_for_co_branding_campaign_recommendations/">A Unified Online-Offline Framework for Co-Branding Campaign Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个联名营销推荐系统性研究：二分图建模+方差感知CUCB在线学习+部分枚举离线预算分配，理论保证近优regret与1-1/e近似，真实数据收益提升12%-73%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;选择偏差&quot;,&quot;潜在外生变量&quot;,&quot;蒙特卡洛似然&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/addressing_correlated_latent_exogenous_variables_in_debiased_recommender_sy/">Addressing Correlated Latent Exogenous Variables in Debiased Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>放松外生变量独立假设，用双变量正态潜变量建模选择与偏好相关性，蒙特卡洛似然估计实现去偏，Coat/Yahoo!R3/KuaiRec 全指标领先。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;捷径偏置&quot;,&quot;用户聚类&quot;,&quot;多塔网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/breaker_removing_shortcut_cues_with_user_clustering_for_single_slot_recomme/">Breaker: Removing Shortcut Cues with User Clustering for Single-slot Recommendation System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用用户表征聚类辅助任务+多塔簇专属偏好建模，破除单槽推荐中用户固有倾向的捷径偏置，离线Recall@1提升7%并上线美团支付。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;扩散推荐&quot;,&quot;多样性控制&quot;,&quot;类别偏好解耦&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/generative_rec/controlling_diversity_at_inference_guiding_diffusion_recommender_models_wit/">Controlling Diversity at Inference: Guiding Diffusion Recommender Models with Targeted Category Preferences</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出解耦扩散模型 D3Rec，通过类别偏好引导的反向去噪过程，在推理阶段灵活控制推荐准确率-多样性权衡。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;数据版权保护&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;,&quot;数据水印&quot;,&quot;感受野&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/data_watermarking_for_sequential_recommender_systems/">Data Watermarking for Sequential Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次定义序列推荐数据集/单用户双场景数据水印，用感受野约束插入连续物品序列，5 模型 3 数据集上水印 NDCG 近 1.0 且效用无损。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;数据生成/基准构建&quot;,&quot;概率生成模型&quot;,&quot;长尾分布混合&quot;,&quot;可控合成数据&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/flexible_generation_of_preference_data_for_recommendation_analysis/">Flexible Generation of Preference Data for Recommendation Analysis</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>HYDRA 以用户-物品匹配、物品流行度、用户活跃度三因子混合分布生成可控合成偏好数据，复现真实基准的度分布与算法排序。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;端侧推荐&quot;,&quot;动态子网络&quot;,&quot;超网络参数生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/forward_once_for_all_structural_parameterized_adaptation_for_efficient_clou/">Forward Once for All: Structural Parameterized Adaptation for Efficient Cloud-coordinated On-device Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用结构控制器+结构引导映射器为每台设备动态组装子网络，一次前向即完成端侧适配，兼顾隐私、带宽与算力。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;分布式图学习&quot;,&quot;子图采样表格化&quot;,&quot;工业级 GNN 系统&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/gigl_large_scale_graph_neural_networks_at_snapchat/">GiGL: Large-Scale Graph Neural Networks at Snapchat</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>Snapchat 开源十亿级图学习库 GiGL，以表格化子图采样+PyG 解耦实现 900M 节点/16.8B 边图 12 小时内端到端训练推理，支撑 35+ 线上发布。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;联邦训练通信&quot;,&quot;梯度聚类压缩&quot;,&quot;自适应动作共享&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/gradients_as_an_action_towards_communication_efficient_federated_recommende/">Gradients as an Action: Towards Communication-Efficient Federated Recommender Systems via Adaptive Action Sharing</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将物品梯度聚类为少量“动作”共享传输，配合自适应聚类与带宽感知压缩，在 ML-100K/1M/Lastfm-2K 上以最高 96.88% 压缩率不损性能。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;交错实验&quot;,&quot;反事实评估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/harnessing_the_power_of_interleaving_and_counterfactual_evaluation_for_airb/">Harnessing the Power of Interleaving and Counterfactual Evaluation for Airbnb Search Ranking</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出竞争对式 Team Draft 交错实验与在线反事实评估，用双层随机化与位置差增益估计器将 Airbnb 搜索排序实验灵敏度提升 50–100 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;C2C 推荐数据集&quot;,&quot;三塔 Transformer 多步召回&quot;,&quot;无 SKU 商品表征&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/merrec_a_large_scale_multipurpose_mercari_dataset_for_consumer_to_consumer_/">MerRec: A Large-scale Multipurpose Mercari Dataset for Consumer-to-Consumer Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个大规模 C2C 推荐数据集 MerRec（5.56M 用户/83M 商品/1.27B 交互），并配套三塔 Mercatran 模型与四任务基准。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;权重衰减&quot;,&quot;流行度偏置&quot;,&quot;嵌入模长初始化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/collaborative_filtering/on_the_role_of_weight_decay_in_collaborative_filtering_a_popularity_perspec/">On the Role of Weight Decay in Collaborative Filtering: A Popularity Perspective</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示权重衰减本质是将流行度编码进嵌入模长，提出 PRISM 初始化策略替代权重衰减，NDCG 最高提升 4.77% 且训练加速 38.48%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;Offline RL&quot;,&quot;Robust MDP&quot;,&quot;Ad Load Optimization&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/rl_bandit_rec/session_level_dynamic_ad_load_optimization_using_offline_robust_reinforceme/">Session-Level Dynamic Ad Load Optimization using Offline Robust Reinforcement Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将会话级广告加载建模为鲁棒MDP，用IPM不确定集导出免额外开销的鲁棒对决DQN，离线AUCC提升80%+，线上A/B双位数增益。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;数据剪枝/重训&quot;,&quot;Shapley Value&quot;,&quot;FastSHAP&quot;,&quot;交互价值评估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/self_supervised_denoising/shapley_value_driven_data_pruning_for_recommender_systems/">Shapley Value-driven Data Pruning for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 FastSHAP 实时估计交互对训练损失的边际贡献，按 Shapley 值剪枝低效用交互并重训，四数据集 Recall/NDCG 平均提升 3.72%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;因果干预&quot;,&quot;时序演化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/taming_recommendation_bias_with_causal_intervention_on_evolving_personal_po/">Taming Recommendation Bias with Causal Intervention on Evolving Personal Popularity</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出演化个人流行度度量与因果去混杂训练，并在推理期用移动平均趋势外推做干预，兼顾用户-物品演化一致性，Full-Ranking 下 Recall@20 最高提升 20.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;Bradley-Terry&quot;,&quot;算法排名一致性&quot;,&quot;数据集分类学&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/bradley_terry_rankings_for_recommender_systems_across_dataset_taxonomies/">Bradley-Terry Rankings for Recommender Systems Across Dataset Taxonomies</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Bradley-Terry 概率排序替代指标均值聚合，在 14 算法×89 数据集上给出数据集感知的推荐算法排名与免运行预测。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Uplift Modeling&quot;,&quot;Cannibalization&quot;,&quot;SUTVA&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/canniuplift_a_holistic_framework_for_mitigating_seller_and_incentive_cannib/">CanniUplift: A Holistic Framework for Mitigating Seller and Incentive Cannibalization in E-commerce Uplift Modeling</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用平台级GMV一致性约束与核销路径分解，缓解多卖家电商uplift建模中的卖家级与激励级蚕食，线上ΔGMV相对提升4.08%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;因果提升建模 (Uplift Modeling)&quot;,&quot;离线回放 (Offline Replay)&quot;,&quot;双稳健估计 (Doubly Robust)&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/deep_learning_causal_retrieval_optimization_for_efficient_e_commerce_distri/">Deep-learning Causal Retrieval Optimization for Efficient e-commerce Distribution in Pinterest</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将购物候选生成器触发建模为因果决策，用 DR 多任务提升学习+随机 Holdout+线性回放，线上触发率降 85% 而购物会话持平。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;有效秩&quot;,&quot;表征塌缩&quot;,&quot;Token Mixing&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/ctr_feature_interaction/expand_more_shrink_less_shaping_effective_rank_dynamics_for_dense_scaling_i/">Expand More, Shrink Less: Shaping Effective-Rank Dynamics for Dense Scaling in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>从有效秩视角诊断 RankMixer 的阻尼振荡塌缩，提出参数化全混合与 GLU-PFFN 实现“多扩张少收缩”，Criteo/Avazu 上 AUC 提升超 0.001。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Field-Aware Transformer&quot;,&quot;Basis-Composed Hypernetwork&quot;,&quot;Scaling Law&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/ctr_feature_interaction/from_scaling_to_structured_expressivity_rethinking_transformers_for_ctr_pre/">From Scaling to Structured Expressivity: Rethinking Transformers for CTR Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用域感知参数重构 Transformer 块，将复杂度从词表规模 n 转向域数 F，配合基组合超网络实现可预测幂律缩放，离线 AUC 最高 +4.38%，线上 CTR +2.33%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;访客旅程建模&quot;,&quot;工业搜索排序&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/sequential_rec/journeyformer_encoding_airbnb_guest_journey_with_sequence_modeling/">JourneyFormer: Encoding Airbnb Guest Journey with Sequence Modeling</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>Airbnb 搜索排序的工业级序列建模方案，以频次分层长短期序列+统一ID嵌入+因果Transformer编码访客旅程，离线NDCG+1.48%、线上Bookers+0.55%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;离线评测&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-a-Judge&quot;,&quot;语义代理&quot;,&quot;MNAR 去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/llm_as_a_judge_for_reliable_and_explainable_offline_evaluation_in_top_k_rec/">LLM-as-a-Judge for Reliable and Explainable Offline Evaluation in Top-K Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 从用户文本行为构建语义偏好代理替代 holdout ID 匹配，以 reasoning-then-scoring 生成可解释 Top-K 评测，模型排序一致性较常规测试提升 40–150%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;创作者竞争博弈&quot;,&quot;偏差-方差权衡&quot;,&quot;正则化策略&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/lower_bias_higher_welfare_how_creator_competition_reshapes_bias_variance_tr/">Lower Bias, Higher Welfare: How Creator Competition Reshapes Bias-Variance Tradeoff in Recommendation Platforms?</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>博弈论建模创作者竞争如何重塑平台正则化选择，理论与实验一致表明策略环境下应弱正则（低偏差）以最大化用户福利。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;策略个性化&quot;,&quot;Uplift建模&quot;,&quot;因果推断&quot;,&quot;双边市场&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/multi_task_reranking/personalizing_marketplace_policies_with_competing_objectives_and_constraine/">Personalizing Marketplace Policies with Competing Objectives and Constrained Experiments: Evidence from a Job Marketplace</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>双边市场免费额度阈值个性化：集成CATE+混合排序+线性外推，护栏风险降10%+，线上验证外推精度r=0.85</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;召回/精排/重排全链路&quot;,&quot;内容冷启动&quot;,&quot;探索与去偏&quot;,&quot;工业级度量框架&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/cold_start_rec/pinequalizer_full_funnel_content_exploration_and_debiasing_system_at_pinter/">PinEqualizer: Full Funnel Content Exploration and Debiasing System at Pinterest</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向工业级搜索/推荐全漏斗的新内容冷启动系统，以去偏替代纯探索，配合三层可扩展度量框架，线上新鲜内容曝光提升350%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;嵌入几何校正&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/rethinking_popularity_bias_in_collaborative_filtering_via_analytical_vector/">Rethinking Popularity Bias in Collaborative Filtering via Analytical Vector Decomposition</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 BPR 优化在嵌入空间刻出“流行度方向”的几何偏差，提出非对称方向分解校正 DDC，在 5 个骨干上 MRR@10 提升 6%–29% 且 AvgPop@10 降 21%–47%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;个性化安全对齐&quot;,&quot;GDPO 多奖励解耦&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/agent_conversational_rec/safecrs_personalized_safety_alignment_for_llm_based_conversational_recommen/">SafeCRS: Personalized Safety Alignment for LLM-Based Conversational Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 SafeRec 个性化安全基准与 Safe-SFT+Safe-GDPO 两阶段框架，在 SafeMovie 上将安全违规率相对降低 96.5% 且保持竞争力推荐质量。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;谱坍缩&quot;,&quot;流行度偏差&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/the_pitfall_of_scaling_up_uncovering_and_mitigating_popularity_bias_amplifi/">The Pitfall of Scaling Up: Uncovering and Mitigating Popularity Bias Amplification in Scaling Transformer-based Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 Transformer 序列推荐随深度加深发生谱坍缩并放大流行度偏差，提出逐层注意力列和与 FFN 谱范数正则 SPRINT，在 6 数据集上同时提升准确率与长尾公平性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;约束解码&quot;,&quot;稀疏矩阵&quot;,&quot;语义ID&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/generative_rec/vectorizing_the_trie_efficient_constrained_decoding_for_llm_based_generativ/">Vectorizing the Trie: Efficient Constrained Decoding for LLM-based Generative Retrieval on Accelerators</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将前缀树约束展平为静态 CSR 稀疏转移矩阵，把不规则树遍历转为全向量化稀疏算子，实现 O(1) I/O 的严格约束解码，线上延迟仅 0.033ms/步。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;KDD&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;基准审计&quot;,&quot;推理期干预&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/KDD/2026/llm_rec/whose_name_comes_up_ii_benchmarking_and_intervention_based_auditing_of_llm_/">Whose Name Comes Up? II: Benchmarking and Intervention-Based Auditing of LLM-Based Scholar Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining">KDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 LLMScholarBench，联合审计 22 个 LLM 在 5 类学者推荐任务、3 类推理期干预下的技术质量与社会代表性权衡。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;在线推理服务(端到端)&quot;,&quot;GPU 嵌入缓存&quot;,&quot;层次化参数服务器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/a_gpu_specialized_inference_parameter_server_for_large_scale_deep_recommend/">A GPU-specialized Inference Parameter Server for Large-Scale Deep Recommendation Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>HugeCTR HPS 用 GPU 嵌入缓存 + CPU/SSD 三级层次存储，把 TB 级嵌入表推理下沉到 GPU，端到端延迟相对 CPU 基线加速 5–62×。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;可复现性研究&quot;,&quot;BERT4Rec&quot;,&quot;掩码自监督训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2022/sequential_rec/a_systematic_review_and_replicability_study_of_bert4rec_for_sequential_reco/">A Systematic Review and Replicability Study of BERT4Rec for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
