<p>系统复盘 134 次 BERT4Rec vs SASRec 对比，指出原始实现默认配置严重欠拟合（需 30× 训练时长），并给出 Hugging Face 高效复现与 ALBERT4Rec 改进。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;增量学习&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;Warm-Start&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2022/ctr_feature_interaction/an_incremental_learning_framework_for_large_scale_ctr_prediction/">An Incremental Learning framework for Large-scale CTR Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>Taboola 用 Warm-Start 初始化并从旧教师蒸馏，仅在数小时新样本上微调，训练提速 12.5×，线上 A/B 的 RPM 提升 0.53%–0.85%，新内容 CTR 显著上升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回/输入序列层&quot;,&quot;对抗攻击与鲁棒性&quot;,&quot;Dirichlet邻域采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/defending_substitution_based_profile_pollution_attacks_on_sequential_recomm/">Defending Substitution-Based Profile Pollution Attacks on Sequential Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个替换式画像污染攻击+防御框架，用梯度引导攻击、Dirichlet邻域采样与对抗训练，NDCG@10波动降34.4%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2022&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;在线探索与排序策略&quot;,&quot;差分隐私&quot;,&quot;线性Contextual Bandit&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2022/rl_bandit_rec/dynamic_global_sensitivity_for_differentially_private_contextual_bandits/">Dynamic Global Sensitivity for Differentially Private Contextual Bandits</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用模型收敛的动态全局敏感度+树机制降低隐私LinUCB噪声，将附加Regret降至$\tilde O(\log T\sqrt{T}/\epsilon)$，在合成与LastFM上优于Private LinUCB。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;MC Dropout 不确定性估计&quot;,&quot;实时竞价出价探索&quot;,&quot;反馈回路破环&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2022/rl_bandit_rec/exploration_with_model_uncertainty_at_extreme_scale_in_real_time_bidding/">Exploration with Model Uncertainty at Extreme Scale in Real-Time Bidding</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 MC Dropout 的请求级预测不确定性驱动 RTB 出价探索，线上 A/B 相对提升 Revenue 5.0%、CTR 9.6%、离线 AUC +0.24%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;上下文融合&quot;,&quot;组公平性&quot;,&quot;时间偏差&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/exploring_the_impact_of_temporal_bias_in_point_of_interest_recommendation/">Exploring the Impact of Temporal Bias in Point-of-Interest Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出以签到时段（工作/休闲）划分用户组，量化 GeoSoCa/LORE 等上下文感知 POI 模型的时间不公平，并用和式/加权和融合替代乘积融合以降低 ΔnDCG。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;互惠推荐 RRS&quot;,&quot;可转移效用匹配 (TU Matching)&quot;,&quot;IPFP 均衡求解&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2022/collaborative_filtering/matching_theory_based_recommender_systems_in_online_dating/">Matching Theory-based Recommender Systems in Online Dating</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Choo-Siow 可转移效用匹配替代传统融合函数，把互惠偏好与用户承接容量统一到闭式均衡解，并在 tapple 千万级用户平台落地。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排/离线评估&quot;,&quot;Commonality 共性度量&quot;,&quot;文化公民权 Cultural Citizenship&quot;,&quot;公共媒体 PSM&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/measuring_commonality_in_recommendation_of_cultural_content_recommender_sys/">Measuring Commonality in Recommendation of Cultural Content: Recommender Systems to Enhance Cultural Citizenship</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出群体级共性度量 commonality，以 RBP 折损类别召回与联合概率刻画全体用户的共同文化曝光；MovieLens-1M 上 20 个系统排序显示其与 NDCG、α-NDCG 显著负相关，与公平性指标不相关。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2022&quot;,&quot;工业架构与系统&quot;,&quot;精排训练与在线推理服务&quot;,&quot;Hybrid Sparse Embedding&quot;,&quot;Hierarchical Parameter Server&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/merlin_hugectr_gpu_accelerated_recommender_system_training_and_inference/">Merlin HugeCTR: GPU-accelerated Recommender System Training and Inference</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向 TB 级 CTR 模型的 GPU 训练/推理一体化框架：混合稀疏并行、嵌入训练缓存 ETC 与三级分层参数服务器 HPS，MLPerf DLRM 训练最高 24.6×、在线推理 5~62× 加速。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;规范性多样性&quot;,&quot;秩感知散度&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/radio_rank_aware_divergence_metrics_to_measure_normative_diversity_in_news_/">RADio: Rank-Aware Divergence Metrics to Measure Normative Diversity in News Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将新闻规范性多样性统一为秩感知 f-散度（JS）框架，在 MIND 上对 6 种推荐器做五维 DART 度量与敏感性分析。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;全链路设计治理（非排序阶段）&quot;,&quot;算法厌恶&quot;,&quot;人本推荐&quot;,&quot;用户主体性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/recommender_systems_and_algorithmic_hate/">Recommender Systems and Algorithmic Hate</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以多平台案例研究归纳“算法厌恶”的四类成因（无知、伤害、目标错位、主体性缺失）并提出人本设计、透明化与可配置三条缓解议程。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2022&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Transformer&quot;,&quot;长短期兴趣建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2022/sequential_rec/rethinking_personalized_ranking_at_pinterest_an_end_to_end_approach/">Rethinking Personalized Ranking at Pinterest: An End-to-End Approach</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>PinnerFormer 用稠密全动作损失编码长期兴趣，实时序列捕捉短期意图，线上 Repin +12.5%、Ads CTR +14.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;iALS&quot;,&quot;频率缩放正则化&quot;,&quot;超参调优&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2022/collaborative_filtering/revisiting_the_performance_of_ials_on_item_recommendation_benchmarks/">Revisiting the Performance of iALS on Item Recommendation Benchmarks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>复核 iALS 在四个经典基准上的性能，用频率缩放正则、参考尺度 λ* 与超大嵌入维度这套调参配方，把被广泛认为落后的 iALS 拉回到 SOTA 水平。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回（KGNN 训练加速）&quot;,&quot;激活量化&quot;,&quot;随机舍入&quot;,&quot;显存压缩&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2022/graph_rec/tinykg_memory_efficient_training_framework_for_knowledge_graph_neural_recom/">TinyKG: Memory-Efficient Training Framework for Knowledge Graph Neural Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用均匀量化+随机舍入把 KGNN 前向激活压缩为 INT2 存显存，反向反量化算梯度，激活内存降 7× 而 Recall@20 仅降约 1.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;位置偏置建模&quot;,&quot;因果去偏与离线评估&quot;,&quot;曝光概率校准&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/a_probabilistic_position_bias_model_for_short_video_recommendation_feeds/">A Probabilistic Position Bias Model for Short-Video Recommendation Feeds</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Yule-Simon 滚动预算的生存函数给出闭式曝光概率，仅 3 参数的 C-3PO 刷新 NLL 并让离线-在线相关性较 DCG 提升 16%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;Contextual Bandit&quot;,&quot;实时机器学习平台&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/rl_bandit_rec/adaptex_a_self_service_contextual_bandit_platform/">AdaptEx: A Self-Service Contextual Bandit Platform</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>Expedia 自服务上下文 Bandit 平台，Sampler–Flink–Spark 实时闭环，支持 TS/BLR/RLS/IGW/级联 Bandit，把多臂试验从 ~10 周压缩到 1 周。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;TU Matching&quot;,&quot;互惠推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/fast_and_examination_agnostic_reciprocal_recommendation_in_matching_markets/">Fast and Examination-agnostic Reciprocal Recommendation in Matching Markets</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Choo–Siow 可转移效用匹配做互惠推荐重排，摆脱检查函数依赖并用 (2d+2) 维内积实现 ANN 检索，匹配总量超越 Naive/Reciprocal 且与 SW 持平。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;数据采集与反馈&quot;,&quot;评分通胀&quot;,&quot;随机对照实验&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/interface_design_to_mitigate_inflation_in_recommender_systems/">Interface Design to Mitigate Inflation in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用Piki音乐App自然实验与RCT证明评分通胀源于用户异质与个性化曝光，计时器界面可提升评分信息量。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;特征工程与 AutoML 模型搜索（精排前置）&quot;,&quot;基数归一化互信息 CardMI&quot;,&quot;3MR 特征排序&quot;,&quot;哈希交互特征流式估计&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/ctr_feature_interaction/outrank_speeding_up_automl_based_model_search_for_large_sparse_data_sets_wi/">OutRank: Speeding up AutoML-based Model Search for Large Sparse Data sets with Cardinality-aware Feature Ranking</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出基数归一化互信息 CardMI 与 3MR 非短视特征排序，流式哈希估计交互与冗余，在 1.5M 生产 CTR 数据上实现约 30% AutoML 加速且 F1 无损。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排（评分预测）&quot;,&quot;差分隐私&quot;,&quot;集合矩阵分解&quot;,&quot;公共物品特征&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/private_matrix_factorization_with_public_item_features/">Private Matrix Factorization with Public Item Features</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出DP-CMF，将公共物品元数据/相似度/公开评分以集合矩阵分解融入用户级DP矩阵分解，在ML10M评分预测上显著缩小隐私-效用差距。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;变分自编码器&quot;,&quot;表征中立性&quot;,&quot;冷启动新用户&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/providing_previously_unseen_users_fair_recommendations_using_variational_au/">Providing Previously Unseen Users Fair Recommendations Using Variational Autoencoders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在 Mult-VAE 召回上切分隐状态 z/b 并用解析高斯 KL 解耦敏感属性，使用户不相交划分下的新用户也能获得公平推荐</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;端到端评测基准&quot;,&quot;Shilling Attack&quot;,&quot;攻击者知识约束&quot;,&quot;可复现性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/recad_towards_a_unified_library_for_recommender_attack_and_defense/">RecAD: Towards A Unified Library for Recommender Attack and Defense</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>统一推荐攻击-防御基准库，标准化数据/模型/工作流三模块与双轨评估协议，揭示攻击者知识比例与检测失效现象。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/重排&quot;,&quot;最优传输&quot;,&quot;拥堵缓解&quot;,&quot;职位推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/recon_reducing_congestion_in_job_recommendation_using_optimal_transport/">ReCon: Reducing Congestion in Job Recommendation using Optimal Transport</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可微 Sinkhorn 最优传输与 CNE 联合训练，在职位推荐中同时优化拥堵与相关性，取得更优 Pareto 权衡。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Explainable Recommendation&quot;,&quot;Adversarial Robustness&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/stability_of_explainable_recommendation/">Stability of Explainable Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>实证揭示特征级可解释推荐器在参数随机/FGSM扰动下解释稳定性显著下降，采样排序评估显示对抗噪声危害更大。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;可复现性审计&quot;,&quot;GRU4Rec&quot;,&quot;离线评测协议&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/the_effect_of_third_party_implementations_on_reproducibility/">The Effect of Third Party Implementations on Reproducibility</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>审计 GRU4Rec 六个第三方复现：特征缺失与 bug 使 recall@20 相对官方下降 13%–99%，训练慢 2–336 倍，呼吁复现须对齐官方实现。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;时长偏差去偏&quot;,&quot;噪声观看校正&quot;,&quot;因果分解&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/uncovering_user_interest_from_biased_and_noised_watch_time_in_video_recomme/">Uncovering User Interest from Biased and Noised Watch Time in Video Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将观看时长因果分解为兴趣、时长偏差与噪声三项，用分时长 GMM + 敏感度控制校正生成去偏标签，KuaiRand/WeChat 上 GAUC 相对提升约 10%，线上 VV +8.35%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;离线评测协议&quot;,&quot;时间泄漏&quot;,&quot;负采样偏差&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/widespread_flaws_in_offline_evaluation_of_recommender_systems/">Widespread Flaws in Offline Evaluation of Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统梳理离线评测四大缺陷（数据-任务错配、过度预处理、时间泄漏、负采样），用 GRU4Rec 实证证明其可致模型排序反转与数十个百分点虚高。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;投毒攻击&quot;,&quot;代理模型重训练加速&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/accelerating_the_surrogate_retraining_for_poisoning_attacks_against_recomme/">Accelerating the Surrogate Retraining for Poisoning Attacks against Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 Gradient Passing，在反传中沿交互图传递梯度以逼近级联效应，一次更新≈多轮重训练，攻击 Recall@50 提升 18%–177% 且耗时降 27%–43%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;竞价偏差 (Auction Bias)&quot;,&quot;场景感知动态网络&quot;,&quot;后验信号辅助任务&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/ctr_feature_interaction/aie_auction_information_enhanced_framework_for_ctr_prediction_in_online_adv/">AIE: Auction Information Enhanced Framework for CTR Prediction in Online Advertising</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次揭示广告竞价偏差，用 AM2 场景感知价格辅助任务与 BCM 出价重加权两个可插拔模块利用后验竞价信号，iPinYou 上 AUC 平均 +1.01%，线上 eCPM +5.76%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;教师偏差泄漏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/bridging_the_gap_unpacking_the_hidden_challenges_in_knowledge_distillation_/">Bridging the Gap: Unpacking the Hidden Challenges in Knowledge Distillation for Online Ranking Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>针对推荐蒸馏中的数据漂移、教师选型与多学生标签共享三大工业挑战，提出持续更新教师 + 分离 logits 的辅助蒸馏框架，线上互动率最高提升 1.13%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;post-filter&quot;,&quot;multi-sided fairness&quot;,&quot;POI recommendation&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/capri_fair_integration_of_multi_sided_fairness_in_contextual_poi_recommenda/">CAPRI-FAIR: Integration of Multi-sided Fairness in Contextual POI Recommendation Framework</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>免训练后置重排同时注入POI提供者与消费者公平，在Yelp/Gowalla上以Precision与GCE评测权衡长尾曝光和不活跃用户精度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/端到端排序&quot;,&quot;神经架构搜索 (NAS)&quot;,&quot;线性注意力&quot;,&quot;模型压缩与推理加速&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/sequential_rec/dns_rec_data_aware_neural_architecture_search_for_recommender_systems/">DNS-Rec: Data-aware Neural Architecture Search for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用零掩码超网+双层控制器+数据感知门为 SASRec 类序列推荐自动搜索宽度/深度，在 FLOPs 约束下保持精度并将推理时延最高降 87.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/候选集排序&quot;,&quot;新闻推荐数据集&quot;,&quot;Beyond-Accuracy 评估&quot;,&quot;性别导向偏置&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/eb_nerd_a_large_scale_dataset_for_news_recommendation/">EB-NeRD: A Large-Scale Dataset for News Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>发布百万用户丹麦新闻推荐数据集与 RecSys&#x27;24 Challenge 基准，通过打乱位置的曝光日志揭示流行度偏置与性别导向差异。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AI Magazine&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排/前端解释层&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;,&quot;音乐推荐系统&quot;,&quot;知识图谱多跳解释&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/explainability_in_music_recommender_systems/">Explainability in Music Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>立场综述：把 RS 解释目标与 XAI 目标对齐，梳理特征/样例/知识图谱三类解释及用户研究与离线指标双轨评估，并剖析工业 MRS 的‘简洁 vs 复杂’矛盾。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信隐私&quot;,&quot;解释层/决策支持&quot;,&quot;多利益相关方推荐&quot;,&quot;职业知识图谱&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/explainable_multi_stakeholder_job_recommender_systems/">Explainable Multi-Stakeholder Job Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>招聘推荐三方利益相关方解释偏好研究 + 职业知识图谱注意力 GNN（OKRA），发现用户无法区分真实/随机解释，主张转向决策支持式解释。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;后处理(图数据增广)&quot;,&quot;消费者公平性 (Consumer Fairness)&quot;,&quot;图协同过滤增广&quot;,&quot;采样策略&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/fair_augmentation_for_graph_collaborative_filtering/">Fair Augmentation for Graph Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>复现并形式化“公平图增广”后处理范式，推导 3 类新采样策略，在 5 数据集×16 模型上揭示大图高效用模型有效、公平知识不可迁移。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;双边匹配市场&quot;,&quot;嫉妒自由&quot;,&quot;Nash Social Welfare&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/fair_reciprocal_recommendation_in_matching_markets/">Fair Reciprocal Recommendation in Matching Markets</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用NSW与Frank-Wolfe交替优化双边互惠推荐，在保持匹配数同时近零嫉妒，合成/真实约会数据验证。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;离线评测/可复现性&quot;,&quot;绿色推荐&quot;,&quot;碳足迹&quot;,&quot;能耗测量&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/from_clicks_to_carbon_the_environmental_toll_of_recommender_systems/">From Clicks to Carbon: The Environmental Toll of Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>复现79篇RecSys 2013/2023论文管线，硬件测能耗并折算CO2e，揭示深度学习论文约42倍碳排放与能效评估缺口。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;训练阶段（召回表征鲁棒训练）&quot;,&quot;对抗训练&quot;,&quot;投毒攻击&quot;,&quot;用户脆弱性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/improving_the_shortest_plank_vulnerability_aware_adversarial_training_for_r/">Improving the Shortest Plank: Vulnerability-Aware Adversarial Training for Robust Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用用户拟合度估计脆弱性并逐用户自适应对抗扰动幅度，全库排序下攻击成功率平均降 21.53%，推荐指标不降反升</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;FwFM&quot;,&quot;低秩分解&quot;,&quot;低延迟推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/ctr_feature_interaction/low_rank_field_weighted_factorization_machines_for_low_latency_item_recomme/">Low Rank Field-Weighted Factorization Machines for Low Latency Item Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以对角+低秩重参数化 FwFM 字段交互矩阵，上下文缓存使逐物品推断降至 $O(\rho|I|k)$，同参数下优于剪枝并降低线上延迟。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;上下文 Bandit&quot;,&quot;贝叶斯决策论&quot;,&quot;变分推断&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/position_paper_why_the_shooting_in_the_dark_method_dominates_recommender_sy/">Position Paper: Why the Shooting in the Dark Method Dominates Recommender Systems Practice; A Call to Abandon Anti-Utopian Thinking</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>论证工业界「离线代理指标盲选 + A/B 验证」缺乏理论依据，主张用定制似然与变分贝叶斯直接瞄准奖励优化。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;线上评测与实验平台&quot;,&quot;A/B 测试&quot;,&quot;代理指标与统计功效&quot;,&quot;多重假设检验校正&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/powerful_a_b_testing_metrics_and_where_to_find_them/">Powerful A/B-Testing Metrics and Where to Find Them</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用数百个历史 A/B 实验构建指标校验管线，量化 I/II/III 类错误与统计功效；联合 DAU/Engagers/TimeSpent 在 95% 置信下相对降低 II 类错误 35%，或把所需样本量缩小 3.5 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;拆分学习&quot;,&quot;标签差分隐私&quot;,&quot;LoRA&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/ctr_feature_interaction/privacy_preserving_conversion_modeling_in_data_clean_room/">Privacy Preserving Conversion Modeling in Data Clean Room</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>数据洁净室内以批量聚合梯度+LoRA 适配器+标签 DP 去偏实现隐私合规的 CVR 联合训练，ROC-AUC 仅降 0.2% 而通信开销降 4 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;多任务学习与列表级重排&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;跨内容类型排序&quot;,&quot;曝光预算与多样性&quot;,&quot;线上 A/B 测试&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/multi_task_reranking/ranking_across_different_content_types_the_robust_beauty_of_multinomial_ble/">Ranking Across Different Content Types: The Robust Beauty of Multinomial Blending</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用多项式混合(MB)在打分后按内容类型采样组列表，实现可解释、可重训稳定的跨内容类型曝光预算，Amazon Music 线上 A/B 播客时长 +18.82% 且整体互动 +2.23%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;工业级推荐系统框架&quot;,&quot;端到端训练与推理&quot;,&quot;多后端数据框&quot;,&quot;可复现评测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/replay_a_recommendation_framework_for_experimentation_and_production_use/">RePlay: a Recommendation Framework for Experimentation and Production Use</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>RePlay 以统一 Dataset/FeatureSchema 打通预处理、切分、训练、调参与评测，支持 Pandas/Polars/Spark 及 CPU/GPU/集群。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;协同过滤&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;BPR&quot;,&quot;可复现性研究&quot;,&quot;负采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/collaborative_filtering/revisiting_bpr_a_replicability_study_of_a_common_recommender_system_baselin/">Revisiting BPR: A Replicability Study of a Common Recommender System Baseline</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统复现 BPR 并解耦四大实现特征，证明调优后的 BPR 在全局时间切分下可追平甚至超越 EASE/Mult-VAE。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;计算社会选择&quot;,&quot;异构公平性&quot;,&quot;公平感知重排&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/social_choice_for_heterogeneous_fairness_in_recommendation/">Social Choice for Heterogeneous Fairness in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用计算社会选择的多智能体架构 SCRUF-D 将三类异构提供方公平性定义分别交由独立 Agent，在分配—聚合两阶段中实现多目标公平重排，且几乎不损 nDCG。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;在线列表探索&quot;,&quot;Engagement-Utility 错位&quot;,&quot;流行度偏差&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/rl_bandit_rec/the_fault_in_our_recommendations_on_the_perils_of_optimizing_the_measurable/">The Fault in Our Recommendations: On the Perils of Optimizing the Measurable</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用重复选择模型揭示 engagement 与 utility 错位，提出 PEAR 后验探索；δ=0.99 时 utility +53.4% 仅损 engagement 0.12%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;数据采集与评测基准&quot;,&quot;用户信念 (Beliefs)&quot;,&quot;选择行为建模&quot;,&quot;反事实评估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/the_movielens_beliefs_dataset_collecting_pre_choice_data_for_online_recomme/">The MovieLens Beliefs Dataset: Collecting Pre-Choice Data for Online Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出在 MovieLens 首页以非侵入方式采集用户对未观看影片的“选择前信念”，开源 3,199 用户/28,457 条信念+172 万评分数据集，并量化响应选择偏差与推荐-信念覆盖率。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;重听行为建模&quot;,&quot;ACT-R 认知架构&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/sequential_rec/transformers_meet_act_r_repeat_aware_and_sequential_listening_session_recom/">Transformers Meet ACT-R: Repeat-Aware and Sequential Listening Session Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 ACT-R 认知激活权重改造 Transformer 会话序列建模，兼顾重听与探索，Last.fm/Deezer 全库 Top-10 排序 NDCG 提升 32.5%/9.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;公平性度量选择&quot;,&quot;医疗推荐&quot;,&quot;用户研究&quot;,&quot;情境敏感设计&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/understanding_fairness_in_recommender_systems_a_healthcare_perspective/">Understanding Fairness in Recommender Systems: A Healthcare Perspective</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>131人问卷研究：在高危/低危两种医疗推荐场景下让公众从四种公平度量中选择，发现公众理解度低、偏好随情境漂移且无共识。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2024&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;离线评估/策略学习&quot;,&quot;逆倾向得分 IPS&quot;,&quot;反事实方差缩减&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2024/rl_bandit_rec/text_rm_ope_off_policy_estimation_with_pairs_of_policies/">Δ-OPE: Off-Policy Estimation with Pairs of Policies</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 off-policy 评估从单策略估值改为成对策略差值估计，用加性控制变量 Δβ-IPS 显著降方差，仿真与线上 A/B 双验证。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;评测体系&quot;,&quot;多利益相关者&quot;,&quot;价值敏感设计&quot;,&quot;数字档案&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/social_group_rec/a_multistakeholder_approach_to_value_driven_co_design_of_recommender_system/">A Multistakeholder Approach to Value-Driven Co-Design of Recommender System Evaluation Metrics in Digital Archives</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>通过 5×5 焦点小组将档案领域五类利益相关者价值映射为四阶段研究漏斗评估指标框架，提出研究路径质量、元数据加权相关性等指标方向。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回/偏好推断&quot;,&quot;离散选择模型&quot;,&quot;核密度估计&quot;,&quot;曝光偏差&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/collaborative_filtering/a_non_parametric_choice_model_that_learns_how_users_choose_between_recommen/">A Non-Parametric Choice Model That Learns How Users Choose Between Recommended Options</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 sigmoid 核密度估计学习误差分布，无需假设 MNL/ENL 即可同时推断用户偏好与选择机制，抗竞争型曝光偏差。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;捆绑推荐公平性&quot;,&quot;曝光偏差&quot;,&quot;双层公平评估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/a_reproducibility_study_of_product_side_fairness_in_bundle_recommendation/">A Reproducibility Study of Product-side Fairness in Bundle Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个捆绑推荐商品侧公平性复现研究，揭示捆绑级与物品级曝光分布不一致、指标间结论分歧，并发现用户捆绑偏好越强曝光越公平。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准与数据集选择&quot;,&quot;Algorithm Performance Space&quot;,&quot;PCA 可视化&quot;,&quot;数据集元特征&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/multimodal_rec/aps_explorer_navigating_algorithm_performance_spaces_for_informed_dataset_s/">APS Explorer: Navigating Algorithm Performance Spaces for Informed Dataset Selection</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 29 算法 × 96 数据集的性能向量投影为可交互 PCA 性能空间，用可视化工具支撑可辩护的数据集选择。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;画像生成&quot;,&quot;用户研究&quot;,&quot;因果去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/llm_rec/biases_in_llm_generated_musical_taste_profiles_for_recommendation/">Biases in LLM-Generated Musical Taste Profiles for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>通过 64 人用户研究揭示 LLM 生成音乐品味画像存在流派/国别系统性偏差，且用户认同度与下游推荐指标仅弱相关。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;随机游走&quot;,&quot;规范目标分布&quot;,&quot;新闻推荐多样性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/multi_task_reranking/d_rdw_diversity_driven_random_walks_for_news_recommender_systems/">D-RDW: Diversity-Driven Random Walks for News Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将规范目标分布(NTD)作为线性约束嵌入随机游走采样，实现可解释、低能耗的新闻多样化重排，能耗降低一个数量级。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Transformer 序列建模&quot;,&quot;模块化基准评测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/sequential_rec/esasrec_enhancing_transformer_based_recommendations_in_a_modular_fashion/">eSASRec: Enhancing Transformer-based Recommendations in a Modular Fashion</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统消融 Transformer 序列推荐各模块增强，提出 SASRec+LiGR+SS 组合 eSASRec，在时间切分与覆盖率指标下与 HSTU 同处帕累托前沿。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;极值理论&quot;,&quot;量子特征选择&quot;,&quot;QUBO/QAOA&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/collaborative_filtering/estimating_quantum_execution_requirements_for_feature_selection_in_recommen/">Estimating Quantum Execution Requirements for Feature Selection in Recommender Systems Using Extreme Value Theory</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用极值理论对 QAOA 求解 QUBO 特征选择的输出建模，估计达到经典 SA 水平所需的量子执行次数，并在模拟器与真实 QPU 上验证。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;离线评测&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-a-Judge&quot;,&quot;用户画像&quot;,&quot;播客推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/llm_rec/evaluating_podcast_recommendations_with_profile_aware_llm_as_a_judge/">Evaluating Podcast Recommendations with Profile-Aware LLM-as-a-Judge</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用90天收听历史蒸馏自然语言用户画像，驱动零样本LLM做剧集级与模型级离线评判，47人用户研究中与人工判断高度一致。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;离线回放评估&quot;,&quot;线性上下文 Bandit&quot;,&quot;探索-利用权衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/rl_bandit_rec/exploitation_over_exploration_unmasking_the_bias_in_linear_bandit_recommend/">Exploitation Over Exploration: Unmasking the Bias in Linear Bandit Recommender Offline Evaluation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>大规模实证揭示线性 Bandit 离线回放评估系统性偏袒纯利用策略，贪心模型在 89% 数据集上不劣于探索变体。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与POI推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;流行度校准&quot;,&quot;上下文感知POI推荐&quot;,&quot;流行度偏差&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/exploring_the_effect_of_context_awareness_and_popularity_calibration_on_pop/">Exploring the Effect of Context-Awareness and Popularity Calibration on Popularity Bias in POI Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统评测上下文感知模型与校准流行度(CP)重排在POI推荐中的去偏效果，发现二者组合可在保精度同时最贴近用户流行度画像。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;扩散推荐公平性&quot;,&quot;消费者与提供者公平&quot;,&quot;多准则权衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/generative_rec/how_fair_is_your_diffusion_recommender_model/">How Fair is Your Diffusion Recommender Model?</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个针对 DiffRec/L-DiffRec 的公平性实证研究，在 ML1M/FTKY 上以 Full-Ranking 评估效用与消费者/提供者公平，揭示扩散模型放大偏见而 L-DiffRec 可缓解。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;复现研究&quot;,&quot;流行度偏差&quot;,&quot;儿童用户群体&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/cross_domain_multi_scenario/impacts_of_mainstream_driven_algorithms_on_recommendations_for_children_acr/">Impacts of Mainstream-Driven Algorithms on Recommendations for Children Across Domains: A Reproducibility Study</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>跨电影/音乐/图书三域复现儿童与主流用户偏好偏差，并引入流行度偏差指标，揭示推荐算法对儿童群体的域特异性失效。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;特征工程/在线推理&quot;,&quot;Spark-Keras 等价翻译&quot;,&quot;训练-服务一致性&quot;,&quot;特征预处理管线&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/collaborative_filtering/kamae_bridging_spark_and_keras_for_seamless_ml_preprocessing/">Kamae: Bridging Spark and Keras for Seamless ML Preprocessing</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 PySpark 预处理管线一对一翻译为等价 Keras 层，消除离线/在线特征工程重复实现，Expedia 线上延迟降 61%、成本降 58%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;虚拟用户生成&quot;,&quot;跨域公平性&quot;,&quot;双注意力机制&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/cross_domain_multi_scenario/leave_no_one_behind_fairness_aware_cross_domain_recommender_systems_for_non/">Leave No One Behind: Fairness-Aware Cross-Domain Recommender Systems for Non-Overlapping Users</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>为无源域数据的非重叠用户生成虚拟源域画像，双注意力+限制器实现模型无关的跨域公平性纠偏，UGF最高降94.13%且整体精度不降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双曲几何&quot;,&quot;三元组损失&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/leveraging_geometric_insights_in_hyperbolic_triplet_loss_for_improved_recom/">Leveraging Geometric Insights in Hyperbolic Triplet Loss for Improved Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>洛伦兹双曲三元组损失 TriplH，以物品-物品项自适应间隔替代固定 margin，兼顾精度、去流行度偏差与 3 倍推理加速。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;离线评估&quot;,&quot;离策略估计&quot;,&quot;元分析&quot;,&quot;BLUE&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/collaborative_filtering/meta_off_policy_estimation/">Meta Off-Policy Estimation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多个 OPE 估计器视为相关研究做固定效应元分析，用 BLUE 闭式解融合并给出保守置信区间，OBD 上方差降超 50%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;反事实解释&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;LLM 画像过滤&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/mitigating_popularity_bias_in_counterfactual_explanations_using_large_langu/">Mitigating Popularity Bias in Counterfactual Explanations using Large Language Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 LLM 生成用户画像并贪心剔除“出格”历史项，作为 ACCENT 反事实解释的即插即用预处理，使解释流行度分布更贴合用户偏好。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Off-Policy Evaluation&quot;,&quot;双向匹配推荐&quot;,&quot;逆倾向得分&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/rl_bandit_rec/off_policy_evaluation_and_learning_for_matching_markets/">Off-Policy Evaluation and Learning for Matching Markets</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向双向匹配市场提出 DiPS/DPR 两阶段奖励 OPE 估计器，利用一阶段 scout 信号降方差，合成+Wantedly 真实 A/B 日志验证 MSE 与策略选择显著优于 DM/IPS/DR。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;流行度偏置&quot;,&quot;向量模长重标定&quot;,&quot;物品公平性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/cold_start_rec/on_inherited_popularity_bias_in_cold_start_item_recommendation/">On Inherited Popularity Bias in Cold-Start Item Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示生成式冷启动模型继承热模型流行度偏置，提出基于向量模长重标定的免训练后处理去偏方法，在三个多媒体数据集上显著提升物品公平性与曝光多样性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;离线评测&quot;,&quot;负采样策略&quot;,&quot;曝光偏差&quot;,&quot;元评测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/collaborative_filtering/on_the_reliability_of_sampling_strategies_in_offline_recommender_evaluation/">On the Reliability of Sampling Strategies in Offline Recommender Evaluation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以全观测 KuaiRec 为真值，四维度（分辨率/保真度/鲁棒性/预测力）系统解耦曝光偏差与采样偏差，给出采样策略选择指南。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;稀疏自编码器&quot;,&quot;神经元转向&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/opening_the_black_box_interpretable_remedies_for_popularity_bias_in_recomme/">Opening the Black Box: Interpretable Remedies for Popularity Bias in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用稀疏自编码器定位流行度神经元并做激活转向，在 ML-1M/Last.fm 上以极小精度损失换取显著曝光公平提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;模型反演攻击&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec 隐私&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/privacy_risks_of_llm_empowered_recommender_systems_an_inversion_attack_pers/">Privacy Risks of LLM-Empowered Recommender Systems: An Inversion Attack Perspective</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次系统研究 LLM 推荐系统的 logits 反演攻击，提出相似度引导精炼，可恢复 65% 交互物品与 87% 年龄性别。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;主题偏置&quot;,&quot;公平性评估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/cold_start_rec/reading_between_the_lines_a_study_of_thematic_bias_in_book_recommender_syst/">Reading Between the Lines: A Study of Thematic Bias in Book Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 BERTopic 为 Book-Crossing 打主题标签，构建数据-推荐-用户三阶段主题偏置评估框架，揭示长尾小众读者被主流主题同质化的现象。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双效用行为建模&quot;,&quot;局部贪心最优策略&quot;,&quot;站外选项估计&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/collaborative_filtering/recommendation_and_temptation/">Recommendation and Temptation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将用户选择解耦为 enrichment 与 temptation 双效用并引入站外选项，证明局部贪心最优并给出可估计算子，MovieLens 与仿真全面超越基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;冷热阈值&quot;,&quot;p-core 过滤&quot;,&quot;评估协议&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/cold_start_rec/recommendation_is_a_dish_better_served_warm/">Recommendation Is a Dish Better Served Warm</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统量化冷启动阈值：物品侧训练交互扫描+用户侧推理历史截断，揭示 6–15 条交互的冷热分界并批判 p-core 过滤的任意性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;训练数据治理/模型遗忘&quot;,&quot;成员推断攻击 (MIA)&quot;,&quot;差分隐私审计&quot;,&quot;隐私风险评分&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/recps_privacy_risk_scoring_for_recommender_systems/">RecPS: Privacy Risk Scoring for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>基于差分隐私推导交互级隐私分数，用 RecLiRA 成员推断攻击估计 TPR/FPR，指导细粒度交互移除以兼顾隐私与效用。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/向量检索&quot;,&quot;嵌入反演攻击&quot;,&quot;嵌入量化防御&quot;,&quot;隐私保护&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/rethinking_the_privacy_of_text_embeddings_a_reproducibility_study_of_text_e/">Rethinking the Privacy of Text Embeddings: A Reproducibility Study of \&quot;Text Embeddings Reveal (Almost) As Much As Text\&quot;</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>复现 Vec2Text 嵌入反演攻击，揭示其长度敏感性，并提出免调参 8-bit 量化作为轻量隐私防御。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;Ricci 曲率&quot;,&quot;根因归因&quot;,&quot;金融图推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/ricciflowrec_a_geometric_root_cause_recommender_using_ricci_curvature_on_fi/">RicciFlowRec: A Geometric Root Cause Recommender Using Ricci Curvature on Financial Graphs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Ollivier/Forman-Ricci 曲率与 Ricci 流在动态金融图上做根因归因，以曲率风险惩罚重排资产，NDCG@10 提升 4.9%、Top-10 波动降 28%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;公平性评估&quot;,&quot;组公平与个体公平&quot;,&quot;LLM推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/stairway_to_fairness_connecting_group_and_individual_fairness/">Stairway to Fairness: Connecting Group and Individual Fairness</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次用同一族度量（SD/Gini/Atk）在 3 数据集 8 组 LLMRec 运行上实证组公平与个体公平的错位，揭示组间公平掩盖组内与个体不公。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;评测与可复现性审计&quot;,&quot;超参数优化&quot;,&quot;框架默认值&quot;,&quot;早停策略&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/collaborative_filtering/the_hidden_cost_of_defaults_in_recommender_system_evaluation/">The Hidden Cost of Defaults in Recommender System Evaluation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>审计 RecBole 未文档化的 early_stop=10 默认策略如何静默截断随机搜索与贝叶斯优化，量化搜索路径不稳定性与可复现性风险。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;离线评测协议&quot;,&quot;数据切分&quot;,&quot;全局时间切分&quot;,&quot;时序泄漏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/sequential_rec/time_to_split_exploring_data_splitting_strategies_for_offline_evaluation_of/">Time to Split: Exploring Data Splitting Strategies for Offline Evaluation of Sequential Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统对比 LOO 与全局时间切分及目标/验证变体，揭示 LOO 时序泄漏与排序失真，推荐 GTS+Last/Random/Successive 协议。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2025&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;保形风险控制&quot;,&quot;负反馈去害&quot;,&quot;重复消费替换&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2025/social_group_rec/you_don_t_bring_me_flowers_mitigating_unwanted_recommendations_through_conf/">You Don&#x27;t Bring Me Flowers: Mitigating Unwanted Recommendations Through Conformal Risk Control</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用保形风险控制对任意排序器做后处理，以“重复安全内容替换”替代纯删除，在 KuaiRand 上可证明地把不想要内容比例压到用户指定 α 以下且保住 nDCG。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;可验证奖励&quot;,&quot;pass^k 可靠性&quot;,&quot;Reveal-Tagged Elicitation&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/agent_conversational_rec/rec_a_verifiable_benchmark_for_agentic_recommender_systems/">$τ$-Rec: A Verifiable Benchmark for Agentic Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可验证目录谓词奖励、reveal-tagged 信息揭示机制与 pass^k 可靠性指标替代 LLM-as-judge，揭示智能体推荐在隐藏约束下的可靠性悬崖。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;评测协议&quot;,&quot;并列感知评估&quot;,&quot;分数压缩&quot;,&quot;BPR 温度缩放&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/social_group_rec/are_we_really_making_progress_in_group_recommendation_unmasking_the_tie_bre/">Are We Really Making Progress in Group Recommendation? Unmasking the Tie-Breaking Illusion</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示群组推荐中训练期 sigmoid 分数压缩与评估期确定性破平交互导致的系统性评估偏差，提出并列感知 HR/NDCG 精确期望协议，使多个 SOTA 方法报告增益大幅缩水。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;多智能体协同过滤&quot;,&quot;拓扑连通性安全&quot;,&quot;LLM 红队攻防&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/attacking_and_defending_multi_agent_collaborative_filtering_systems_through/">Attacking and Defending Multi-Agent Collaborative Filtering Systems Through Connectivity</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在 AgentCF 上系统复现 6 类攻击与 4 类防御，揭示候选数 k 与目录集中度 ρ 对用户/物品分区的非对称、非单调调控规律。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;差分隐私后处理&quot;,&quot;Margin 稳定性证书&quot;,&quot;锚点混合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/decoupled_learning_and_selection_in_slate_recommendation_for_privacy_and_st/">Decoupled Learning and Selection in Slate Recommendation for Privacy and Stability Under Noisy Scores</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将列表推荐拆为随机打分器+确定性选择器，给出隐私后处理契约与可审计的 margin 稳定性证书，锚点加权使 Flip@K 降 53–81%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;容量探针&quot;,&quot;基准效度&quot;,&quot;成对转移模型&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/sequential_rec/do_sequential_recommendation_benchmarks_really_require_higher_order_sequenc/">Do Sequential Recommendation Benchmarks Really Require Higher-Order Sequence Modelling?</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用两个不学高阶序列表征的近期加权成对探针（SeqRules/PCTM）在 eSASRec 协议下反超 Transformer，质疑主流基准的容量测量效度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;工业级全链路架构与评测基准&quot;,&quot;新闻推荐&quot;,&quot;可解释性&quot;,&quot;定性访谈研究&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/do_we_care_about_personalization_and_explainability_an_interview_study_with/">Do We Care About Personalization and Explainability? An Interview Study with News Recommendation Engineers</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>对 9 家新闻机构 15 位工程师的半结构化访谈，揭示个性化采纳受编辑控制/隐私/资源约束，可解释性几乎只用于内部调试而非终端用户。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;轮播界面点击模型&quot;,&quot;离策略评估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/from_click_modeling_to_offline_and_off_policy_evaluation_in_carousel_recomm/">From Click Modeling to Offline and Off-Policy Evaluation in Carousel Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>基于 RecGaze 眼动数据推翻轮播界面四大行为假设，提出以可观测变量为核心的点击模型设计框架、离散选择点击模型与 N2DCG 重构，并规划轮播专用 OPE 路线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与POI推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;虚拟用户生成&quot;,&quot;提供方公平性&quot;,&quot;潜在扩散模型&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/give_the_long_tail_more_space_promoting_provider_fairness_in_next_poi_recom/">Give the Long-tail More SPACE: Promoting Provider Fairness in Next POI Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以社区推断+非平衡最优传输配额+约束引导潜扩散生成虚拟用户，在物理可达与供给约束下提升长尾POI曝光，公平性最高提升94.11%且精度不降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;异构内容排序&quot;,&quot;MoE&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/multi_task_reranking/heterogeneous_ranking_in_industrial_scale_recommender_systems_a_case_study/">Heterogeneous Ranking in Industrial-Scale Recommender Systems: A Case Study</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向 Google Discover 异构信息流的 HA-MoE 多门控专家架构，配合 LENS 可观测性与 DL-AUC 双层评估，离线与线上均获稳定增益。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;离线评估&quot;,&quot;曝光份额预测&quot;,&quot;反事实因果推断&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/impression_share_prediction_an_offline_evaluation_task_for_ranking_systems/">Impression Share Prediction: An Offline Evaluation Task for Ranking Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出曝光份额预测作为离线评估新任务，用因果DAG证明反事实可识别，PatchTST编码器在首小时恢复+22% L1。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;轻量排序头&quot;,&quot;在线持续学习&quot;,&quot;任务冲突隔离&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/multi_task_reranking/lightweight_ranking_heads_accelerating_multi_task_experimentation_in_produc/">Lightweight Ranking Heads: Accelerating Multi-Task Experimentation in Production Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 stop-gradient + 无状态日训的轻量头动态注入新任务，免骨干重训，将多任务实验周期从数周压缩至数天。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;稀疏特征选择&quot;,&quot;特征重要性排序&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/ctr_feature_interaction/lo_far_a_cost_aware_local_filter_for_sparse_feature_ranking_in_industrial_a/">LO-FAR: A Cost-Aware Local Filter for Sparse Feature Ranking in Industrial Ad Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用CPU级逐特征独立局部估计器替代GPU重训式置换/门控重要性，在475个稀疏ID-list特征上以约2 CPU小时完成排序并保持下游NE增益。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;成员推理攻击&quot;,&quot;In-Context Learning&quot;,&quot;隐私泄露&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/membership_inference_attacks_on_in_context_examples_in_llm_based_recommende/">Membership Inference Attacks on In-Context Examples in LLM-based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次针对 ICL 式 LLM 推荐系统提出 ItemMem 与 RecInertia 两种成员推理攻击，黑盒下攻击优势最高近 1.0，现有提示抽取防御基本失效。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;隐变量测量模型&quot;,&quot;时长去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/multimodal_rec/mend_the_measurement_gap_latent_user_preference_modeling_for_short_form_vid/">Mend the Measurement Gap: Latent User Preference Modeling for Short-Form Video Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将异构行为反馈视为隐价值状态的噪声测量，用受限基线+因子化路由瓶颈解耦时长混杂，YouTube Shorts 线上主指标提升 2.67%</p>
</dd>
