<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;用户表征学习&quot;,&quot;多专家舰队&quot;,&quot;免日志评估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/collaborative_filtering/mosaic_a_fleet_of_user_embedding_specialists_for_recommendation_at_meta/">Mosaic: A Fleet of User Embedding Specialists for Recommendation at Meta</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以四类异构专家模型族构建用户表征舰队，配合 MRM 与余弦冗余损失最大化增量信息，并提出免日志 CoEval 评估，离线 NE 稳定下降、线上 A/B 显著提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;嵌入表哈希&quot;,&quot;零碰撞线性探测&quot;,&quot;嵌入新鲜度&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/collaborative_filtering/multi_probe_zero_collision_hash_mpzch_mitigating_embedding_collisions_and_e/">Multi-Probe Zero Collision Hash (MPZCH): Mitigating Embedding Collisions and Enhancing Model Freshness in Large-Scale Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用线性探测+身份/元数据辅助张量与主动淘汰，在工业级嵌入表上实现零碰撞并提升新内容新鲜度，训练/推理延迟几乎不变。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;标签偏移&quot;,&quot;规范对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/normative_alignment_of_recommender_systems_via_internal_label_shift/">Normative Alignment of Recommender Systems via Internal Label Shift</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将编辑/公平目标建模为属性层内部标签偏移，用免重训的闭式重加权实现系统级规范对齐，KL 大幅下降且 AUC 基本无损。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;收敛效度&quot;,&quot;候选集采样&quot;,&quot;稀疏反馈偏差&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/on_the_convergent_validity_of_offline_evaluation_designs_for_recommender_sy/">On the Convergent Validity of Offline Evaluation Designs for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以稠密真值反馈为参照，用 Kendall&#x27;s τ 检验稀疏离线评测设计的收敛效度，发现无普适最优设计且 KuaiRec 上排序近乎反转。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;冷启动路由&quot;,&quot;LambdaRank&quot;,&quot;信息流生态&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/cold_start_rec/predicting_custom_feed_returns_for_new_bluesky_posts_a_prospective_study/">Predicting Custom-Feed Returns for New Bluesky Posts: A Prospective Study</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将新帖作为查询、信息流作为候选的冷启动路由任务，构建 collect-first/label-later 基准，LambdaRank 达 cR@10 0.7361。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;云边协同&quot;,&quot;隐私保护推荐&quot;,&quot;DPP 多样性正则&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/pricorec_a_privacy_aware_cloud_device_collaborative_framework_for_ad_recomm/">PriCoRec: A Privacy-Aware Cloud-Device Collaborative Framework for Ad Recommendation under Feature Constraints</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将敏感特征留在端侧，云侧预排加 DPP 多样性正则、端侧轻量排序加云端 logit 蒸馏，三数据集 gAUC/R@10 全面领先且时延可控。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果去偏&quot;,&quot;概率用户聚类&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/probabilistic_residual_learning_for_online_recommendations/">Probabilistic Residual Learning for Online Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以即插即用的层次贝叶斯残差模型对固定基座推荐器做概率用户聚类与 do-calculus 混杂去偏，冷启动跨域 Recall@20 最高提升约 7.6 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双塔检索&quot;,&quot;多智能体架构搜索&quot;,&quot;奖励黑客&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/collaborative_filtering/recevolve_a_knowledge_driven_autonomous_agent_system_for_recommender_system/">RecEvolve: A Knowledge-Driven Autonomous Agent System for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将双塔召回模型的架构搜索全生命周期交给多智能体闭环，41 次生产级训练换来 NDCG@50 相对 +19.9%、线上满意度 +3.77%，并意外暴露奖励黑客漏洞。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;残差主导&quot;,&quot;末项依赖&quot;,&quot;可解释性诊断&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/sequential_rec/residual_dominance_as_a_structural_account_of_last_item_reliance_in_causal_/">Residual Dominance as a Structural Account of Last-Item Reliance in Causal Self-Attention Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用范数分解揭示残差连接主导同位置信息保留，并以推理期残差缩放诊断因果自注意力推荐器的末项依赖机制。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;超长序列压缩&quot;,&quot;低秩缓存&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/sequential_rec/sequenceo1_end_to_end_ultra_long_100k_sequence_modeling_in_recommendation_w/">SequenceO1: End-to-End Ultra-Long (100K) Sequence Modeling in Recommendation with Low-Rank Caching</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可学习原型压缩 100K 历史为定长 sketch 并缓存复用，端到端精排训练/推理 FLOPs 降 49.9×/63.9×，线上 Finish +2.33%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;冷启动&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;课程学习&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;连续训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/cold_start_rec/soft_curriculum_learning_for_optimizing_fresh_and_generalized_recommendatio/">Soft Curriculum Learning for Optimizing Fresh and Generalized Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用损失退火与图内权重替代刚性数据过滤，在连续训练中打破流行度反馈环，线上冷启动互动 +5.70%、满意度 +0.54% 且吞吐无损。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-a-Judge&quot;,&quot;可解释推荐评估&quot;,&quot;用户研究&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/agent_conversational_rec/the_utility_of_llms_in_recommender_systems_explanation_evaluation/">The Utility of LLMs in Recommender Systems Explanation Evaluation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统评测 14 个 LLM 作为推荐解释评审员与 216 名人类评分的一致性，发现排序相关中等但绝对评分一致性低，并给出四条实用准则。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;训练种子方差&quot;,&quot;模型选择稳定性&quot;,&quot;可复现性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/training_seeds_and_model_selection_stability_in_recommender_system_evaluati/">Training seeds and model-selection stability in recommender-system evaluation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>固定数据划分与超参、仅变训练种子，从用户级指标敏感性、验证选模稳定性、验证-测试一致性与推荐列表一致性四层量化种子噪声，主张种子应纳入评估协议。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;位置偏差控制函数&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;图消息传递&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/unbiased_recommender_systems_with_implicit_feedback/">Unbiased Recommender Systems with Implicit Feedback</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>博士论文愿景：以控制函数法纠偏排序位置偏差、以事后投影与消息传递加权纠偏 GNN 协同过滤的流行度偏差。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;在线实验平台&quot;,&quot;曝光感知日志&quot;,&quot;双表面策略解耦&quot;,&quot;视频推荐用户研究&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/watchlens_a_configurable_platform_for_online_video_recommendation_experimen/">WatchLens: A Configurable Platform for Online Video Recommendation Experiments</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>开源视频推荐在线实验平台，解耦界面/内容池/双表面策略并原生记录曝光上下文，30人被试验证观看页策略对会话留存的影响。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;曝光公平性审计&quot;,&quot;固定效应回归&quot;,&quot;自定义Feed生态&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/RecSys/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/whose_posts_get_ranked_identical_text_exposure_gaps_in_bluesky_custom_feeds/">Whose Posts Get Ranked: Identical-Text Exposure Gaps in Bluesky Custom Feeds</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Conference on Recommender Systems">RecSys</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>基于自然重复帖的曝光审计：同文本跨作者在250个Bluesky自定义Feed中曝光差异达33%，作者历史主导排名</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回/精排双阶段&quot;,&quot;开放基准与可复现性&quot;,&quot;Full-Ranking 评测协议&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/bars_towards_open_benchmarking_for_recommender_systems/">BARS: Towards Open Benchmarking for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出含 7 项标准产物的开放基准流水线，固化数据划分与评测协议 ID，在 6 数据集 70+ 模型上完成 8000+ 次全库排序与 CTR 复现实验，揭示近年增益被系统性夸大。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;Two-sided Fairness&quot;,&quot;Constrained Re-ranking&quot;,&quot;Greedy MILP Relaxation&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/cpfair_personalized_consumer_and_producer_fairness_re_ranking_for_recommend/">CPFair: Personalized Consumer and Producer Fairness Re-ranking for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用加性贪心重排把用户侧与物品侧公平偏差同时作为软惩罚，8 数据集上 mCPF 平均降 12.8%~46.9% 且精度不降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;用户公平&quot;,&quot;复现性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/experiments_on_generalizability_of_user_oriented_fairness_in_recommender_sy/">Experiments on Generalizability of User-Oriented Fairness in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统复现并泛化评估用户公平重排 UFR：8 数据集 6 基础模型 2 分组，揭示领域/分组/基模型依赖与公平-效用权衡。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排解释&quot;,&quot;反事实解释&quot;,&quot;公平-效用权衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/explainable_fairness_in_recommendation/">Explainable Fairness in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出反事实可解释公平框架 CEF，用最小特征扰动解释曝光不公平，并在三数据集擦除评测中取得更优公平-效用权衡。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;归一化系数 r&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;LightGCN&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/investigating_accuracy_novelty_performance_for_graph_based_collaborative_fi/">Investigating Accuracy-Novelty Performance for Graph-based Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>理论证明 GNN 对称归一化是流行度偏置之源，提出零开销插件 r-AdjNorm，通过单一系数 r 控制归一化强度，在三个基准上不损精度的同时显著提升新颖性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排/排序策略&quot;,&quot;期望曝光&quot;,&quot;多边公平&quot;,&quot;Plackett-Luce&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/joint_multisided_exposure_fairness_for_recommendation/">Joint Multisided Exposure Fairness for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出六类用户-物品双侧群体曝光公平指标，分解差异/相关性，并用可微 Plackett-Luce 随机排序优化 GG-F。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;自然语言用户画像&quot;,&quot;可审阅推荐 (Scrutability)&quot;,&quot;大语言模型&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/on_natural_language_user_profiles_for_transparent_and_scrutable_recommendat/">On Natural Language User Profiles for Transparent and Scrutable Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出以自然语言用户画像替代稠密向量作为推荐的唯一中间表示，分解为画像生成与 NL 推荐两步，并用 LaMDA 少样本提示做定性可行性验证。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;概率校准&quot;,&quot;保序回归&quot;,&quot;自适应分箱&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/ctr_feature_interaction/posterior_probability_matters_doubly_adaptive_calibration_for_neural_predic/">Posterior Probability Matters: Doubly-Adaptive Calibration for Neural Predictions in Online Advertising</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 AdaCalib，用后验统计引导的分段线性保序函数族做字段级概率校准，并以频率驱动的自适应分箱匹配字段取值，Field-RCE 相对降 10%~70%，线上 CVR +5.05%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;数据发布/预处理&quot;,&quot;隐私可控合成数据&quot;,&quot;Gumbel-Softmax 离散采样&quot;,&quot;隐私-效用权衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/privacy_preserving_synthetic_data_generation_for_recommendation_systems/">Privacy-Preserving Synthetic Data Generation for Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>UPC-SDG 用贡献度感知选择+Gumbel-Softmax 可微生成，按用户隐私偏好(k,γ)替换原始交互，兼顾数据/物品双层隐私与效用。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Provider Fairness&quot;,&quot;Adversarial Debiasing&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/profairrec_provider_fairness_aware_news_recommendation/">ProFairRec: Provider Fairness-aware News Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出公平/偏置表征解耦、对抗学习与正交正则，在MIND上提升新闻提供者曝光公平且精度基本持平</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;供应商群体公平&quot;,&quot;Hellinger 距离&quot;,&quot;最大边际相关性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/regulating_group_exposure_for_item_providers_in_recommendation/">Regulating Group Exposure for Item Providers in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>SIGIR 2022 短文，将供应商按敏感属性分组，提出基于 Hellinger 距离与 MMR 的贪心重排序，使推荐曝光贴合平台指定目标分布，在几乎不损失 NDCG 的前提下显著提升少数群体曝光与列表级多样性/新颖性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;自校正损失&quot;,&quot;持续学习&quot;,&quot;CTR 预估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/reloop_a_self_correction_continual_learning_loop_for_recommender_systems/">ReLoop: A Self-Correction Continual Learning Loop for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 hinge 式自校正损失惩罚“比上一版模型预测更差”的样本，零推理开销地把增量重训练变成纠错闭环，公开集 AUC 最高 +4.69%，线上 CTR +1.46%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;参数高效提示微调 (Prompt Tuning)&quot;,&quot;对抗去偏 (Adversarial Debiasing)&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/selective_fairness_in_recommendation_via_prompts/">Selective Fairness in Recommendation via Prompts</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出选择性公平任务与 PFRec：冻结 SASRec、以属性专属软提示+Adapter 配合对抗训练，用极少参数支持 2^m 种敏感属性组合的去偏。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;自监督对比学习与交互去噪&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;记忆效应 (Memorization Effect)&quot;,&quot;元学习重加权&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/self_supervised_denoising/self_guided_learning_to_denoise_for_robust_recommendation/">Self-Guided Learning to Denoise for Robust Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>利用推荐模型早期“抗噪期”的记忆效应收集干净样本，以元学习方式学出自适应重加权函数指导后续训练，模型无关地提升 Recall@20 最多 15%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;嵌入维度搜索&quot;,&quot;单次剪枝&quot;,&quot;混合维度嵌入&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/ctr_feature_interaction/single_shot_embedding_dimension_search_in_recommender_system/">Single-shot Embedding Dimension Search in Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>SSEDS 用一次前向-反向的梯度显著性打分完成字段级嵌入维度剪枝，90% 参数下 AUC 反升，并在微信订阅号 A/B 提升约 4% CTR。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;对抗训练&quot;,&quot;梯度反转层&quot;,&quot;变分自编码器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/unlearning_protected_user_attributes_in_recommendations_with_adversarial_tr/">Unlearning Protected User Attributes in Recommendations with Adversarial Training</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在 MultVAE 潜在向量上接梯度反转对抗头，剥离性别信息；攻击者探针 BAcc 从 0.71 降至 0.57，NDCG 仅微降且与末轮基线无显著差异</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排/推理阶段&quot;,&quot;反事实推断&quot;,&quot;过滤气泡&quot;,&quot;用户可控推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/user_controllable_recommendation_against_filter_bubbles/">User-controllable Recommendation Against Filter Bubbles</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出用户可控推荐原型 UCRS 与因果增强的推理期框架 UCI，用反事实推断扣除过时用户 ID 表征效应，并支持四类控制指令，在保持精度的同时显著降低隔离度与多数类目支配。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;替代商品推荐&quot;,&quot;弱监督学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/collaborative_filtering/a_transformer_based_substitute_recommendation_model_incorporating_weakly_su/">A Transformer-Based Substitute Recommendation Model Incorporating Weakly Supervised Customer Behavior Data</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以商品标题对+XLMR交互匹配替代关系，PR对数回归与负采样去噪，11市场单模型A/B收入+19%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;离线强化学习&quot;,&quot;马太效应&quot;,&quot;熵惩罚&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/rl_bandit_rec/alleviating_matthew_effect_of_offline_reinforcement_learning_in_interactive/">Alleviating Matthew Effect of Offline Reinforcement Learning in Interactive Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>指出离线 RL 的保守性惩罚会放大马太效应，提出熵惩罚项 DORL，在 KuaiRec/KuaiRand 交互环境中累计收益提升 79.3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;预训练与提示调优&quot;,&quot;后门攻击&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/attacking_pre_trained_recommendation/">Attacking Pre-trained Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示预训练序列推荐模型提供者可植入后门，提出基础替换与提示增强攻击，在 Leave-One-Out 协议下将目标物品曝光率提升数百倍且保持隐蔽。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;连续时间点过程&quot;,&quot;个性化位置去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/coupa_an_industrial_recommender_system_for_online_to_offline_service_platfo/">COUPA: An Industrial Recommender System for Online to Offline Service Platforms</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用连续时间点过程+个性化位置选择器联合建模 O2O 场景的时间周期性与位置偏置，Alipay 线上 CTR 提升 2.47%–5.63%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;点积模型嵌入维度&quot;,&quot;流行度偏差与物品公平性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/collaborative_filtering/curse_of_low_dimensionality_in_recommender_systems/">Curse of \&quot;Low\&quot; Dimensionality in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>论证点积模型低维嵌入是可表示排序数指数级塌缩的根源，实证其加剧流行度偏差、损害多样性与公平性，并给出上界 $n^{2d}$ 与锥体刻画。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;AAAI&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;生成式端到端&quot;,&quot;Diffusion Recommender&quot;,&quot;Popularity Bias&quot;,&quot;Optimal Transport&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/fairdiff_mitigating_the_self_reinforcing_matthew_effect_in_diffusion_recomm/">FairDiff: Mitigating the Self-Reinforcing Matthew Effect in Diffusion Recommender Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示扩散推荐模型优化偏置+先验失配叠加的自强化马太效应，用推理期流行度条件引导与最优传输语义校准即插即用地同时提升精度与长尾公平。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;纵向联邦学习&quot;,&quot;条件扩散模型生成嵌入&quot;,&quot;标签推断攻击防御&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/ctr_feature_interaction/fedads_a_benchmark_for_privacy_preserving_cvr_estimation_with_vertical_fede/">FedAds: A Benchmark for Privacy-Preserving CVR Estimation with Vertical Federated Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个基于阿里真实广告日志的纵向联邦 CVR 基准，含 1130 万样本数据集、扩散式未对齐样本合成 (Diffu-AT) 与梯度 mixup+投影隐私防御 (MixPro) 的系统评测。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;统计显著性检验&quot;,&quot;Copula 模拟&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/inference_at_scale_significance_testing_for_large_search_and_recommendation/">Inference at Scale: Significance Testing for Large Search and Recommendation Experiments</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Copula 模拟实证 5 种成对显著性检验：大样本下 Sign/Wilcoxon 一类错误率严重膨胀，功效全部饱和至 100%，应改用效应量判据。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/候选重排&quot;,&quot;数据集与评测基准&quot;,&quot;个性化商品搜索&quot;,&quot;真实查询与冷启动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/jdsearch_a_personalized_product_search_dataset_with_real_queries_and_full_i/">JDsearch: A Personalized Product Search Dataset with Real Queries and Full Interactions</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>发布京东 JDsearch 数据集：真实 query、不裁剪冷尾商品、200 候选内精排评估，TEM 较 QEM MRR 提升 25.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;物品侧公平曝光&quot;,&quot;生存分析&quot;,&quot;时效分布建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/measuring_item_global_residual_value_for_fair_recommendation/">Measuring Item Global Residual Value for Fair Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用生存分析建模物品级时效分布 GRV，以模型无关的加权融合实现新老物品公平曝光，MIND 上 NeuMF 的 HR@5 提升 131%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;稳定学习&quot;,&quot;HSIC 去相关&quot;,&quot;样本重加权&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/multimodal_rec/mining_stable_preferences_adaptive_modality_decorrelation_for_multimedia_re/">Mining Stable Preferences: Adaptive Modality Decorrelation for Multimedia Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 HSIC 度量模态相关度并做样本重加权，把多模态推荐从虚假偏好拉到稳定偏好，四骨干四数据集 IID 平均涨 3–22%、OOD 涨 2–48%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与POI本地生活推荐&quot;,&quot;端侧推理/召回&quot;,&quot;去中心化协同学习&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏互信息最大化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/model_agnostic_decentralized_collaborative_learning_for_on_device_poi_recom/">Model-Agnostic Decentralized Collaborative Learning for On-Device POI Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>MAC 允许端侧 POI 推荐模型结构异构，用参考数据软决策+互信息蒸馏做去中心化协同，采样邻居降噪，1/6 模型体积下 HR@10 超 DCLR 3.8%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;排序/精排&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;IPS 逆倾向得分估计&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/popularity_debiasing_from_exposure_to_interaction_in_collaborative_filterin/">Popularity Debiasing from Exposure to Interaction in Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 IPL 准则——物品交互量应正比于喜欢它的用户数，用 IPS 估计并构造 std 正则，去偏同时反涨推荐精度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;不变学习&quot;,&quot;表征解耦&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/ctr_feature_interaction/reformulating_ctr_prediction_learning_invariant_feature_interactions_for_re/">Reformulating CTR Prediction: Learning Invariant Feature Interactions for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>按时间切分环境、解耦环境不变/特异嵌入做不变学习，剔除不稳定特征交叉；ML-10M/Douban 上 AUC 相对 FM 提升 1.63%/0.18%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;策略学习/端到端排序&quot;,&quot;真实工业数据集&quot;,&quot;离线策略评估&quot;,&quot;Slate 推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/rl_bandit_rec/rl4rs_a_real_world_dataset_for_reinforcement_learning_based_recommender_sys/">RL4RS: A Real-World Dataset for Reinforcement Learning based Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>发布首个开源真实工业级 RL 推荐数据集（Slate/SeqSlate），配套四层评估框架与数据理解工具；PPO 线上 GMV 较 DIEN 提升 23.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;全链路离线评估&quot;,&quot;全局时间线&quot;,&quot;数据泄漏&quot;,&quot;评测协议&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/take_a_fresh_look_at_recommender_systems_from_an_evaluation_standpoint/">Take a Fresh Look at Recommender Systems from an Evaluation Standpoint</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>指出随机/留一划分缺失全局时间线导致 Popularity 基线病态与未来数据泄漏，提出 Recent Popularity 与时间窗评测方案。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;投毒攻击&quot;,&quot;反事实解释&quot;,&quot;神经逻辑推理&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/the_dark_side_of_explanations_poisoning_recommender_systems_with_counterfac/">The Dark Side of Explanations: Poisoning Recommender Systems with Counterfactual Examples</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次揭示反事实解释 API 的可被投毒风险：用 CF 训练 Horn 子句代理模型并反向优化生成虚假用户画像，Yelp/ML-100K 上 HR@10 最高相对提升 45%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端篮子生成&quot;,&quot;Next Basket Recommendation&quot;,&quot;重复-探索解耦&quot;,&quot;评测范式批判&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/sequential_rec/are_we_really_achieving_better_beyond_accuracy_performance_in_next_basket_r/">Are We Really Achieving Better Beyond-Accuracy Performance in Next Basket Recommendation?</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出TREx解耦重复/探索商品，用简单重复模块保精度、探索模块提升公平/多样性，并批判现有beyond-accuracy评测可被“走捷径”。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;超图卷积&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;位置编码解耦&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/cadrec_contextualized_and_debiased_recommender_model/">CaDRec: Contextualized and Debiased Recommender Model</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以超图卷积融合序列自注意力抑制过平滑，并用位置编码流行度与个体偏差双路解耦及梯度加权重整实现去偏，全库排序下 R@20 相对提升 4.2%–4.8%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;离线评估与度量基准&quot;,&quot;联合公平-相关性度量&quot;,&quot;Kendall τ 一致性&quot;,&quot;排序敏感度探针&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/can_we_trust_recommender_system_fairness_evaluation_the_role_of_fairness_an/">Can We Trust Recommender System Fairness Evaluation? The Role of Fairness and Relevance</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次系统体检 9 个「相关性+公平」联合度量，用 4 数据集 × 4 推荐器 × 4 类探针揭示其互相矛盾、位置不敏感与分数压缩三大病灶。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端生成式&quot;,&quot;长期留存优化&quot;,&quot;离线仿真评测基准&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/rl_bandit_rec/easyrl4rec_an_easy_to_use_library_for_reinforcement_learning_based_recommen/">EasyRL4Rec: An Easy-to-use Library for Reinforcement Learning Based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建五个真实数据集上的轻量 RL 推荐环境与统一长期收益评测管线（FreeB/NX_0_/NX_10_），系统复现 15+ 策略并揭示离散动作、On-Policy、偏好高估等关键结论。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;纵向联邦学习&quot;,&quot;表征迁移与知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;未对齐数据利用&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/ctr_feature_interaction/fedud_exploiting_unaligned_data_for_cross_platform_federated_click_through_/">FedUD: Exploiting Unaligned Data for Cross-Platform Federated Click-Through Rate Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用知识蒸馏把对齐样本上学到的 guest 表征迁移到 host 未对齐样本，让纵向联邦 CTR 的未对齐流量也能受益，AUC 提升约 0.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;长尾罕见病公平性&quot;,&quot;自监督预训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/leave_no_patient_behind_enhancing_medication_recommendation_for_rare_diseas/">Leave No Patient Behind: Enhancing Medication Recommendation for Rare Disease Patients</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>RAREMed 用统一疾病-手术序列 Transformer + 序列匹配/自重建双自监督预训练，显著抬升罕见病患者的用药推荐精度并压缩群体间公平性方差。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;嵌入演化监控&quot;,&quot;流行度偏置&quot;,&quot;实时训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/monitoring_the_evolution_of_behavioural_embeddings_in_social_media_recommen/">Monitoring the Evolution of Behavioural Embeddings in Social Media Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在 1.8 亿用户短视频生产系统上对比批量与实时物品嵌入的演化，用成熟度、学习量与 ℓ2 范数指标量化实时更新收敛快 5 倍且显著缓解流行度偏置。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;端到端训练&quot;,&quot;模型投毒攻击&quot;,&quot;用户级梯度裁剪&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/poisoning_decentralized_collaborative_recommender_system_and_its_countermea/">Poisoning Decentralized Collaborative Recommender System and Its Countermeasures</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出面向去中心化协同推荐的 PAMN 自适应投毒与 UCSU 用户级裁剪防御，在 ML/Amazon 上大幅提升目标物品曝光且保持 HR。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2024&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;Uplift建模&quot;,&quot;因果去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2024/rl_bandit_rec/treatment_effect_estimation_for_user_interest_exploration_on_recommender_sy/">Treatment Effect Estimation for User Interest Exploration on Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将Top-N类目曝光比视为多元连续处理，用IPW去偏ADRF/MTEF估计与动态规划优化兴趣探索，RCT评测R@10最高相对提升17.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;可复现性研究&quot;,&quot;意图感知推荐&quot;,&quot;基线调优&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/a_worrying_reproducibility_study_of_intent_aware_recommendation_models/">A Worrying Reproducibility Study of Intent-Aware Recommendation Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>复现 5 个意图感知推荐模型，发现 2 个不可复现、全部被调优后的传统非神经基线超越，揭示方法学顽疾。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/精排前的域分组阶段&quot;,&quot;域聚类&quot;,&quot;因果发现&quot;,&quot;迁移增益&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/cross_domain_multi_scenario/cdc_causal_domain_clustering_for_multi_domain_recommendation/">CDC: Causal Domain Clustering for Multi-Domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用孤立/混合双亲和矩阵+因果发现系数自适应融合，联合优化目标域簇与训练源域集，50+域上DomainAUC提升4.14‰/6.76‰，线上eCPM+4.9%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果推断&quot;,&quot;干预分布&quot;,&quot;处理效应&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/sequential_rec/csrec_rethinking_sequential_recommendation_from_a_causal_perspective/">CSRec: Rethinking Sequential Recommendation from A Causal Perspective</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将序列推荐重构为因果干预下的决策接受概率建模，用递归约束隔离系统影响，AHR@0.2 提升约 1.5–3 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端策略学习&quot;,&quot;离线强化学习&quot;,&quot;动态奖励塑形&quot;,&quot;双智能体&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/rl_bandit_rec/darlr_dual_agent_offline_reinforcement_learning_for_recommender_systems_wit/">DARLR: Dual-Agent Offline Reinforcement Learning for Recommender Systems with Dynamic Reward</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>双智能体离线 RL 用选择器聚合相似用户动态重塑奖励与不确定性惩罚，四数据集累积奖励全面超越 ROLeR/DORL。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;数据预处理与基准构建&quot;,&quot;可复现性&quot;,&quot;数据划分&quot;,&quot;跨框架互操作&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/datarec_a_python_library_for_standardized_and_reproducible_data_management_/">DataRec: A Python Library for Standardized and Reproducible Data Management in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>基于 55 篇顶会论文调研构建的统一数据管理库，提供可复现的过滤/划分/版本校验与跨框架导出，覆盖 18 个内置数据集。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;数据投毒攻击&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;,&quot;双提升攻击目标&quot;,&quot;多样性重排生成&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/diversity_aware_dual_promotion_poisoning_attack_on_sequential_recommendatio/">Diversity-aware Dual-promotion Poisoning Attack on Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示攻击与推荐目标梯度冲突，提出双提升MSE攻击目标与多样性重排序列生成，在保推荐精度的同时显著提升目标物品曝光。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;数据集记忆&quot;,&quot;流行度偏置&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/llm_rec/do_llms_memorize_recommendation_datasets_a_preliminary_study_on_movielens_1/">Do LLMs Memorize Recommendation Datasets? A Preliminary Study on MovieLens-1M</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用少样本提示探测 GPT/Llama 对 MovieLens-1M 的物品、用户与交互记忆覆盖率，揭示记忆量与推荐性能强相关且放大流行度偏置。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;新物品公平性&quot;,&quot;DQN 奖励设计&quot;,&quot;动态推荐系统&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/rl_bandit_rec/enhancing_new_item_fairness_in_dynamic_recommender_systems/">Enhancing New-item Fairness in Dynamic Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 DQN 智能体 + 三重奖励（探索/公平/准确）在动态推荐系统中提升新物品曝光公平性，KuaiRec 上 TGF 降 86%+ 且 HR 反升 39%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;来源偏见 (Source Bias)&quot;,&quot;反馈回路 (Feedback Loop)&quot;,&quot;AIGC 内容生态&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/exploring_the_escalation_of_source_bias_in_user_data_and_recommender_system/">Exploring the Escalation of Source Bias in User, Data, and Recommender System Feedback Loop</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 AIGC 在用户-数据-系统反馈回路中被逐步放大的来源偏见，并提出基于 L1 损失的黑盒去偏方法维持内容生态平衡。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;离线评测基准&quot;,&quot;池化评测&quot;,&quot;流行度偏置&quot;,&quot;偏好型 qrels&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/extending_movielens_32m_to_provide_new_evaluation_objectives/">Extending MovieLens-32M to Provide New Evaluation Objectives</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 TREC 池化法为 ML-32M 构建 51 用户偏好型 qrels，揭示传统 train/test 评测的流行度偏置并给出新评测目标。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;无ID推荐&quot;,&quot;文本投毒攻击&quot;,&quot;多智能体协作&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/id_free_not_risk_free_llm_powered_agents_unveil_risks_in_id_free_recommende/">ID-Free Not Risk-Free: LLM-Powered Agents Unveil Risks in ID-Free Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示无ID推荐器可被LLM多智能体改写文本攻击，提出TextSimu攻击与RewriteDetection检测，Hit@50最高提升数倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Decision Transformer&quot;,&quot;反事实数据增广&quot;,&quot;曝光偏差&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/sequential_rec/improving_sequential_recommenders_through_counterfactual_augmentation_of_sy/">Improving Sequential Recommenders through Counterfactual Augmentation of System Exposure</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Decision Transformer 建模全量曝光序列，并以随机/自改进反事实增广+用户模拟器挖掘未曝光兴趣，KuaiRand 上 Recall@20 相对提升约 87%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Truth Decay&quot;,&quot;LLM 生成假新闻&quot;,&quot;新闻推荐可信性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/llm_generated_fake_news_induces_truth_decay_in_news_ecosystem_a_case_study_/">LLM-Generated Fake News Induces Truth Decay in News Ecosystem: A Case Study on Neural News Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建约 7.5 万条人机混合新闻推荐数据集，模拟 LLM 生成假新闻在候选、历史、训练集三阶段渗透，揭示真实新闻排序优势被侵蚀的 Truth Decay 现象并以困惑度给出解释。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;机器遗忘&quot;,&quot;图推荐&quot;,&quot;影响编码器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/pre_training_for_recommendation_unlearning/">Pre-training for Recommendation Unlearning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出预训练式影响编码器 UnlearnRec，将遗忘请求映射为嵌入漂移，免重训实现高效图推荐遗忘，10x 加速且保持精度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;离线评估&quot;,&quot;用户模拟&quot;,&quot;大五人格推断&quot;,&quot;合成数据保真度&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/agent_conversational_rec/pub_an_llm_enhanced_personality_driven_user_behaviour_simulator_for_recomme/">PUB: An LLM-Enhanced Personality-Driven User Behaviour Simulator for Recommender System Evaluation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用大五人格驱动 LLM Agent 生成合成交互日志，Jaccard 相似度 0.31 逼近真实序列，并复现 7 种推荐算法的 nDCG 排序趋势。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;眼动追踪数据集&quot;,&quot;轮播界面位置偏置&quot;,&quot;点击模型&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/recgaze_the_first_eye_tracking_and_user_interaction_dataset_for_carousel_in/">RecGaze: The First Eye Tracking and User Interaction Dataset for Carousel Interfaces</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个轮播界面眼动+点击+光标+选择解释数据集，87用户3477次交互，揭示F型浏览与位置偏置。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信审计&quot;,&quot;平台推荐审计&quot;,&quot;算法审计可复现性&quot;,&quot;Sockpuppeting 仿真&quot;,&quot;个性化因子&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/revisiting_algorithmic_audits_of_tiktok_poor_reproducibility_and_short_term/">Revisiting Algorithmic Audits of TikTok: Poor Reproducibility and Short-term Validity of Findings</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>复现 TikTok 推荐审计发现可复现性极差、结论仅短期有效，观看行为才是最强个性化信号，且结论高度依赖度量选择。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐评估&quot;,&quot;幻觉与事实性&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-a-Judge&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/toward_holistic_evaluation_of_recommender_systems_powered_by_generative_mod/">Toward Holistic Evaluation of Recommender Systems Powered by Generative Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 Gen-RecSys 评估挑战二分法与 WH-六问 + 三维目标(D1–D5)整体评估框架，用场景化案例揭示传统 NDCG 无法覆盖的幻觉、偏见与政策合规风险。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;公平采样&quot;,&quot;倾向分免估计&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/unbiased_collaborative_filtering_with_fair_sampling/">Unbiased Collaborative Filtering with Fair Sampling</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>通过对称补充采样使每个用户/物品等概率成为正负样本，无需估计倾向分即可消除流行度偏置，全库排序下 Recall/NDCG 与 ARP 双优。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;线性自编码器&quot;,&quot;数据自适应归一化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/why_is_normalization_necessary_for_linear_recommenders/">Why is Normalization Necessary for Linear Recommenders?</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>理论剖析线性自编码器归一化对流行度/邻域偏差的作用，提出数据自适应归一化 DAN，六数据集全库排序下长尾最高提升 128.57%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;可复现性评测&quot;,&quot;重复捷径去重&quot;,&quot;用户效用指标&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/agent_conversational_rec/a_standardized_re_evaluation_of_conversational_recommender_systems_on_the_r/">A Standardized Re-evaluation of Conversational Recommender Systems on the ReDial Dataset</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>统一复现 7 个 CRS 模型，揭示 Recall@1 复现粒度鸿沟、约 50% 精度来自重复捷径、增益多源于 LLM 骨干而非架构创新。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;扩散推荐&quot;,&quot;自引导&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/adaptive_autoguidance_for_item_side_fairness_in_diffusion_recommender_syste/">Adaptive Autoguidance for Item-Side Fairness in Diffusion Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可学习逐步引导权重替代固定自引导，配合流行度正则，在扩散推荐中实现物品侧公平与精度近无损的平衡。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;离线评估&quot;,&quot;逆倾向得分&quot;,&quot;控制变量&quot;,&quot;方差缩减&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/additive_control_variates_dominate_self_normalisation_in_off_policy_evaluat/">Additive Control Variates Dominate Self-Normalisation in Off-Policy Evaluation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>证明最优加性基线 β*-IPS 在 MSE 上渐近严格优于 SNIPS，并给出精确方差差解析式与排序场景 β*⊥⊥-IPM 扩展。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;显式稀疏&quot;,&quot;多视图特征过滤&quot;,&quot;迭代竞争稀疏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/ctr_feature_interaction/beyond_dense_connectivity_explicit_sparsity_for_scalable_recommendation/">Beyond Dense Connectivity: Explicit Sparsity for Scalable Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示稠密MLP隐式权重稀疏现象，提出 filter-then-fuse 多视图显式稀疏框架 SSR，在十亿级工业数据与三个公开集上以更低算力超越 RankMixer。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;对抗分布对齐&quot;,&quot;语义离散化去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/agent_conversational_rec/bridging_passive_and_active_enhancing_conversation_starter_recommendation_v/">Bridging Passive and Active: Enhancing Conversation Starter Recommendation via Active Expression Modeling</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用对抗分布对齐+RQ-VAE语义离散化把用户主动查询引入对话开场白推荐，打破曝光-点击回音室，线上活跃天数+0.04%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;折扣率时序建模&quot;,&quot;傅里叶时频分解&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/ctr_feature_interaction/cheaper_is_better_a_discount_aware_network_for_conversion_rate_prediction_i/">Cheaper is Better: A Discount-Aware Network for Conversion Rate Prediction in E-commerce Recommendation System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 FFT 时频分解 + 用户/上下文双重去偏 + 折扣回归辅助任务建模商品折扣率时序，离线 AUC +1.61%、线上 pCVR +3.63%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;多视角效用对齐&quot;,&quot;逆优化偏好推断&quot;,&quot;金融对话推荐基准&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/agent_conversational_rec/conv_finre_a_conversational_and_longitudinal_benchmark_for_utility_grounded/">Conv-FinRe: A Conversational and Longitudinal Benchmark for Utility-Grounded Financial Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用逆优化从纵向决策轨迹反推用户风险偏好，构建四视图多视角金融推荐基准，揭示 LLM 效用对齐与行为模仿的根本张力。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;延迟反馈建模&quot;,&quot;反事实因果推断&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/ctr_feature_interaction/counterfactual_multi_task_learning_for_delayed_conversion_modeling_in_e_com/">Counterfactual Multi-task Learning for Delayed Conversion Modeling in E-commerce Sales Pre-Promotion</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向大促预热期“集中式延迟转化”的因果多任务 CVR 模型，联合直接/延迟转化建模、个性化门控迁移与 ATC 反事实正则，离线 AUC 显著超越延迟反馈基线并落地线上 A/B。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双塔模型&quot;,&quot;在线学习&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/collaborative_filtering/cs3_efficient_online_capability_synergy_for_two_tower_recommendation/">CS3: Efficient Online Capability Synergy for Two-Tower Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以塔内自校正、跨塔EMA同步与级联模型共享三机制增强双塔召回，离线三数据集AUC一致提升，线上广告收入+8.36%且QPS仅降0.59%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;提供者公平&quot;,&quot;异质效用建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/equity_vs_equality_optimizing_ranking_fairness_for_tailored_provider_needs/">Equity vs. Equality: Optimizing Ranking Fairness for Tailored Provider Needs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出以增益为基的公平框架与 EquityRank 梯度重排算法，按提供者异质偏好分配曝光与销量收益，离线/在线仿真均取得更优效果-公平权衡。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Machine Unlearning&quot;,&quot;Benchmark&quot;,&quot;Sequential Unlearning&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/erase_a_real_world_aligned_benchmark_for_unlearning_in_recommender_systems/">ERASE -- A Real-World Aligned Benchmark for Unlearning in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向真实场景的推荐系统机器遗忘基准，覆盖 CF/SBR/NBR 三任务、七种遗忘算法、九数据集九模型，揭示近似遗忘的鲁棒性与效率短板。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;联邦学习&quot;,&quot;残差向量量化&quot;,&quot;多市场CTR&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/cross_domain_multi_scenario/fedmm_federated_collaborative_signal_quantization_for_multi_market_ctr_pred/">FedMM: Federated Collaborative Signal Quantization for Multi-Market CTR Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用双层 RQ-VAE 码本作联邦传输介质，对齐跨市场不相交 ID 空间，AUC 全面超越本地/联邦基线且通信量降 20×。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;延迟反馈&quot;,&quot;反馈轨迹&quot;,&quot;后验校准&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/ctr_feature_interaction/follow_the_trace_exploiting_post_click_trajectories_for_online_delayed_conv/">Follow the TRACE: Exploiting Post-Click Trajectories for Online Delayed Conversion Rate Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将点击后行为演化建模为反馈轨迹，用贝叶斯分解+可靠性门控回溯补全动态修正未转化样本后验，Criteo AUC 0.8382 超越 DEFUSE。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排/评估&quot;,&quot;眼动追踪&quot;,&quot;点击模型&quot;,&quot;位置偏置&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/following_the_eye_tracking_evidence_established_web_search_assumptions_fail/">Following the Eye-Tracking Evidence: Established Web-Search Assumptions Fail in Carousel Interfaces</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 RecGaze 眼动数据系统证伪 F 型注意力、层级点击与标题导航等四条 Web 搜索假设，提出轮播界面特有的 L 型注视模式。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;反事实解释&quot;,&quot;可复现性基准&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/from_top_1_to_top_k_a_reproducibility_study_and_benchmarking_of_counterfact/">From Top-1 to Top-K: A Reproducibility Study and Benchmarking of Counterfactual Explanations for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>统一复现11种反事实解释器，首次提出列表级Top-K评估协议与PN-R位置敏感指标，揭示显式格式下有效性-稀疏性权衡与图解释器可扩展性瓶颈。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;直播推荐数据集&quot;,&quot;长尾公平性&quot;,&quot;多行为建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/kuailive_a_real_time_interactive_dataset_for_live_streaming_recommendation/">KuaiLive: A Real-time Interactive Dataset for Live Streaming Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个含直播间生命周期与多行为实时交互的快手直播推荐数据集，构建 Top-K/CTR 基准并揭示主播长尾公平性挑战。</p>
</dd>
