<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;正交约束&quot;,&quot;嵌入坍缩&quot;,&quot;稀疏缩放&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/collaborative_filtering/ocp_orthogonal_constrained_projection_for_sparse_scaling_in_industrial_comm/">OCP: Orthogonal Constrained Projection for Sparse Scaling in Industrial Commodity Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在 Item-ID 投影层上施加 Stiefel 流形正交约束，抑制稀疏缩放下的嵌入坍缩，京东线上 UCXR +12.97%、GMV +8.95%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;数据预处理/评估协议&quot;,&quot;Sub-Sequence Splitting&quot;,&quot;可复现性研究&quot;,&quot;数据增强&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/sequential_rec/pay_attention_to_sequence_split_uncovering_the_impacts_of_sub_sequence_spli/">Pay Attention to Sequence Split: Uncovering the Impacts of Sub-Sequence Splitting on Sequential Recommendation Models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示子序列切分(SSS)在数据读取阶段被隐性使用导致模型评估失真，系统分析SSS生效条件与数据分布重塑机理。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;提供方曝光公平&quot;,&quot;分层KL分解&quot;,&quot;可微排序网络&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/post_hoc_provider_fairness_adaptation_via_hierarchical_exposure_alignment/">Post-hoc Provider Fairness Adaptation via Hierarchical Exposure Alignment</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>冻结基座+轻量公平适配器，用分层曝光对齐(HEFA)与可微NDCG联合优化，实现即插即用的提供方曝光公平。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;数据投毒攻击&quot;,&quot;提示蒸馏&quot;,&quot;序列推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/llm_rec/prompt_unknown_promotion_attacks_against_llm_based_sequential_recommender_s/">Prompt-Unknown Promotion Attacks against LLM-based Sequential Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在系统提示词与受害模型均不可见的全黑盒设定下，用进化式提示蒸馏+代理模型+文本与行为双投毒实现长尾商品推广攻击，Beauty 上 HR@10 提升约 3.9 倍。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;粗排/库存预估&quot;,&quot;单调回归&quot;,&quot;预算-效果曲线&quot;,&quot;Reach &amp; Frequency&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/ctr_feature_interaction/r_f_inventory_a_large_scale_dataset_for_monotonic_inventory_estimation_in_r/">R&amp;F-Inventory: A Large-Scale Dataset for Monotonic Inventory Estimation in Reach and Frequency Advertising</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个公开的 R&amp;F 合约库存预估数据集，以「定向-排期-频控」为上下文提供完整预算-效果曲线，定义单点预测与多维单调曲线重建两大基准任务。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;全局时间线&quot;,&quot;滑动窗口评估&quot;,&quot;增量学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/recnexteval_a_reference_implementation_for_temporal_next_batch_recommendati/">RecNextEval: A Reference Implementation for Temporal Next-Batch Recommendation Evaluation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出滑动时间窗 next-batch 评估框架，强制全局时间线防泄漏并支持增量学习，含 Python 库与 Web UI。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Request-Only Optimization&quot;,&quot;HSTU&quot;,&quot;特征去重&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/generative_rec/request_only_optimization_for_recommendation_systems/">Request-Only Optimization for Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以用户请求为训练样本单元，在数据源头去重用户特征，实现训练吞吐 2-6 倍提升并支撑 HSTU 生成式推荐规模化落地。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;GPU 向量检索&quot;,&quot;Bloom Index 特征过滤&quot;,&quot;模型化服务架构&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/collaborative_filtering/silvertorch_a_unified_model_based_system_to_democratize_large_scale_recomme/">SilverTorch: A Unified Model-based System to Democratize Large-Scale Recommendation on GPUs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 ANN 检索与特征过滤下沉为 PyTorch 模型层，GPU Bloom Index 与 Int8 融合 ANN 协同设计，吞吐提升 23.7×、成本效率提升 13.35×。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;推理时知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;投机预计算&quot;,&quot;工业级全链路架构&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/solaris_speculative_offloading_of_latent_based_representation_for_inference/">SOLARIS: Speculative Offloading of Latent-bAsed Representation for Inference Scaling</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>借鉴投机解码思想，异步预计算基础模型用户-物品隐表征并分层补全覆盖，实现推理时知识蒸馏，Meta 广告线上收入 +0.67%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测协议与数据审计&quot;,&quot;数据划分&quot;,&quot;时间泄漏&quot;,&quot;冷启动暴露&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/splitlight_an_exploratory_toolkit_for_recommender_systems_datasets_and_spli/">SplitLight: An Exploratory Toolkit for Recommender Systems Datasets and Splits</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>开源数据集与划分审计工具箱，量化时间泄漏、冷启动暴露、重复消费与分布漂移，揭示预处理决策可使 NDCG@10 变动 1.2%–60%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;公平重排&quot;,&quot;流形优化&quot;,&quot;注意力经济&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/the_attention_market_interpreting_online_fair_re_ranking_as_manifold_optimi/">The Attention Market: Interpreting Online Fair Re-ranking as Manifold Optimization under Walrasian Equilibrium</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将在线公平重排建模为注意力市场瓦尔拉斯均衡，用供需双侧梯度校正对齐排序流形，7 数据集 42 设置几乎全胜。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;多智能体LLM&quot;,&quot;反事实解释&quot;,&quot;可持续旅游推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/agent_conversational_rec/trace_a_conversational_framework_for_sustainable_tourism_recommendation_wit/">TRACE: A Conversational Framework for Sustainable Tourism Recommendation with Agentic Counterfactual Explanations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>多智能体 LLM 框架以澄清提问+反事实解释实现可持续旅游推荐的非强制绿色引导，24 人用户研究验证。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;后验价值对齐&quot;,&quot;线性 Boosting 归因&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/multi_task_reranking/uniboost_global_coordination_with_value_alignment_for_fair_and_efficient_tr/">Uniboost: Global Coordination with Value Alignment for Fair and Efficient Traffic Allocation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用后验价值对齐把抽象混合分映射到有效完播率语义空间，再以独立线性 boosting 解耦多业务加权，线上 A/B 全指标正向。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双塔检索&quot;,&quot;相关性蒸馏&quot;,&quot;难负样本挖掘&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/collaborative_filtering/unified_supervision_for_walmart_s_sponsored_search_retrieval_via_joint_sema/">Unified Supervision for Walmart&#x27;s Sponsored Search Retrieval via Joint Semantic Relevance and Behavioral Engagement Modeling</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以语义相关性为主监督、互动为相关项内偏好信号，融合交叉编码器分级标签、多通道检索先验与去偏互动构建统一双塔训练目标，离线 P@25 +10.5%、NDCG@25 +5.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;在线A/B实验评估&quot;,&quot;后分层&quot;,&quot;CUPED&quot;,&quot;重尾指标&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/variance_reduction_for_heavy_tailed_monetization_metrics_in_ranking_experim/">Variance Reduction for Heavy-Tailed Monetization Metrics in Ranking Experiments via Post-Stratification</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Research and Development in Information Retrieval">SIGIR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用后分层+CUPED压制重尾GMV方差，等效置信度下节省约45%流量，已在ShareChat 40+线上实验落地。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR-AP 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;对抗训练&quot;,&quot;特征公平&quot;,&quot;自适应扰动&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR-AP/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/automatic_feature_fairness_in_recommendation_via_adversaries/">Automatic Feature Fairness in Recommendation via Adversaries</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Information Retrieval in the Asia Pacific">SIGIR-AP</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出以特征域为粒度的公平性目标，用自适应对抗扰动按「组合多样性」定扰动强度、按「特征频率」定对抗权重，在 FM 主干上同时提升准确率与组间公平性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR-AP 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;逆倾向加权&quot;,&quot;线性自编码器&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR-AP/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/accurate_and_diverse_recommendations_via_propensity_weighted_linear_autoenc/">Accurate and Diverse Recommendations via Propensity-Weighted Linear Autoencoders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Information Retrieval in the Asia Pacific">SIGIR-AP</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用对数 Sigmoid 重定义 IPS 倾向分，避免幂律对热门物品过度惩罚，在 EASE 类线性自编码器上以闭式解实现覆盖率大幅提升且精度不降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR-AP 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;用户模拟&quot;,&quot;效用指标&quot;,&quot;奖励成本框架&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR-AP/2025/agent_conversational_rec/limitations_of_current_evaluation_practices_for_conversational_recommender_/">Limitations of Current Evaluation Practices for Conversational Recommender Systems and the Potential of User Simulation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Information Retrieval in the Asia Pacific">SIGIR-AP</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>批判静态数据集与 Recall@N 评估失真，提出奖励/成本效用框架与 RDL 指标，并用 LLM 用户模拟器替代真人评估。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;SIGIR-AP 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;展示偏差&quot;,&quot;合成前缀数据增广&quot;,&quot;神经 LTR&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/SIGIR-AP/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/synthetic_prefixes_to_mitigate_bias_in_real_time_neural_query_autocomplete/">Synthetic Prefixes to Mitigate Bias in Real-Time Neural Query Autocomplete</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM SIGIR Conference on Information Retrieval in the Asia Pacific">SIGIR-AP</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用无 QAC 会话的完整查询合成前缀训练数据，缓解展示偏差，并以 O(n) 成对近似实现列表级损失，线上 MRR 提升 0.92%–1.49%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDD&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果推断去偏&quot;,&quot;社交网络混杂&quot;,&quot;逆倾向加权&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDD/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/be_causal_de_biasing_social_network_confounding_in_recommendation/">Be Causal: De-biasing Social Network Confounding in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Knowledge Discovery from Data">ACM TKDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 MNAR 选择偏差归因于社交网络混杂，用 node2vec 混杂表征 + 倾向暴露模型 + Wasserstein 平衡表征去混杂，在 Epinions/Ciao/ML-1M 上 MAE 相对最强基线降 27.7%/11.4%/19.1%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;FAccTRec@RecSys&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排/重排稳定性微调&quot;,&quot;Rank-Preserving Regularization&quot;,&quot;Training Data Perturbation&quot;,&quot;Rank List Stability (RBO)&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDD/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/finest_stabilizing_recommendations_by_rank_preserving_fine_tuning/">FINEST: Stabilizing Recommendations by Rank-Preserving Fine-Tuning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Knowledge Discovery from Data">ACM TKDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用伪扰动模拟+top-K 保序正则微调序列推荐器，CASPER 扰动下 RBO 最高 +24%、Jaccard +136% 且精度不降</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDD&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;数据异质性&quot;,&quot;双层聚类&quot;,&quot;IPS 去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDD/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/exploring_and_exploiting_data_heterogeneity_in_recommendation/">Exploring and Exploiting Data Heterogeneity in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Knowledge Discovery from Data">ACM TKDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出双层聚类 BHE 分层挖掘推荐数据预测机制与协变量分布异质性，用于多子模型泛化与 IPS 去偏，Yelp/MovieLens 上 NDCG 提升 20%+。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDD&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;扩散推荐&quot;,&quot;可复现评测基准&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDD/2026/generative_rec/eval4direc_a_unified_and_systematic_evaluation_framework_for_diffusion_base/">Eval4DiRec: A Unified and Systematic Evaluation Framework for Diffusion-based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Knowledge Discovery from Data">ACM TKDD</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个面向扩散推荐的开源统一评测框架，覆盖 5 场景 14 模型，标准化扩散推理配置并揭示噪声调度与步数的关键影响。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;数据预处理/召回前净化&quot;,&quot;职业恶意用户检测&quot;,&quot;注意力度量学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/detect_professional_malicious_user_with_metric_learning_in_recommender_syst/">Detect Professional Malicious User with Metric Learning in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 HDAN 情感分与 LFM 评分构造“情感落差”画像筛候选，再用注意力度量学习聚类识别职业恶意用户，F-score 最高 0.987。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果推断&quot;,&quot;反事实去偏&quot;,&quot;知识图谱推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2023/graph_rec/causal_inference_for_knowledge_graph_based_recommendation/">Causal Inference for Knowledge Graph based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用因果图拆解 KG 推荐中结构混淆与属性捷径偏差，以 do 干预+反事实推断实现去偏属性偏好建模，Full-Ranking 下 Recall@20 提升 3.79%~6.66%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回/评分预测&quot;,&quot;本地差分隐私&quot;,&quot;矩阵分解&quot;,&quot;高斯混合模型&quot;,&quot;有界Laplace&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/private_and_utility_enhanced_recommendations_with_local_differential_privac/">Private and Utility Enhanced Recommendations with Local Differential Privacy and Gaussian Mixture Model</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出BLP本地扰动与MoG-MF噪声建模，在LDP下用10折CV、RMSE/F-score验证精度提升与单轮通信优势。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果蒸馏&quot;,&quot;前门调整&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/causal_distillation_for_alleviating_performance_heterogeneity_in_recommende/">Causal Distillation for Alleviating Performance Heterogeneity in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用前门调整+异构多教师蒸馏近似因果效应，在采样排序下提升尾部用户并降低性能异质性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;本地差分隐私&quot;,&quot;属性推断攻击&quot;,&quot;自适应隐私预算&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/comprehensive_privacy_analysis_on_federated_recommender_system_against_attr/">Comprehensive Privacy Analysis on Federated Recommender System against Attribute Inference Attacks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>先用梯度式属性推断攻击逐组件量化 FedRec 泄漏差异，再提出按脆弱度分配隐私预算的自适应 LDP 机制 APM，在几乎不损 Hit@20 的前提下显著降低属性推断 F1。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;可微架构搜索&quot;,&quot;嵌入维度搜索&quot;,&quot;特征选择&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2024/ctr_feature_interaction/i_razor_a_differentiable_neural_input_razor_for_feature_selection_and_dimen/">i-Razor: A Differentiable Neural Input Razor for Feature Selection and Dimension Search in DNN-Based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用带 0 维吸收算子的可微算子选择把字段选择与嵌入维度搜索合并为一次性联合优化，CPT 剪枝导出配置后重训，AUC 提升且参数量降 35%+。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;前门调整 (Front-door Adjustment)&quot;,&quot;隐混淆因子 (Hidden Confounder)&quot;,&quot;多任务因果推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/mitigating_hidden_confounding_effects_for_causal_recommendation/">Mitigating Hidden Confounding Effects for Causal Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用前门调整将物品特征对点赞的因果效应拆成两个可识别部分效应，Tiktok/Kwai/Taobao 全库排序 N@50 最高提升 19.4%，低活跃用户增益尤大。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;多任务学习与列表级重排&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;结果多样性&quot;,&quot;MMR与DPP&quot;,&quot;曝光公平&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2024/multi_task_reranking/result_diversification_in_search_and_recommendation_a_survey/">Result Diversification in Search and Recommendation: A Survey</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次统一搜索与推荐的结果多样性分类体系：概念层区分个体级/系统级与外在/内在多样性，度量层分无关/含相关性两族，方法层按干预时机分预/中/后处理与在线 Bandit/RL。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;语义评估&quot;,&quot;话题标签嵌入&quot;,&quot;同义词库&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/reval_a_semantic_evaluation_framework_for_hashtag_recommendation/">#REVAL: a semantic evaluation framework for hashtag recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出语义评估框架 #REval，用 BERTag 话题标签嵌入构建同义词库，以 #REval-hit-ratio 替代精确匹配，修正话题标签推荐评估的系统性低估。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与POI推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;POI推荐综述&quot;,&quot;联邦学习&quot;,&quot;隐私安全&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/a_survey_on_point_of_interest_recommendation_models_architectures_and_secur/">A Survey on Point-of-Interest Recommendation: Models, Architectures, and Security</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个贯通模型演进、架构部署与安全隐私三维度的POI推荐综述，覆盖LDA到LLM/DM、集中式到联邦式及投毒/同态加密防护。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;测试时训练&quot;,&quot;OOD 推荐&quot;,&quot;自蒸馏聚类&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/dual_test_time_training_for_out_of_distribution_recommender_system/">Dual Test-time Training for Out-of-distribution Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个将测试时训练引入 OOD 推荐，用自蒸馏+对比双任务在测试阶段适配用户/物品特征漂移，全库排序下 R@50 最高提升 11.56%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Machine Unlearning&quot;,&quot;LoRA Adapter Aggregation&quot;,&quot;隐私保护&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/llm_rec/exact_and_efficient_unlearning_for_large_language_model_based_recommendatio/">Exact and Efficient Unlearning for Large Language Model-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个面向 LLMRec 的精确遗忘框架 APA：语义分片训练子 LoRA、仅重训受影响分片，并以样本自适应参数级聚合实现单次推理，遗忘提速约 4 倍且 AUC 反超全量重训。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;,&quot;对抗数据增强&quot;,&quot;群体公平性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/fairdgcl_fairness_aware_recommendation_with_dynamic_graph_contrastive_learn/">FairDgcl: Fairness-aware Recommendation with Dynamic Graph Contrastive Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用对抗式视图生成器+判别器动态生成公平增强视图，在四个数据集上同时提升准确率与群体公平性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;双边公平&quot;,&quot;最小效用保证&quot;,&quot;二分搜索&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/fairsort_learning_to_fair_rank_for_personalized_recommendations_in_two_side/">FairSort: Learning to Fair Rank for Personalized Recommendations in Two-Sided Platforms</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将重排视为“跑道”而非背包，用公平提升速度+二分搜索保证用户最低效用，实现双边公平与个性化推荐的三方权衡。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端联邦训练&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;个性化联邦学习 PFL&quot;,&quot;双层优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/personalized_recommendation_models_in_federated_settings_a_survey/">Personalized Recommendation Models in Federated Settings: A Survey</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首篇以“个性化模型”为核心的 FedRecSys 综述，给出双层优化定义并系统梳理四类挑战与解法。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;托攻击&quot;,&quot;鲁棒推荐&quot;,&quot;对抗防御&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/towards_robust_recommendation_a_review_and_an_adversarial_robustness_evalua/">Towards Robust Recommendation: A Review and an Adversarial Robustness Evaluation Library</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统综述推荐系统对抗/非对抗鲁棒性，提出 ShillingREC 评测库统一攻击与防御模型评估。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;倾向分校准&quot;,&quot;逆倾向加权去偏&quot;,&quot;不确定性校准&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2025/ctr_feature_interaction/uncertainty_calibration_for_counterfactual_propensity_estimation_in_recomme/">Uncertainty Calibration for Counterfactual Propensity Estimation in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示倾向分估计严重失准问题，提出以ECE为质量度量的模型无关校准框架，理论证明校准倾向分降低IPS偏差，在Coat/Yahoo/KuaiRand上显著提升CVR去偏效果。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TKDE&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;感知差距指数&quot;,&quot;谱图理论&quot;,&quot;连边推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TKDE/2026/social_group_rec/quantifying_and_minimizing_perception_gap_in_social_networks/">Quantifying and Minimizing Perception Gap in Social Networks</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Knowledge and Data Engineering">IEEE TKDE</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出连续意见下的感知差距指数 PGI，用谱图理论刻画网络连通性与社群结构对局部-全局认知偏差的影响，并证明连边推荐最小化 PGI 的不可近似性，给出近最优贪心启发式。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TMM&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦学习&quot;,&quot;本地差分隐私原型&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TMM/2025/cross_domain_multi_scenario/federated_user_preference_modeling_for_privacy_preserving_cross_domain_reco/">Federated User Preference Modeling for Privacy-Preserving Cross-Domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：IEEE Transactions on Multimedia">IEEE TMM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用评论+潜在正物品挖掘综合偏好，再以 LDP 原型经联邦聚合跨域迁移，四组 CDR 任务 HR@10 平均提升约 13%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;曝光公平&quot;,&quot;最大流&quot;,&quot;多边公平&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/a_graph_based_approach_for_mitigating_multi_sided_exposure_bias_in_recommen/">A Graph-based Approach for Mitigating Multi-sided Exposure Bias in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>FairMatch 以二部图最大流重排联合提升物品/供应商曝光公平与聚合多样性，在 Last.fm/MovieLens 上保持可接受精度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;离线评估协议&quot;,&quot;全局时间线&quot;,&quot;数据泄漏&quot;,&quot;非采样排序&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/a_critical_study_on_data_leakage_in_recommender_system_offline_evaluation/">A Critical Study on Data Leakage in Recommender System Offline Evaluation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>TOIS 2023｜去偏公平/工业评测｜系统揭示 leave-last-one-out 忽略全局时间线造成未来交互泄漏，量化四模型四数据集上精度与排序失稳，并提出 timeline 增量评测方案。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回与推荐评估&quot;,&quot;下一购物篮推荐&quot;,&quot;重复-探索分解&quot;,&quot;可复现评估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/sequential_rec/a_next_basket_recommendation_reality_check/">A Next Basket Recommendation Reality Check</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>复现三类NBR方法，提出重复/探索指标与GP-TopFreq基线，三数据集全物品Top-K显示深度模型无一致优势。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;全链路(数据/排序/重排)&quot;,&quot;双边公平&quot;,&quot;摊销公平&quot;,&quot;校准公平&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/a_survey_on_the_fairness_of_recommender_systems/">A Survey on the Fairness of Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首篇系统综述：以“过程/结果×群体/个体×七概念”定义体系、三视角分类与数据-排序-重排方法学，统一 60+ 篇公平推荐文献并指明双边联合公平等空白。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果后门调整&quot;,&quot;混合专家 MoE&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/addressing_confounding_feature_issue_for_causal_recommendation/">Addressing Confounding Feature Issue for Causal Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用后门调整 + MoE 专家架构重估 P(Y|U,do(X))，消除视频时长等混杂特征对交互的虚假直接影响，在 Kwai/Wechat 上相对最优基线平均提升 26.9%/5.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果推断去偏&quot;,&quot;倾向性得分 IPS&quot;,&quot;反馈回路&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/bias_and_debias_in_recommender_system_a_survey_and_future_directions/">Bias and Debias in Recommender System: A Survey and Future Directions</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以反馈回路为骨架统一 7 类推荐偏差的因果定义，给出 IPS/插补/因果图等去偏方法分类学与开放挑战。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/模型训练&quot;,&quot;随机数据集&quot;,&quot;上界最小化&quot;,&quot;系统诱导偏差&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/bounding_system_induced_biases_in_recommender_systems_with_a_randomized_dat/">Bounding System-Induced Biases in Recommender Systems with A Randomized Dataset</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>TOIS 2023 去偏推荐：以随机策略完整反馈定义理想损失，推导三角不等式与可分性泛化上界，提出 DUB 直接优化可解上界项；在 Yahoo! R3 与 Product 上 AUC 稳定超越强基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果推断去偏&quot;,&quot;混淆变量平衡&quot;,&quot;对抗学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/debiased_recommendation_with_user_feature_balancing/">Debiased Recommendation with User Feature Balancing</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以用户特征投影+IPM分布平衡替代IPS实现因果去偏，配合裁剪/采样/对抗三种平衡项策略与潜混淆建模，在6个数据集上NDCG@10最高提升6.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;学习哈希&quot;,&quot;离散优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/lightfr_lightweight_federated_recommendation_with_privacy_preserving_matrix/">LightFR: Lightweight Federated Recommendation with Privacy-preserving Matrix Factorization</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用学习哈希把联邦 MF 的用户/物品表征压成二值码，配联邦离散坐标下降与梯度聚合，精度追平联邦 SOTA，存储与推理开销降约一个量级。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信隐私&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;隐私推断攻击&quot;,&quot;曝光数据保护&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/on_the_user_behavior_leakage_from_recommender_system_exposure/">On the User Behavior Leakage from Recommender System Exposure</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示系统曝光序列可反推用户历史点击的隐私漏洞，用编码器-解码器黑盒攻击在 Zhihu/MIND 上实现最高 87.72% Recall@10，并提出两阶段曝光替换保护机制。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;工业架构与评测基准&quot;,&quot;隐私计算&quot;,&quot;数据披露机制&quot;,&quot;多智能体模拟&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/studying_the_impact_of_data_disclosure_mechanism_in_recommender_systems_via/">Studying the Impact of Data Disclosure Mechanism in Recommender Systems via Simulation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出隐私感知推荐框架，以隐私计算建模用户细粒度数据披露决策，并用多智能体 bandit 模拟证明细粒度机制优于“全有或全无”。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/Top-K 排序&quot;,&quot;视觉感知推荐系统投毒&quot;,&quot;引导扩散模型&quot;,&quot;对抗样本不可感知性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/adversarial_item_promotion_on_visually_aware_recommender_systems_by_guided_/">Adversarial Item Promotion on Visually-Aware Recommender Systems by Guided Diffusion</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用引导扩散模型把对抗扰动注入高斯噪声，生成高保真对抗商品图以提升长尾商品曝光，全库排序下 ER@5 最高 +56.8%、FID 降 87.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;视频长度去偏&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;长度条件采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/alleviating_video_length_effect_for_micro_video_recommendation/">Alleviating Video-Length Effect for Micro-video Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出VLDRec，用长度分组、进度标签与长度条件采样多任务去偏视频时长效应，在快手/微信固定总时长评估上@120提升1.81%/11.32%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;全库排序/端到端协同过滤&quot;,&quot;因果解耦&quot;,&quot;偏好漂移&quot;,&quot;时序VAE&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/causal_disentangled_recommendation_against_user_preference_shifts/">Causal Disentangled Recommendation Against User Preference Shifts</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出CDR，以时序VAE推断未观测因素并建模多环境偏好漂移，用可学习稀疏矩阵解耦类别偏好；全库排序下R@10较次优提升19.5%–28.1%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回/精排&quot;,&quot;因果推断&quot;,&quot;反事实数据增广&quot;,&quot;后门调整与 IPW/DR&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/causal_inference_in_recommender_systems_a_survey_and_future_directions/">Causal Inference in Recommender Systems: A Survey and Future Directions</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以数据偏差、数据缺失/噪声、超越精度三类病理为主轴，系统梳理 40 篇工作中后门调整、IPW/DR、反事实增广与因果发现四类工具的用法、代价与开放问题</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;交互式策略/重排&quot;,&quot;离线强化学习&quot;,&quot;因果推断&quot;,&quot;过滤气泡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/rl_bandit_rec/cirs_bursting_filter_bubbles_by_counterfactual_interactive_recommender_syst/">CIRS: Bursting Filter Bubbles by Counterfactual Interactive Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将因果推断嵌入离线RL，用曝光核生成反事实满意度奖励，在KuaiEnv全观测环境击破过滤气泡并提升长期累积满意度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;事后解释与公平性诊断&quot;,&quot;反事实解释&quot;,&quot;离线强化学习&quot;,&quot;异构图属性归因&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/counterfactual_explanation_for_fairness_in_recommendation/">Counterfactual Explanation for Fairness in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用异构图属性节点做反事实解释，以离线强化学习搜索最小属性集解释曝光不公平，擦除评估下 NDCG 与 Gini 双优。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐端到端训练&quot;,&quot;图像投毒攻击&quot;,&quot;扩散模型净化与检测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/manipulating_visually_aware_federated_recommender_systems_and_its_counterme/">Manipulating Visually-aware Federated Recommender Systems and Its Countermeasures</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示视觉联邦推荐中模型投毒失效、图像投毒生效的新后门，并提出扩散模型引导净化+免训练检测的 GDMPD 防御，ER@5 由 1.0 压至 0.0。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;因果/信息论&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/robust_collaborative_filtering_to_popularity_distribution_shift/">Robust Collaborative Filtering to Popularity Distribution Shift</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>PopGo 用同构 shortcut model 量化交互级流行度捷径并以掩码预测去偏，在 OOD/ID 全库排序上同时提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS 2024&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/重排评估&quot;,&quot;会话多样性&quot;,&quot;准确率-多样性关系&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2024/sequential_rec/understanding_diversity_in_session_based_recommendation/">Understanding Diversity in Session-Based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>统一16个会话推荐基线的准确率/多样性评测，揭示非多样化模型亦可高多样性，且准确率-多样性关系随模型与数据变化。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;嵌入表压缩&quot;,&quot;轻量化推荐基准&quot;,&quot;端侧部署&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/a_thorough_performance_benchmarking_on_lightweight_embedding_based_recommen/">A Thorough Performance Benchmarking on Lightweight Embedding-based Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>统一协议横评 7 类轻量嵌入压缩方法，提出 MagPrune 强基线，并首次量化跨任务迁移性与树莓派端侧效率瓶颈。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果去混杂&quot;,&quot;混杂因子解耦&quot;,&quot;双目标跨域推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/cross_domain_multi_scenario/causal_deconfounding_via_confounder_disentanglement_for_dual_target_cross_d/">Causal Deconfounding via Confounder Disentanglement for Dual-Target Cross-Domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>显式解耦单域/跨域观测混杂因子，经后门调整保留正向效应并消除负向偏差，七数据集 HR@10 平均提升 6.17%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;因果推断&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;公平表征学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/causality_inspired_fair_representation_learning_for_multimodal_recommendati/">Causality-Inspired Fair Representation Learning for Multimodal Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>因果启发的模态解耦与公平/不公平关系挖掘，实现多模态推荐的反事实公平，公平性显著优于 SOTA 且精度可比。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦学习&quot;,&quot;语义聚类&quot;,&quot;FFT 频域滤波&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/cross_domain_multi_scenario/federated_semantic_learning_for_privacy_preserving_cross_domain_recommendat/">Federated Semantic Learning for Privacy-preserving Cross-domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 FFMSR，用多层 PLM+MoE 提取语义、服务器端聚类融合跨域语义、FFT 滤波去噪，在完全非重叠联邦场景下 Full-Ranking 提升 1.4%–5.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦序列推荐&quot;,&quot;dX-privacy 序列扰动&quot;,&quot;对比学习知识迁移&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/llm_rec/fellas_enhancing_federated_sequential_recommendation_with_llm_as_external_s/">FELLAS: Enhancing Federated Sequential Recommendation with LLM as External Services</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LLM 作为外部服务为联邦序列推荐提供物品级与序列级表征，配合 dX-privacy 扰动与对比学习，在 Full-Ranking 下显著超越 FedSeqRec 并逼近集中式训练。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;知识利用率&quot;,&quot;鲁棒性评估&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/graph_rec/kg4receval_does_knowledge_graph_really_matter_for_recommender_systems/">KG4RecEval: Does Knowledge Graph Really Matter for Recommender Systems?</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 KGER 指标与 KG4RecEval 框架，系统扰动/删除 KG 知识，发现 KG 对推荐精度贡献常为负或近零，挑战 KG 必有益的主流认知。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;反事实推断&quot;,&quot;维度坍缩&quot;,&quot;谱编码器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/llm_rec/mitigating_propensity_bias_of_large_language_models_for_recommender_systems/">Mitigating Propensity Bias of Large Language Models for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用谱条件 iVAE 编码 LLM 侧信息避免维度坍缩，并以反事实推断消除 LLM 倾向性偏差，在 6 个 CF 骨干上全库排序一致提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多智能体强化学习&quot;,&quot;黑盒对抗攻击&quot;,&quot;社交推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/multi_agent_attacks_for_black_box_social_recommendations/">Multi-agent Attacks for Black-box Social Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用多智能体强化学习协同生成冷启动物品画像与跨社区社交画像，对黑盒社交推荐发起无目标投毒/规避攻击，全库排序下 NDCG@10 相对下降约 19%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;后处理/模型修正&quot;,&quot;属性遗忘&quot;,&quot;分布距离MMD&quot;,&quot;函数空间正则&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/post_training_attribute_unlearning_in_recommender_systems/">Post-Training Attribute Unlearning in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出锚点分布MMD与函数空间排序正则的PoT-AU双损失，多类属性遗忘复杂度降至O(T)且推荐性能近无损。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;隐私保护&quot;,&quot;序列混淆&quot;,&quot;自监督修改器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/sequential_rec/privacy_preserving_sequential_recommendation_with_collaborative_confusion/">Privacy-Preserving Sequential Recommendation with Collaborative Confusion</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用相似序列引导的逐项修改器在推荐前混淆交互序列，66% 修改率下推荐精度仍超 99% SOTA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;联邦学习&quot;,&quot;指数机制&quot;,&quot;对比去噪&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2025/sequential_rec/ptf_fsr_a_parameter_transmission_free_federated_sequential_recommender_syst/">PTF-FSR: A Parameter Transmission-Free Federated Sequential Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用指数机制生成扰动序列替代参数传输，实现模型隐私与轻量通信的联邦序列推荐框架，通信降至 KB 级。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果发现&quot;,&quot;分布外泛化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2026/cross_domain_multi_scenario/causal_invariant_cross_domain_out_of_distribution_recommendation/">Causal-Invariant Cross-Domain Out-of-Distribution Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>统一建模跨域与单域双重分布偏移，用双层因果结构与 LLM 引导混杂因子发现实现跨域 OOD 推荐，HR@10 平均提升 6.34%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TOIS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;逆倾向加权&quot;,&quot;列表级学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TOIS/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/mitigating_popularity_bias_in_recommendation_with_global_listwise_learning_/">Mitigating Popularity Bias in Recommendation with Global Listwise Learning and Progressive Bi-Weighting</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Information Systems">ACM TOIS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 IPS 加权多项似然实现列表级全局无偏学习，并以 Bi-Weighting 平滑倾向估计与渐进式去偏，六数据集 Recall@20 平均提升 9.43%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;全链路(召回/排序/重排)&quot;,&quot;高阶连通性&quot;,&quot;综述与分类体系&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2023/graph_rec/a_survey_of_graph_neural_networks_for_recommender_systems_challenges_method/">A Survey of Graph Neural Networks for Recommender Systems: Challenges, Methods, and Directions</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出阶段/场景/目标/应用四维分类法，围绕图构建、传播聚合、优化与效率四大挑战系统梳理 GNN 推荐，并开源论文-代码索引库</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果去偏&quot;,&quot;前门调整&quot;,&quot;未观测混杂&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/deconfounded_causal_collaborative_filtering/">Deconfounded Causal Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以客观物品特征为前门中介，统一去除未观测混杂，并在 Amazon/Yelp 的 SKEW/RAND 采样排序上显著提升 nDCG@5。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;单位一致性&quot;,&quot;张量补全&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2023/collaborative_filtering/tensor_completion_with_provable_consistency_and_fairness_guarantees_for_rec/">Tensor Completion with Provable Consistency and Fairness Guarantees for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以单位一致性约束替代秩/范数优化，实现可证明唯一、共识排序与尺度公平的矩阵/张量补全；MovieLens-1M 扰动测试零违例。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排/曝光多样性&quot;,&quot;纵向用户研究&quot;,&quot;曝光多样性&quot;,&quot;隐式联想测试&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/assessing_the_impact_of_music_recommendation_diversity_on_listeners_a_longi/">Assessing the Impact of Music Recommendation Diversity on Listeners: A Longitudinal Study</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>12 周 110 人纵向对照实验，用 SC-IAT 隐式联想与 Guttman 开放性量表量化高/低多样性音乐推荐对听众态度的长期塑造效应。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;价值敏感设计&quot;,&quot;多利益相关者优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/building_human_values_into_recommender_systems_an_interdisciplinary_synthes/">Building Human Values into Recommender Systems: An Interdisciplinary Synthesis</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>22位跨学科作者综合：价值→指标→排序干预的七步落地流程、三类度量数据源与多利益相关者权衡防护，属立场综述而非实验论文。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;离线评估&quot;,&quot;分布性评估&quot;,&quot;曝光公平性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/distributionally_informed_recommender_system_evaluation/">Distributionally-Informed Recommender System Evaluation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出从点估计均值转向分布性评估，区分样本/子群/利益相关者三类分布与五类不确定性来源，并在MovieLens案例中揭示均值掩盖的性别公平与曝光公平差异。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;离线评估与模型验证&quot;,&quot;邻域切片评估&quot;,&quot;子群公平性&quot;,&quot;Welch t 检验&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/group_validation_in_recommender_systems_framework_for_multi_layer_performan/">Group Validation in Recommender Systems: Framework for Multi-layer Performance Evaluation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 PCC-KNN 将评分数据切成可重叠邻域，以损失差+Welch 单侧 t 检验筛出关键邻域，暴露被全局均值掩盖的小用户群失效。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;在线探索与推荐策略&quot;,&quot;Incentive-Aware Bandit&quot;,&quot;Randomized Pooling&quot;,&quot;Ex-Post Fairness&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2024/rl_bandit_rec/incentive_aware_recommender_systems_in_two_sided_markets/">Incentive-Aware Recommender Systems in Two-Sided Markets</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出激励兼容的随机池化 Bandit 推荐，已知/未知机会成本下分别用 ARP/MARP 兼顾探索、公平与次线性 regret。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回+精排+重排&quot;,&quot;联邦学习&quot;,&quot;神经网络压缩&quot;,&quot;隐私保护&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/a_privacy_preserving_system_for_movie_recommendations_using_federated_learn/">A Privacy Preserving System for Movie Recommendations Using Federated Learning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>端到端联邦电影推荐系统，提出 FedQ 队列式联邦训练缓解非独立同分布与小样本问题，结合 NNC 压缩降低通信开销，在 MovieLens 25M 上验证 16 万客户端规模。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;全链路（召回/排序/解释）&quot;,&quot;可信推荐 TRS&quot;,&quot;跨视角权衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/a_survey_on_trustworthy_recommender_systems/">A Survey on Trustworthy Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统梳理可解释、公平、隐私、鲁棒、可控五大可信推荐维度及其交叉关系，提出跨视角关系矩阵与评估局限反思。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;社会选择&quot;,&quot;多智能体公平&quot;,&quot;动态重排&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/dynamic_fairness_aware_recommendation_through_multi_agent_social_choice/">Dynamic Fairness-aware Recommendation through Multi-agent Social Choice</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多利益相关方公平推荐建模为「分配+聚合」两阶段社会选择问题，SCRUF-D 框架动态调度多个公平性智能体，在真实与合成数据上实现多维权衡。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;数据投毒攻击&quot;,&quot;矩阵分解&quot;,&quot;用户簇定向攻击&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/evaluating_impact_of_user_cluster_targeted_attacks_in_matrix_factorisation_/">Evaluating Impact of User-Cluster Targeted Attacks in Matrix Factorisation Recommenders</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统分析 MF 推荐中面向用户簇的投毒攻击，揭示 V 矩阵是攻击传播主通道，簇内真实评分量决定脆弱性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测与度量&quot;,&quot;单品公平&quot;,&quot;曝光公平&quot;,&quot;度量归一化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/evaluation_measures_of_individual_item_fairness_for_recommender_systems_a_c/">Evaluation Measures of Individual Item Fairness for Recommender Systems: A Critical Study</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统批判 8 种单品曝光公平度量，修正 5 类理论缺陷并给出归一化方案与选用指南。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/重排/端到端&quot;,&quot;曝光感知推荐&quot;,&quot;系统性文献综述&quot;,&quot;曝光偏差&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/impression_aware_recommender_systems/">Impression-Aware Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首篇曝光感知推荐系统(IARS)系统性综述，提出统一理论框架与三维分类体系，厘清模型、数据集与评估协议。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;模型更新/遗忘&quot;,&quot;影响函数&quot;,&quot;推荐遗忘&quot;,&quot;图卷积溢出影响&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/recommendation_unlearning_via_influence_function/">Recommendation Unlearning via Influence Function</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用影响函数一步更新模型实现推荐遗忘，联合直接损失影响与图卷积溢出影响，剪枝加速后较重训提速 250 倍以上。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;权重共享NAS&quot;,&quot;超网路径采样&quot;,&quot;软硬件协同设计&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2025/ctr_feature_interaction/towards_automated_model_design_on_recommender_systems/">Towards Automated Model Design on Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用权重共享超网 NAS 在稠密连接+异构算子+维度+量化空间上自动搜索 CTR 模型，Criteo/KDD 降 0.001~0.003 LogLoss，FLOPs 降 8.5×。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;双边匹配推荐&quot;,&quot;嫉妒自由&quot;,&quot;Nash社会福利&quot;,&quot;Sinkhorn&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/balancing_fairness_and_high_match_rates_in_reciprocal_recommender_systems_a/">Balancing Fairness and High Match Rates in Reciprocal Recommender Systems: A Nash Social Welfare Approach</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将双边匹配推荐建模为推荐机会分配，用 Nash 社会福利交替优化实现近无嫉妒的公平推荐，并以 Sinkhorn 加速至大规模场景。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS 2026&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;多智能体协作&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2026/agent_conversational_rec/collab_rec_an_llm_based_agentic_framework_for_balancing_recommendations_in_/">Collab-Rec: An LLM-based Agentic Framework for Balancing Recommendations in Tourism</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>三角色 LLM 智能体 + 确定性 Moderator 多轮约束精炼，在 900 条旅游查询上兼顾约束满足与长尾多样性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;可复现性研究&quot;,&quot;扩散模型推荐&quot;,&quot;弱基线问题&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2026/generative_rec/diffusion_recommender_models_and_the_illusion_of_progress_a_concerning_stud/">Diffusion Recommender Models and the Illusion of Progress: A Concerning Study of Reproducibility and a Conceptual Mismatch</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="生成式推荐与离散语义 ID">GenRec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>复现 9 个 SIGIR&#x27;23-24 扩散推荐模型，仅 25% 结果可复现，调优简单基线全面反超，并揭示 DDPM 与 top-n 推荐任务的概念错配。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;音乐推荐评估&quot;,&quot;LLM-as-Judge&quot;,&quot;提示工程&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2026/llm_rec/music_recommendation_with_large_language_models_challenges_opportunities_an/">Music Recommendation with Large Language Models: Challenges, Opportunities, and Evaluation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统梳理 LLM 驱动音乐推荐在用户建模、物品建模与自然语言请求三方面的变革，并提出 NLP 启发的评估分类体系与 MRS 成功/风险维度框架。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;时空轨迹与POI推荐&quot;,&quot;全链路(数据/算法/评估)&quot;,&quot;立场与综述论文&quot;,&quot;多利益相关方推荐&quot;,&quot;评估协议批判&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2026/poi_spatiotemporal_rec/point_of_interest_recommendation_pitfalls_and_viable_solutions/">Point of Interest Recommendation: Pitfalls and Viable Solutions</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统梳理POI推荐在数据集、算法与评估三维度的20项陷阱，并提出六方向研究议程与陷阱-方案映射表</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准与元研究&quot;,&quot;可复现性&quot;,&quot;实验方法论&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/reproducibility_in_recommender_systems_a_survey/">Reproducibility in Recommender Systems: A Survey</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>对 RecSys 可复现性赛道 2020–2025 年 51 篇论文的结构化元分析，揭示其方法论高度同质化、以“扩展”替代“严格复现”的实践落差。</p>
</dd>
