<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;TORS&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;帕累托前沿&quot;,&quot;公平性-相关性联合评测&quot;,&quot;推荐系统评测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/TORS/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/robustness_analysis_of_pareto_based_joint_evaluation_of_fairness_and_releva/">Robustness Analysis of Pareto-based Joint Evaluation of Fairness and Relevance in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Transactions on Recommender Systems">ACM TORS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 DPFR：以测试集真实标签构建相关性-公平性帕累托前沿，用模型得分到前沿参考点的距离联合评估推荐系统，比现有联合度量更稳健。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排（包级列表组合枚举）&quot;,&quot;ZDD 零压缩决策图&quot;,&quot;固定参数可解性 (FPT)&quot;,&quot;群组公平 (Proportionality / Envy-freeness)&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/enumerating_fair_packages_for_group_recommendations/">Enumerating Fair Packages for Group Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 ZDD 压缩枚举全部满足比例公平/无嫉妒约束的包，证明群组规模参数下 FPT，$O(n(K+g)2^g)$内产出万亿级候选并支持交集/top-$K$/采样过滤。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;异构图表征学习&quot;,&quot;分布式训练系统&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2022/graph_rec/graph4rec_a_universal_toolkit_with_graph_neural_networks_for_recommender_sy/">Graph4Rec: A Universal Toolkit with Graph Neural Networks for Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 GNN4Rec 训练统一为五段式流水线，配分布式图引擎与参数服务器，在 ICF/UCF/U2I 召回协议下系统对比 8 类 GNN 并给出工程实践建议。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回/评测基准&quot;,&quot;数据采样与 Coreset&quot;,&quot;Kendall&#x27;s Tau 排名保持性&quot;,&quot;Propensity 去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2022/collaborative_filtering/on_sampling_collaborative_filtering_datasets/">On Sampling Collaborative Filtering Datasets</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>系统刻画 16 种 CF 数据采样策略对算法排名保持性的影响，提出 SVP-CF 代理采样与 Data-genie 采样器推荐器，仅用 10% 数据即可保持排名，带来 5.8× 训练加速。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;离线评测/鲁棒性评测&quot;,&quot;Robustness Evaluation&quot;,&quot;RecBole&quot;,&quot;Subpopulation Fairness&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/rgrecsys_a_toolkit_for_robustness_evaluation_of_recommender_systems/">RGRecSys: A Toolkit for Robustness Evaluation of Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出RGRecSys统一评估推荐模型在子群体、分布偏移、特征变换、攻击与稀疏下的鲁棒性，基于RecBole与MovieLens-100k做案例展示。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM&quot;,&quot;图神经网络推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;子模函数邻居选择&quot;,&quot;长尾损失重加权&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2023/graph_rec/dgrec_graph_neural_network_for_recommendation_with_diversified_embedding_ge/">DGRec: Graph Neural Network for Recommendation with Diversified Embedding Generation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用子模邻居选择、层注意力与长尾损失重加权在嵌入生成阶段直接做多样化，Recall 近似持平 LightGCN 而类目覆盖度显著领先。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2023&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;解耦表征&quot;,&quot;推荐多样性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2023/collaborative_filtering/disentangled_representation_for_diversified_recommendations/">Disentangled Representation for Diversified Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>DCRS 将用户偏好解耦为类别相关/无关分量，用对抗判别器约束，在 Full-Ranking 协议下兼顾准确率与类别多样性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;端到端训练(联邦)&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐遗忘&quot;,&quot;更新校准回滚&quot;,&quot;半难负采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/federated_unlearning_for_on_device_recommendation/">Federated Unlearning for On-Device Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用日志回滚+方向校准实现联邦推荐用户遗忘，配合重要性更新筛选与混合半难负采样，7× 加速且超越全量重训</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排/模型压缩&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;分组排序学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/unbiased_knowledge_distillation_for_recommendation/">Unbiased Knowledge Distillation for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示知识蒸馏的流行度偏差，提出按流行度分组的无偏蒸馏 UnKD；在三个数据集上提升 Recall@10 并显著改善非热门物品。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;无分布不确定性量化&quot;,&quot;分组阈值策略&quot;,&quot;逆倾向加权置信界&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/uncertainty_quantification_for_fairness_in_two_stage_recommender_systems/">Uncertainty Quantification for Fairness in Two-Stage Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用无分布有限样本置信界为两阶段推荐的召回层设计分组阈值策略，保证弱势群体候选集的相关物品数达标且候选规模近最优。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2024&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;特权特征蒸馏&quot;,&quot;列表级蒸馏损失&quot;,&quot;概率校准&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2024/ctr_feature_interaction/calibration_compatible_listwise_distillation_of_privileged_features_for_ctr/">Calibration-compatible Listwise Distillation of Privileged Features for CTR Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将特权特征蒸馏从 pointwise 升级为 listwise，提出校准兼容(calibration-compatible)的 CLID 损失，在 Web30K/Istella-S/阿里线上广告数据上同时提升 NDCG@10 与 GAUC 且不损 LogLoss/ECE。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Distributionally Robust Optimization&quot;,&quot;Exposure Bias&quot;,&quot;SNIPS&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/debiasing_sequential_recommenders_through_distributionally_robust_optimizat/">Debiasing Sequential Recommenders through Distributionally Robust Optimization over System Exposure</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以系统曝光模拟器构造 DRO 名义分布，叠加高曝光惩罚与 SNIPS 评估器，SNIPS 全库排序下 Recall@20 最高 +25.9%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;训练阶段防护&quot;,&quot;模型抽取攻击&quot;,&quot;可微交换矩阵&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/defense_against_model_extraction_attacks_on_recommender_systems/">Defense Against Model Extraction Attacks on Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个推荐模型抽取防御 GRO：以可微交换矩阵反传 top-k 梯度，用学生模型模拟攻击者并最大化其损失，在全库排序下压低代理模型效用且保持目标模型效用。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;稀疏训练&quot;,&quot;模型压缩&quot;,&quot;彩票假设&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/dynamic_sparse_learning_a_novel_paradigm_for_efficient_recommendation/">Dynamic Sparse Learning: A Novel Paradigm for Efficient Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>从零训练固定预算稀疏嵌入表，周期性剪枝/再生权重，训练与推理 MACs 同降 30%–75% 且性能持平。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐与内容理解&quot;,&quot;精排（训练标签生成层）&quot;,&quot;双层优化与元学习&quot;,&quot;时长去偏&quot;,&quot;可微 Top-k&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2024/multimodal_rec/labelcraft_empowering_short_video_recommendations_with_automated_label_craf/">LabelCraft: Empowering Short Video Recommendations with Automated Label Crafting</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将短视频标签生成建模为双层优化，用 MAML 式元学习以平台目标（观看时长/互动/留存）反传梯度训练可微标注模型，Kuaishou/Wechat 上 NWTG@10 较最优基线最高 +4.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2024&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;机器遗忘&quot;,&quot;会话分片聚类&quot;,&quot;隐私保护&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2024/sequential_rec/on_the_effectiveness_of_unlearning_in_session_based_recommendation/">On the Effectiveness of Unlearning in Session-Based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 SRU：相似会话聚片 + 会话-质心双源注意力聚合 + 协同/邻居/随机额外删除，并定义 HIT@K 遗忘有效性度量，遗忘重训提速 3.7× 且精度反超 SISA。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回/生成式表征&quot;,&quot;因果去偏&quot;,&quot;预训练跨域推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/pre_trained_recommender_systems_a_causal_debiasing_perspective/">Pre-trained Recommender Systems: A Causal Debiasing Perspective</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以层次贝叶斯因果去偏框架 PreRec 分离域内流行度偏差与跨域隐式偏差，实现跨市场/跨平台零样本与小样本推荐，Recall@K% 最高提升 87.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2025&quot;,&quot;对话推荐与智能体仿真&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;众包对战评测&quot;,&quot;Elo 评级&quot;,&quot;真实用户反馈&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2025/agent_conversational_rec/crs_arena_crowdsourced_benchmarking_of_conversational_recommender_systems/">CRS Arena: Crowdsourced Benchmarking of Conversational Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出基于成对对战与真实用户反馈的众包 CRS 评测平台，发布 474 段对话数据集与 Elo 榜单，揭示 R@10 与用户满意度负相关。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐聚合&quot;,&quot;组公平&quot;,&quot;正交聚合&quot;,&quot;安全聚合&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/privacy_preserving_orthogonal_aggregation_for_guaranteeing_gender_fairness_/">Privacy-Preserving Orthogonal Aggregation for Guaranteeing Gender Fairness in Federated Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用正交映射+安全聚合让各性别组独立聚合，兼顾隐私与组公平，ML-100K 整体 NDCG@10 提升 7.30%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2025&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;序列多样性&quot;,&quot;近似算法&quot;,&quot;有序哈密顿路径&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2025/multi_task_reranking/sequential_diversification_with_provable_guarantees/">Sequential Diversification with Provable Guarantees</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多样性从集合度量升级为序列期望度量，证明 MaxSSD 的 NP 难性并给出 BτI/SBτI 等常数近似算法，在 7 个数据集上稳定超越 EXPLORE/MMR/DPP。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WSDM 2026&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;方面级语义控制&quot;,&quot;幻觉度量&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WSDM/2026/llm_rec/hadsf_aspect_aware_semantic_control_for_explainable_recommendation/">HADSF: Aspect Aware Semantic Control for Explainable Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM International Conference on Web Search and Data Mining">WSDM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>两阶段受控方面词表+动态个性化抽取，提出ADR/OFR幻觉度量，揭示幻觉-性能非单调关系，小模型可媲美大模型</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;工具变量&quot;,&quot;因果解耦表征&quot;,&quot;搜索推荐联合建模&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/a_model_agnostic_causal_learning_framework_for_recommendation_using_search_/">A Model-Agnostic Causal Learning Framework for Recommendation using Search Data</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用搜索查询作工具变量，将推荐物品嵌入解耦为因果/非因果两部分再融合，DIN/NRHUB 上 AUC 稳定提升约 0.7%–1.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;端到端信息流分发（For You 召回+排序黑盒审计）&quot;,&quot;Sock-Puppet 算法审计&quot;,&quot;信息茧房与过滤气泡&quot;,&quot;Jaccard 信息流差异度量&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/an_empirical_investigation_of_personalization_factors_on_tiktok/">An Empirical Investigation of Personalization Factors on TikTok</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="对话推荐与智能体仿真">CRS</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用双生子傀儡账号审计 TikTok「For You」算法，量化关注/完播/点赞/语言/位置的个性化影响排序与噪声基线</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;AutoML 特征选择&quot;,&quot;Gumbel-Softmax&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/ctr_feature_interaction/autofield_automating_feature_selection_in_deep_recommender_systems/">AutoField: Automating Feature Selection in Deep Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可微控制器 + Gumbel-Softmax 双层优化自动筛选特征域，Avazu 砍掉 50% 域反而涨点并加速推理 4%–17%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;评测与行为测试&quot;,&quot;行为测试 Behavioral Testing&quot;,&quot;流行度偏差与长尾&quot;,&quot;开源评测框架&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/beyond_ndcg_behavioral_testing_of_recommender_systems_with_reclist/">Beyond NDCG: behavioral testing of recommender systems with RecList</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 NLP 行为测试迁移到推荐，用可插拔接口在隐空间做任务不变量、错误距离与切片偏差测试，揭示 HR@10 相近模型的巨大行为差异。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;因果结构学习&quot;,&quot;OOD 泛化&quot;,&quot;负采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/causpref_causal_preference_learning_for_out_of_distribution_recommendation/">CausPref: Causal Preference Learning for Out-of-Distribution Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可微 DAG 学习不变用户偏好并配套反偏好负采样，在 5 类分布偏移下显著超越 NeuMF/LightGCN/IPS/DICE。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/排序打分&quot;,&quot;交叉配对排序损失&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;动态难样本采样&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/cross_pairwise_ranking_for_unbiased_item_recommendation/">Cross Pairwise Ranking for Unbiased Item Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>交叉配对损失抵消用户/物品曝光倾向，实现样本级无偏学习，Recall@20 较 DICE 提升 9.2%–18.2%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;差分隐私矩阵发布&quot;,&quot;异构跨域对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/cross_domain_multi_scenario/differential_private_knowledge_transfer_for_privacy_preserving_cross_domain/">Differential Private Knowledge Transfer for Privacy-Preserving Cross-Domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>两阶段隐私保护跨域推荐：源域用 JLT/SJLT 差分隐私发布评分矩阵，目标域用异构 HeteroCDR 对齐用户表征；留一法+99负采样下 HR@5 相对 ETL 提升 9.1%~11.7%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2022&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;粗排/预拍卖&quot;,&quot;两阶段拍卖&quot;,&quot;Pre-Auction Score&quot;,&quot;GSP&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/ctr_feature_interaction/on_designing_a_two_stage_auction_for_online_advertising/">On Designing a Two-stage Auction for Online Advertising</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 PAS 预拍卖打分，建模广告进入精排 Top-K 的概率，替代粗排 $b\\times ctr$ 贪心选择，在 Amazon Books 与工业拍卖日志上提升社会福利率与收入。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2022&quot;,&quot;多任务学习与列表级重排&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;双边匹配市场&quot;,&quot;双随机排序&quot;,&quot;PBM&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/multi_task_reranking/optimizing_rankings_for_recommendation_in_matching_markets/">Optimizing Rankings for Recommendation in Matching Markets</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>双边匹配市场列表级联合排序，双随机策略优化社会福利，离线仿真匹配数显著提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;模型训练后遗忘（召回/排序通用）&quot;,&quot;Machine Unlearning&quot;,&quot;Balanced Sharding&quot;,&quot;Adaptive Aggregation&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/recommendation_unlearning/">Recommendation Unlearning</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 RecEraser：相似度平衡分片保留协同信息，注意力自适应聚合子模型；全库排序下高效精确遗忘且性能优于 SISA/GraphEraser。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2022&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回（W2W 推荐列表重连）&quot;,&quot;随机游走隔离度&quot;,&quot;图边重连&quot;,&quot;吸收马尔可夫链&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2022/debias_fairness_industrial/rewiring_what_to_watch_next_recommendations_to_reduce_radicalization_pathwa/">Rewiring What-to-Watch-Next Recommendations to Reduce Radicalization Pathways</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2022</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 W2W 推荐图建模为 d-正则有向图，用吸收随机游走定义节点隔离度并提出 NP-难的 k-Rewiring，基于 Sherman-Morrison 增量更新的贪心算法在 YouTube/NELA-GT 上将最坏隔离度降低 40–80%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;排序&quot;,&quot;反事实推断&quot;,&quot;因果去偏&quot;,&quot;会话协同过滤&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/sequential_rec/a_counterfactual_collaborative_session_based_recommender_system/">A Counterfactual Collaborative Session-based Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将会话外因素建模为混杂因子，以自监督伪标签预训练+反事实协同推理消除伪相关，三数据集 R@20 最高提升 21.8%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;逆概率加权 (IPS)&quot;,&quot;未观测混杂平衡权重&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/balancing_unobserved_confounding_with_a_few_unbiased_ratings_in_debiased_re/">Balancing Unobserved Confounding with a Few Unbiased Ratings in Debiased Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用少量无偏评分学习熵最大平衡权重，重加权 IPS/DR/AutoDebias 损失以对抗未观测混杂与模型误设，模型无关且即插即用，NDCG@5 最高相对提升 4.21%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果嵌入解耦&quot;,&quot;从众偏差&quot;,&quot;多任务排序&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/cam2_conformity_aware_multi_task_ranking_model_for_large_scale_recommender_/">CAM2: Conformity-Aware Multi-Task Ranking Model for Large-Scale Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用特征分区+任务无关辅助损失在十亿级视频排序中解耦“从众”与“真实兴趣”因果嵌入，离线 NE 降 0.139%，线上互动 +0.50%、DAU +0.21%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;CTR预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;置信度排序损失&quot;,&quot;在线学习与模型演进&quot;,&quot;知识蒸馏替代&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/ctr_feature_interaction/confidence_ranking_for_ctr_prediction/">Confidence Ranking for CTR Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用“超越线上部署模型”的点式/关系式排序代理损失替代交叉熵与蒸馏，JD 广告精排 A/B 平均 +1.75% CTR。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;从众权重解耦&quot;,&quot;去偏对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/sequential_rec/debiased_contrastive_learning_for_sequential_recommendation/">Debiased Contrastive Learning for Sequential Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 DCRec，用三通道从众权重网络解耦用户真实兴趣与流行度从众，并以加权 InfoNCE 实现去偏对比学习，四数据集 LOO 全库排序 HR@1 最高提升 10.29%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;因果解耦嵌入&quot;,&quot;对比学习样本增广&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/disentangled_causal_embedding_with_contrastive_learning_for_recommender_sys/">Disentangled Causal Embedding With Contrastive Learning For Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用流行度加权双通道对比学习把交互解耦为兴趣与从众两类因果嵌入，OOD 干预测试集 HR@20 较 MACR 提升 93%，快手线上 EVTR +7.36%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;自适应负采样&quot;,&quot;组公平性&quot;,&quot;双层优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/fairly_adaptive_negative_sampling_for_recommendations/">Fairly Adaptive Negative Sampling for Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次从负采样视角治理物品组公平：用组级 G-BCE 感知不公平、动量自适应调组采样分布并与重要性采样 Mixup，ML1M-2 上 Recall-Disp 降 90.97%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;DP-SGD&quot;,&quot;整数规划公平重排&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/fairness_aware_differentially_private_collaborative_filtering/">Fairness-aware Differentially Private Collaborative Filtering</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 DP-SGD 放大活跃/非活跃用户精度鸿沟（Matthew 效应），提出分离式用户-物品梯度扰动 + 整数规划公平重排的 DP-Fair 两阶段框架，在四项真实数据集上同时提升总效用并压降 F_U。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;数据增广&quot;,&quot;双层优化&quot;,&quot;群组公平性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/improving_recommendation_fairness_via_data_augmentation/">Improving Recommendation Fairness via Data Augmentation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 FDA 框架，用嵌入扰动生成跨组“伪交互”并以双层优化做数据增广，无需预定义公平指标即可将 DP/EO 不公平度降低 4%–12%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;联邦召回训练&quot;,&quot;成员推理攻击&quot;,&quot;本地差分隐私&quot;,&quot;参数容量再分配&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/interaction_level_membership_inference_attack_against_federated_recommender/">Interaction-level Membership Inference Attack Against Federated Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次揭示联邦推荐中交互级成员推理攻击：服务器仅凭上传的物品嵌入迭代训练影子模型反推用户正样本，并提出公共参数约束正则以近乎零推荐损失防御。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;不变学习&quot;,&quot;解耦表征&quot;,&quot;流行度分布偏移&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/invariant_collaborative_filtering_to_popularity_distribution_shift/">Invariant Collaborative Filtering to Popularity Distribution Shift</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将流行度偏移重构为分布外泛化问题，用表示增广+距离相关解耦出不变偏好表征，在 5 个真实集与 4 类评测协议下稳定超越主流去偏基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;召回/曝光分配策略&quot;,&quot;Contextual Bandit&quot;,&quot;Exposure Constraints&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/rl_bandit_rec/learning_with_exposure_constraints_in_recommendation_systems/">Learning with Exposure Constraints in Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将提供者最低曝光约束建模为分阶段上下文老虎机，提出EES/DP*/LCB*，达到˜O(T^{2/3}) regret并给出Ω(T^{2/3})下界。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;子模最大化&quot;,&quot;比例表示约束&quot;,&quot;偏置干预&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/maximizing_submodular_functions_for_recommendation_in_the_presence_of_biase/">Maximizing Submodular Functions for Recommendation in the Presence of Biases</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>证明固定公平约束在子模推荐下可失效，提出无偏组校准配额的两阶段贪心；MovieLens离线模拟中14/31类型集合提升≥3%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;Max-min Fairness&quot;,&quot;Provider Fairness&quot;,&quot;Online Resource Allocation&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/p_mmf_provider_max_min_fairness_re_ranking_in_recommender_system/">P-MMF: Provider Max-min Fairness Re-ranking in Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将提供商公平重排建模为MMF资源分配，在对偶空间动量在线求解，四数据集W1@10提升0.6–2.0%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2023&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;重排/混排&quot;,&quot;Thompson Sampling&quot;,&quot;Personalized PageRank&quot;,&quot;探索-利用平衡&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/rl_bandit_rec/pie_personalized_interest_exploration_for_large_scale_recommender_systems/">PIE: Personalized Interest Exploration for Large-Scale Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以 PPR 构建用户-创作者探索空间 + Thompson 采样在线探索 + 概率化混排，在 Facebook Watch 十亿级用户四周 A/B 中 SCC +3.50% 而互动指标不降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;自我图学习&quot;,&quot;本地差分隐私&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2023/debias_fairness_industrial/semi_decentralized_federated_ego_graph_learning_for_recommendation/">Semi-decentralized Federated Ego Graph Learning for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2023</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用服务器协同聚类生成「虚假公共物品」把孤立自我图连成高阶子图，实现设备间 D2D 消息传递的半去中心化联邦推荐，通信开销最低且精度超越 FedPerGNN。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;多任务学习与列表级重排&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;多目标优化&quot;,&quot;数据重采样&quot;,&quot;PID 控制&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/multi_task_reranking/a_data_centric_multi_objective_learning_framework_for_responsible_recommend/">A Data-Centric Multi-Objective Learning Framework for Responsible Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出三层数据采样框架 MoRec，用符号梯度重加权与 PI 控制器统一优化准确率、收入、公平与对齐，最大精度损失 2.76%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Green AI 基准&quot;,&quot;Only-Encode-Once&quot;,&quot;碳排效率指标&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/benchmarking_news_recommendation_in_the_era_of_green_ai/">Benchmarking News Recommendation in the Era of Green AI</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个新闻推荐 Green AI 基准 GreenRec，提出只编码一次 (OLEO) 解耦范式与 ApC 可持续性指标，在 MIND 上评测 30 个模型变体，精度持平 SOTA 且可持续性最高提升 2992%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;协同聚类&quot;,&quot;监督对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/co_clustering_for_federated_recommender_system/">Co-clustering for Federated Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出服务器端按物品类别协同聚类用户并聚合相似组模型，结合全局物品成员监督对比学习，在4个数据集上以1:99采样排序评测提升HR/NDCG。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排（打分校正）&quot;,&quot;Personal Popularity&quot;,&quot;Counterfactual Inference&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/debiasing_recommendation_with_personal_popularity/">Debiasing Recommendation with Personal Popularity</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出用户级个人流行度 PP 与反事实框架 PPAC，用代理变量解耦 PP/GP 直接效应，在等频干预测试集上全排序 Recall@50 最高提升 46.8%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;图神经网络与知识图谱推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;分布鲁棒优化&quot;,&quot;图推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/graph_rec/distributionally_robust_graph_based_recommendation_system/">Distributionally Robust Graph-based Recommendation System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将图聚合重写为平滑正则并嵌入DRO边权，配合GEA扩展稀疏邻域，在8个偏移数据集全库排序NDCG上6/8最优。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果去偏&quot;,&quot;双稳健估计&quot;,&quot;模型校准&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/doubly_calibrated_estimator_for_recommendation_on_data_missing_not_at_rando/">Doubly Calibrated Estimator for Recommendation on Data Missing Not At Random</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出双校准 DR 估计器，用校准专家与三层联合学习同时校准插补误差和倾向性分数，在 Coat/Yahoo!R3/KuaiRec 无偏测试集上 MSE 降低 3.4%–8.4%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;用户侧群体公平&quot;,&quot;动态增量微调&quot;,&quot;可微排序指标&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/ensuring_user_side_fairness_in_dynamic_recommender_systems/">Ensuring User-side Fairness in Dynamic Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>FADE 用增量微调+定期重启与可微 Hit 公平损失，动态压缩用户群体性能差距 48.91% 而性能仅降 2.44%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;分布式检索&quot;,&quot;摊销组曝光公平&quot;,&quot;对偶优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/fairsync_ensuring_amortized_group_exposure_in_distributed_recommendation_re/">FairSync: Ensuring Amortized Group Exposure in Distributed Recommendation Retrieval</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将组曝光约束转为对偶空间中的分布式召回，中心聚合历史公平信息并广播；全库KNN评测在Amazon-Book/Taobao保持100% ESP，Recall较Uncalibrated提升3.4%/9.6%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;异构信息网络&quot;,&quot;差分隐私&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/federated_heterogeneous_graph_neural_network_for_privacy_preserving_recomme/">Federated Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network for Privacy-preserving Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个异构信息网络联邦推荐框架 FedHGNN，用两阶段本地扰动发布交互以恢复被割裂的元路径语义，Leave-One-Out 全库排序下 HR@10 最高提升 34%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端策略仿真&quot;,&quot;过滤气泡&quot;,&quot;多样性分解&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/filter_bubble_or_homogenization_disentangling_the_long_term_effects_of_reco/">Filter Bubble or Homogenization? Disentangling the Long-Term Effects of Recommendations on User Consumption Patterns</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用智能体仿真把过滤气泡与同质化解耦为用户间/用户内多样性两个正交维度，并提出分箱消费与偏斜热门两种新推荐策略。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;图协同过滤&quot;,&quot;对抗图丢弃&quot;,&quot;不变性学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/general_debiasing_for_graph_based_collaborative_filtering_via_adversarial_g/">General Debiasing for Graph-based Collaborative Filtering via Adversarial Graph Dropout</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用可学习偏差度量驱动的对抗式图边丢弃切出偏差感知/缓解双视图，以不变性对比学习实现图协同过滤的通用去偏，NDCG 最高相对提升 15.25%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排/排序阶段&quot;,&quot;交叉双边公平&quot;,&quot;锐度感知损失再加权&quot;,&quot;打分归一化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/intersectional_two_sided_fairness_in_recommendation/">Intersectional Two-sided Fairness in Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 ITFR，用锐度感知损失识别劣势交叉群组 + 协作梯度贡献再加权 + 打分归一化，在 3 个数据集上 CV@20 降低 7.8%–43.5% 且精度不降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;Item-side Fairness&quot;,&quot;LLM-based Recommendation&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/item_side_fairness_of_large_language_model_based_recommendation_system/">Item-side Fairness of Large Language Model-based Recommendation System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 LLM 推荐系统的物品侧不公平源于历史交互失衡与 LLM 语义先验，提出 IFairLRS 的样本重加权与惩罚重排两阶段校准；在 MovieLens1M/Steam 全库排序下显著降低 MGU/DGU 且基本不损 NDCG/HR。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;因果推断&quot;,&quot;反事实去偏&quot;,&quot;场景感知共注意力&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/cross_domain_multi_scenario/m_scan_a_multi_scenario_causal_driven_adaptive_network_for_recommendation/">M-scan: A Multi-Scenario Causal-driven Adaptive Network for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以因果图把场景影响拆为 S→M→Y 与 S→Y 两路，用场景感知共注意力显式抽取跨场景同向兴趣、用反事实消偏器剔除场景直推偏置；Aliccp/Cloud Theme 全场景 AUC 提升 2.13%/0.96%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;多样性推荐&quot;,&quot;用户-类别匹配&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/on_practical_diversified_recommendation_with_controllable_category_diversit/">On Practical Diversified Recommendation with Controllable Category Diversity Framework</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多样性拆为 U-/N-diversity 并两阶段解耦，用 top-K 类别门控实现可控多样性，离线 N-diversity HR@5 较 SOTA +129%，线上转化率 +6.66%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;端侧训练/协同学习共享阶段&quot;,&quot;轨迹推断攻击&quot;,&quot;对抗博弈隐私防御&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/physical_trajectory_inference_attack_and_defense_in_decentralized_poi_recom/">Physical Trajectory Inference Attack and Defense in Decentralized POI Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="时空轨迹与 POI 本地生活推荐">POI</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示去中心化POI推荐中模型/软决策共享的轨迹泄露风险，提出融合目标POI与序列内相关POI隐信息的PTIA攻击与对抗博弈防御AGD，攻击F1达0.61且防御后降至0.16、HR@10仅降约2个点。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Federated Recommendation&quot;,&quot;Poisoning Attack&quot;,&quot;Fake Users&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/poisoning_federated_recommender_systems_with_fake_users/">Poisoning Federated Recommender Systems with Fake Users</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出免本地数据、免聚合规则知识的假用户投毒攻击 PoisonFRS，仅凭服务器下发的物品嵌入估计流行物品并构造目标模型，在 4 个真实数据集与 7 种聚合规则下以 0.03%–0.05% 假用户将目标物品 HR@5 推至 0.70–1.00。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦学习&quot;,&quot;向量量化语义ID&quot;,&quot;提示微调&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/cross_domain_multi_scenario/prompt_enhanced_federated_content_representation_learning_for_cross_domain_/">Prompt-enhanced Federated Content Representation Learning for Cross-domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>联邦语义码本与提示微调实现非重叠隐私保护跨域序列推荐，全库排序R@10最高提升10.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;准确率-多样性权衡&quot;,&quot;消费约束&quot;,&quot;γ-同质性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/reconciling_the_accuracy_diversity_trade_off_in_recommendations/">Reconciling the accuracy-diversity trade-off in recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用消费约束（用户只看 top-k）解释准确率-多样性权衡，证明计入 top-k 目标时最优解自然多样、忽略时完全同质，并以 γ-同质性给出由分布尾部决定的闭式多样性指数。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排/排序&quot;,&quot;开放世界&quot;,&quot;多兴趣传播&quot;,&quot;双稳健估计&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/cross_domain_multi_scenario/rethinking_cross_domain_sequential_recommendation_under_open_world_assumpti/">Rethinking Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation under Open-World Assumptions</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 AMID：多兴趣组建组传播 + 双稳健估计，处理开放世界部分重叠与选择偏差；1:999 采样排序 NDCG@10 最高 +26.48%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;ADMM 分布式优化&quot;,&quot;曝光公平正则&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/scalable_and_provably_fair_exposure_control_for_large_scale_recommender_sys/">Scalable and Provably Fair Exposure Control for Large-Scale Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将曝光公平正则嵌入 iALS，用 ADMM+近端梯度与 Sherman-Morrison 技巧保住可分性，训练开销接近 iALS，在 ML-20M/MSD 等百万级数据上实现可调曝光控制。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端信息流审计&quot;,&quot;探索-利用审计&quot;,&quot;个性化分数&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/rl_bandit_rec/tiktok_and_the_art_of_personalization_investigating_exploration_and_exploit/">TikTok and the Art of Personalization: Investigating Exploration and Exploitation on Social Media Feeds</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用时间线特征激活标注TikTok视频的探索/利用，并以随机与机器人基线审计个性化程度。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;个性化隐私预算&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/towards_personalized_privacy_user_governed_data_contribution_for_federated_/">Towards Personalized Privacy: User-Governed Data Contribution for Federated Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出用户自主数据贡献的联邦推荐 UGFedRec 与 CDCGNNFed，以图修补和云端对比学习提升部分共享场景 Top-20 排序。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;全链路曝光生态分析（非模型方法）&quot;,&quot;Deep Filter Bubble&quot;,&quot;类目层级覆盖度&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/uncovering_the_deep_filter_bubble_narrow_exposure_in_short_video_recommenda/">Uncovering the Deep Filter Bubble: Narrow Exposure in Short-Video Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用三级类目覆盖度在 4 亿交互上量化短视频“深层过滤气泡”，揭示反馈类型与人群差异的结构性成因。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;特征级偏差&quot;,&quot;后处理去偏&quot;,&quot;线性权重重构&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/understanding_and_counteracting_feature_level_bias_in_click_through_rate_pr/">Understanding and Counteracting Feature-Level Bias in Click-Through Rate Prediction</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>逐块屏蔽定位 CTR 线性权重为特征级偏差主因，提出推理期线性权重削减/重构两种微创去偏策略，REO@5 降 14%–47%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构&quot;,&quot;召回/排序安全性评测&quot;,&quot;Uplift Modeling&quot;,&quot;注入攻击预算分配&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/debias_fairness_industrial/uplift_modeling_for_target_user_attacks_on_recommender_systems/">Uplift Modeling for Target User Attacks on Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>面向目标用户注入攻击，以因果 uplift 估计逐用户攻击难度并用背包 DP 分配虚假用户预算，HR@10 相对提升 30%–86%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2024&quot;,&quot;冷启动推荐&quot;,&quot;端到端联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;物品属性表征&quot;,&quot;表征对齐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2024/cold_start_rec/when_federated_recommendation_meets_cold_start_problem_separating_item_attr/">When Federated Recommendation Meets Cold-Start Problem: Separating Item Attributes and User Interactions</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2024</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个联邦冷启动推荐框架 IFedRec：服务器端学习物品属性元网络、客户端保留交互物品嵌入，双向对齐两表征使冷物品仅凭属性即可推理。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;多任务学习&quot;,&quot;粗排&quot;,&quot;跨阶段协同&quot;,&quot;Margin InfoNCE&quot;,&quot;样本选择偏差&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/multi_task_reranking/a_hybrid_cross_stage_coordination_pre_ranking_model_for_online_recommendati/">A Hybrid Cross-Stage Coordination Pre-ranking Model for Online Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 HCCP 粗排模型，融合上游召回与下游精排多级未曝光样本，用 Margin InfoNCE 联合优化一致性与长尾精度，JD 线上 UCVR+14.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;多维评测框架&quot;,&quot;候选位置偏差&quot;,&quot;幻觉&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/beyond_utility_evaluating_llm_as_recommender/">Beyond Utility: Evaluating LLM as Recommender</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出含历史长度敏感性、候选位置偏差、画像生成与幻觉四维的 LLM 推荐多维评测框架，揭示 LLM 在重排与冷启动占优但位置偏差严重。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回+精排+重排&quot;,&quot;创作者侧推荐&quot;,&quot;用户可用性计算&quot;,&quot;双边市场公平性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/creator_side_recommender_system_challenges_designs_and_applications/">Creator-Side Recommender System: Challenges, Designs, and Applications</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出创作者侧推荐系统 DualRec，将用户侧召回/排序镜像为物品找用户，并用用户可用性计算 UAC 解决用户可达性问题，快手线上 DAC 提升 2.9%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测基准&quot;,&quot;敏感内容标签&quot;,&quot;算法放大&quot;,&quot;数据集构建&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/datasets_for_navigating_sensitive_topics_in_recommendation_systems/">Datasets for Navigating Sensitive Topics in Recommendation Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>构建 ML-DDD 与 AO3 两个带敏感内容警告标签的推荐数据集，提出 Warning Amplification 指标，发现个性化 CF 反而去放大敏感内容。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测协议&quot;,&quot;无偏估计&quot;,&quot;随机曝光数据集&quot;,&quot;去偏推荐&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/debias_can_be_unreliable_mitigating_bias_issue_in_evaluating_debiasing_reco/">Debias Can be Unreliable: Mitigating Bias Issue in Evaluating Debiasing Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示随机曝光数据集上传统 Recall 评估与全曝光真值不一致，提出 URE 无偏估计方案并重估去偏方法。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;分布鲁棒优化&quot;,&quot;扩散去噪&quot;,&quot;OOD 泛化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/distributionally_robust_graph_out_of_distribution_recommendation_via_diffus/">Distributionally Robust Graph Out-of-Distribution Recommendation via Diffusion Model</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 DRO 推荐对噪声分布过度加权之弊，用 VGAE+扩散去噪、Sinkhorn 距离与熵正则重构最坏情形优化，OOD 场景最高提升 75.5%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;摊销推理&quot;,&quot;早期融合&quot;,&quot;Cross-Attention&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/sequential_rec/efficient_user_history_modeling_with_amortized_inference_for_deep_learning_/">Efficient user history modeling with amortized inference for deep learning recommendation models</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 cross-attention 追加候选替代逐项拼接，使历史编码可摊销推理，LinkedIn Feed/Ads 延迟降 30%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;外部知识蒸馏&quot;,&quot;流式分布漂移&quot;,&quot;基础模型&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/external_large_foundation_model_how_to_efficiently_serve_trillions_of_param/">External Large Foundation Model: How to Efficiently Serve Trillions of Parameters for Online Ads Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以外部蒸馏+数据增强服务解耦教师训练，用辅助头与学生适配器弥合流式分布漂移，实现万亿参数基础模型零延迟增益服务多业务精排模型。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;扩散模型数据补全&quot;,&quot;反事实去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/multimodal_rec/generating_with_fairness_a_modality_diffused_counterfactual_framework_for_i/">Generating with Fairness: A Modality-Diffused Counterfactual Framework for Incomplete Multimodal Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用模态感知扩散补全缺失模态，并以反事实推断剥离可见性偏差，在三个数据集上同时提升准确率与公平性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;因果推断&quot;,&quot;扩散模型&quot;,&quot;OOD 泛化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/graph_representation_learning_via_causal_diffusion_for_out_of_distribution_/">Graph Representation Learning via Causal Diffusion for Out-of-Distribution Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用结构因果模型定位环境混杂因子，以变分推断+扩散模型学习环境不变图表征，OOD 场景最高提升 36.73%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;离线评测&quot;,&quot;帕累托前沿&quot;,&quot;公平性-相关性联合评测&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/joint_evaluation_of_fairness_and_relevance_in_recommender_systems_with_pare/">Joint Evaluation of Fairness and Relevance in Recommender Systems with Pareto Frontier</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多任务学习与列表级重排">MTL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 DPFR：以测试集构造相关性-公平性帕累托前沿，用模型得分到前沿的欧氏距离作为联合评测指标，揭示现有联合指标排序与帕累托最优解不一致。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;大记忆网络&quot;,&quot;乘积量化&quot;,&quot;记忆参数服务器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/sequential_rec/large_memory_network_for_recommendation/">Large Memory Network for Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用百万级全局共享记忆块压缩用户历史行为，以乘积量化双轴激活实现 O(√n d) 检索，抖音电商搜索 AUC +4.68%、订单/人 +0.87%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Middleman Bias&quot;,&quot;Cross-Encoder&quot;,&quot;关键词相关性&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/ctr_feature_interaction/middleman_bias_in_advertising_aligning_relevance_of_keyphrase_recommendatio/">Middleman Bias in Advertising: Aligning Relevance of Keyphrase Recommendations with Search</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示广告关键词相关性过滤中的“中间人偏差”，用Search相关性判定重构训练标签，并以轻量Cross-Encoder在eBay线上取得CVR+11.6%的收益。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;Shapley Value&quot;,&quot;Embedding Pruning&quot;,&quot;On-device Recommendation&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/on_device_content_based_recommendation_with_single_shot_embedding_pruning_a/">On-device Content-based Recommendation with Single-shot Embedding Pruning: A Cooperative Game Perspective</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用 Shapley 值一次性剪枝 CTR 嵌入表，配合场感知码本替代零填充，无需重训即可适配任意端侧存储预算。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;在线实验/因果评估&quot;,&quot;共生偏差&quot;,&quot;SUTVA违背&quot;,&quot;聚类随机化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/reducing_symbiosis_bias_through_better_a_b_tests_of_recommendation_algorith/">Reducing Symbiosis Bias Through Better A/B Tests of Recommendation Algorithms</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示推荐算法共享训练数据导致的共生偏差，理论建模并仿真对比三种实验设计，用国家级分流实验验证偏差存在。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;大语言模型推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;自对弈偏好优化&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/llm_rec/sprec_self_play_to_debias_llm_based_recommendation/">SPRec: Self-Play to Debias LLM-based Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>揭示 DPO 反向 KL 的 mode-seeking 本质放大流行度偏差，提出自对弈 SPRec 以模型自身输出作负样本自适应去偏，兼顾准确率与公平性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2025&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;类别感知公平度量&quot;,&quot;公平正则化&quot;,&quot;性别偏差&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2025/debias_fairness_industrial/unmasking_gender_bias_in_recommendation_systems_and_enhancing_category_awar/">Unmasking Gender Bias in Recommendation Systems and Enhancing Category-Aware Fairness</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2025</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出6个类别感知的性别偏差度量（含排序折扣）与GBS聚合，并以类别覆盖差作正则项，在3数据集上偏差降50–77%而性能损失有限。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;重排&quot;,&quot;流行度偏差&quot;,&quot;提示工程&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/bridging_semantic_understanding_and_popularity_bias_with_llms/">Bridging Semantic Understanding and Popularity Bias with LLMs</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将流行度偏差拆解为物品侧与用户侧语义，用结构化指令提示引导 RecLLM 双侧去偏，在 LtC/MRMC 与 MRR/F1 上同时超越多样性提示基线。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;延迟反馈建模&quot;,&quot;复购感知双分支&quot;,&quot;在线流式训练&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/ctr_feature_interaction/delayed_feedback_modeling_for_post_click_gross_merchandise_volume_predictio/">Delayed Feedback Modeling for Post-Click Gross Merchandise Volume Prediction: Benchmark, Insights and Approaches</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个后点击GMV延迟反馈基准TRACE，提出复购感知双分支READER，在线流式训练下ACC相对提升2.19%</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;稀疏嵌入&quot;,&quot;渐进剪枝&quot;,&quot;线性自编码器&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/collaborative_filtering/efficient_learning_of_sparse_representations_from_interactions/">Efficient Learning of Sparse Representations from Interactions</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>在 ELSA 线性自编码器训练中渐进剪枝学习高维稀疏物品嵌入，实现最高 100× 压缩仅损 2.5% nDCG，并派生可解释语义分段。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;特征工程/离线特征选择&quot;,&quot;特征重要性估计&quot;,&quot;梯度归因去偏&quot;,&quot;平滑基线&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/fairfs_addressing_deep_feature_selection_biases_for_recommender_system/">FairFS: Addressing Deep Feature Selection Biases for Recommender System</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>识别深度特征选择的三类偏差（层偏差/基线偏差/近似偏差），以平滑基线与聚合近似实现无偏特征重要性估计，AUC 提升最高 0.35%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;社交与群组推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;分布式推荐&quot;,&quot;用户验证共识&quot;,&quot;链接预测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/social_group_rec/from_aggregation_to_selection_user_validated_distributed_social_recommendat/">From Aggregation to Selection: User-Validated Distributed Social Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="社交与群组推荐">Social</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将分布式社交推荐从模型聚合转为用户驱动的算法选择+验证者多数共识投票，提出 Acc@K 共识评测，四数据集 Acc@2 平均提升 4.51%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;流行度去偏&quot;,&quot;用户活跃度重加权&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/identifying_and_upweighting_power_niche_users_to_mitigate_popularity_bias_i/">Identifying and Upweighting Power-Niche Users to Mitigate Popularity Bias in Recommendations</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>识别高活跃-小众偏好用户并对其在BPR损失中双重加权，在三个基准上实现性能与流行度去偏的帕累托双赢。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦持续学习&quot;,&quot;原型迁移&quot;,&quot;自蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/learning_evolving_preferences_a_federated_continual_framework_for_user_cent/">Learning Evolving Preferences: A Federated Continual Framework for User-Centric Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>联邦持续推荐框架 FCUCR：时间感知自蒸馏抗遗忘 + 跨用户原型迁移促协同，四数据集全库排序 HR@10 最高提升 44.0%。</p>
</dd>
