<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;序列与会话推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;长序列建模&quot;,&quot;交叉注意力&quot;,&quot;长度外推&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/sequential_rec/make_it_long_keep_it_fast_end_to_end_10k_long_user_behavior_sequence_modeli/">Make It Long, Keep It Fast: End-to-End 10K Long User Behavior Sequence Modeling for Billion-Scale Douyin Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="序列与会话推荐">Seq</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>以单查询目标-历史交叉注意力(STCA)将长序列建模降至 O(L)，配合请求级批处理(RLB)与稀疏训练稠密推理，在抖音十亿级流量实现 10K 序列端到端排序。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;因果去偏&quot;,&quot;多行为推荐&quot;,&quot;对比学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/mclmr_a_model_agnostic_causal_learning_framework_for_multi_behavior_recomme/">MCLMR: A Model-Agnostic Causal Learning Framework for Multi-Behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="自监督对比学习与交互去噪">SSL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用后门干预+MoE自适应聚合+偏差感知对比学习，作为即插即用框架增强多行为推荐，三数据集全库排序显著提升。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;级联延迟反馈&quot;,&quot;净转化率&quot;,&quot;在线持续学习&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/ctr_feature_interaction/modeling_cascaded_delay_feedback_for_online_net_conversion_rate_prediction_/">Modeling Cascaded Delay Feedback for Online Net Conversion Rate Prediction: Benchmark, Insights and Solutions</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="CTR/CVR 预估与特征交叉">CTR</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个淘宝净转化率流式基准 CASCADE，提出 CVR-RFR 级联+分阶段去偏+延迟感知排序损失的 TESLA，NetCVR 的 RI-AUC 绝对提升 12.41%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;神经符号推理&quot;,&quot;前门调整&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/modeling_endogenous_logic_causal_neuro_symbolic_reasoning_model_for_explain/">Modeling Endogenous Logic: Causal Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning Model for Explainable Multi-Behavior Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="图神经网络与知识图谱推荐">Graph</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用前门调整把多行为链内源逻辑做成可解释因果中介，三路神经逻辑推理在三个数据集 Full-Ranking 上 HR@10 提升 20%+。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;多模态推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐&quot;,&quot;组感知聚合&quot;,&quot;偏好引导蒸馏&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/multimodal_rec/multimodal_enhanced_federated_recommendation_a_group_wise_fusion_approach/">Multimodal-enhanced Federated Recommendation: A Group-wise Fusion Approach</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="多模态推荐与内容理解">MM</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将多模态表征学习卸载至服务器，以组感知聚合+偏好引导蒸馏把多模态语义注入联邦推荐，五数据集全库排序 HR@50 最高提升约 30%。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;强化学习与动态探索推荐&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;库存约束&quot;,&quot;离线策略学习&quot;,&quot;上下文赌博机&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/rl_bandit_rec/off_policy_learning_with_limited_supply/">Off-Policy Learning with Limited Supply</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="强化学习与动态探索推荐">RL</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首次形式化库存受限下的离线策略学习，提出按“相对奖励差”分配稀缺物品的 OPLS，在合成与 KuaiRec 上稳定超越贪心 OPL。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;联邦持续个性化&quot;,&quot;软提示与原型锚定&quot;,&quot;差分隐私&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/prototype_aligned_federated_soft_prompts_for_continual_web_personalization/">Prototype-Aligned Federated Soft-Prompts for Continual Web Personalization</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>冻结骨干上注入双时间尺度软提示，长期提示锚定DP联邦原型库，八基准NDCG@10平均+2.9%且降低遗忘与适应步数。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;评测与系统设计&quot;,&quot;用户一致性度量&quot;,&quot;分层评测&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/quantifying_user_coherence_a_unified_framework_for_analyzing_recommender_sy/">Quantifying User Coherence: A Unified Framework for Analyzing Recommender Systems Across Domains</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="冷启动与长尾推荐">Cold</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>提出 Mean Surprise 与 Mean Conditional Surprise 两个信息论度量刻画用户画像一致性，揭示复杂模型增益集中于高一致用户，并支撑分层评测与用户分群建模。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;联邦训练阶段（客户端梯度投毒）&quot;,&quot;子群投毒攻击&quot;,&quot;联邦推荐安全&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/spattack_subgroup_poisoning_attacks_on_federated_recommender_systems/">Spattack: Subgroup Poisoning Attacks on Federated Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>首个联邦推荐子群投毒攻击：近似-提升范式+对比排斥与自适应加权，0.1%恶意客户端即达100% ER@5且几乎不伤非目标群。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW&quot;,&quot;协同过滤与双塔召回&quot;,&quot;召回&quot;,&quot;Top-K 损失函数&quot;,&quot;分位数回归&quot;,&quot;分布鲁棒优化&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/collaborative_filtering/talos_optimizing_top_k_accuracy_in_recommender_systems/">Talos: Optimizing Top-$K$ Accuracy in Recommender Systems</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="协同过滤与双塔召回">CF</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>用量化阈值把 Top-K 截断改写为分数-阈值比较，配合采样分位回归与约束项，直接优化 Precision@K/Recall@K 并具备 DRO 鲁棒性。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;跨域与多场景推荐&quot;,&quot;精排&quot;,&quot;跨域公平性&quot;,&quot;信息论增益重分配&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/cross_domain_multi_scenario/the_double_edged_sword_of_knowledge_transfer_diagnosing_and_curing_fairness/">The Double-Edged Sword of Knowledge Transfer: Diagnosing and Curing Fairness Pathologies in Cross-Domain Recommendation</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="跨域与多场景推荐">Cross</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>理论揭示跨域推荐中“差异迁移”与“信息增益不均”两类公平性病理，提出模型无关的 CDFA 框架，在 Tenrec QB/QK 上 UGF 最高降 98.97% 且精度不降。</p>
</dd>
<dt data-paper-tags="[&quot;WWW 2026&quot;,&quot;去偏公平与可信推荐&quot;,&quot;生成式推荐&quot;,&quot;Machine Unlearning&quot;,&quot;Influence Function&quot;,&quot;LLM4Rec Fairness&quot;]"><strong><a href="../../venues/WWW/2026/debias_fairness_industrial/towards_fair_large_language_model_based_recommender_systems_without_costly_/">Towards Fair Large Language Model-based Recommender Systems without Costly Retraining</a></strong><span class="paper-tags"><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--venue" title="发表出处：ACM Web Conference">WWW (TheWebConf)</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="去偏公平、可信隐私与工业架构">Debias</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--category" title="大语言模型推荐 (LLM4Rec)">LLM4Rec</span><span class="paper-tag paper-tag--year" title="发表年份">2026</span></span></dt>
<dd>
<p>将 LLM-RS 去偏重构为免重训机器遗忘任务：可学习掩码定位致偏样本 + 影响函数一步参数修正，公平性提升同时精度不降、耗时降 96%。</p>
</dd>
</dl>
